### [1] [LSP-RAG: Latent Scoring Perspectives for Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25229)
*Haochen Liu,Guanghui Min,Song Wang,Yada Zhu,Chen Chen,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LSP-RAG框架，用多视角提示软打分提升知识图谱多跳检索质量，使轻量语言模型也能达到甚至超越大模型驱动的KG-RAG效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM因训练语料不完备，事实性与多跳推理能力不足；KG-RAG可缓解，但现有方法要么依赖大模型（如ChatGPT）引导检索导致算力开销高，要么用轻量模型时检索质量受限，尤其在多跳场景下表现差。

Method: 设计"潜在打分视角"机制：每个视角由两类软提示组合驱动——视角专属软提示诱导出针对候选三元组的独立打分函数；上下文软提示逐跳编码查询与已探索三元组，随推理推进而演化，维持多跳一致性与相关性。多个潜在视角自适应聚合以捕获异构相关性信号，无需人工定义语义角色。

Result: 在多个基于KG的问答基准上优于现有检索系统，包括依赖更大规模语言模型的方法；在有限算力预算下仍表现优异。

Conclusion: 多视角潜在软提示打分可在轻量模型上实现准确、鲁棒的多跳KG检索，为低算力条件下的KG-RAG提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in open-domain question answering, but often struggle with factual accuracy and multi-hop reasoning due to the incompleteness of the training corpora. A promising solution is Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG), which supplements LLMs with structured knowledge retrieved from external knowledge graphs (KGs). However, existing KG-RAG methods either rely on large-scale language models (e.g., ChatGPT) to guide the retrieval process, which leads to high computational costs, or suffer from limited retrieval quality when using lightweight language models, particularly under multi-hop scenarios. We propose LSP-RAG (Latent Scoring Perspectives for Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Knowledge Graphs), a novel KG-RAG framework that enhances hop-wise KG knowledge retrieval through multi-perspective prompted scoring. Each latent scoring perspective is guided by a combination of two types of soft prompts: a perspective-specific soft prompt induces a distinct prompt-conditioned scoring function over candidate triplets, while a contextual soft prompt evolves with each reasoning hop by encoding the query and previously explored KG triplets, enabling the model to preserve consistency and relevance across multi-hop retrieval. Rather than assuming manually defined semantic roles, these latent perspectives are adaptively aggregated to capture heterogeneous relevance signals during retrieval. This design allows LSP-RAG to perform accurate and robust retrieval using lightweight language models. LSP-RAG achieves superior performance across multiple KG-based Question Answering benchmarks compared to existing retrieval systems, including those that rely on much larger language models, demonstrating its effectiveness under limited computational budgets.

</details>


### [2] [Retrieving Patristic References and Modelling Topics in Bullinger's Reformation Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25247)
*Lena Pickartz,Philipp Schaer,Phillip B. Ströbel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统探索布林格书信中教父引文的计算检测与细粒度主题建模：比较词法(BM25)、稠密(双编码器)与混合检索，并对FASTopic做种子引导扩展，发现BM25最强、混合融合显著提升隐含引文召回，主题建模效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布林格书信大量援引教父著作作为权威来源，但此类引文的计算检测基本空白；稀少的拉丁语域内检索模型能否迁移到该领域未知；同时需要一种方法刻画书信中讨论的主题与思想。

Method: 在显式与隐式教父引文上对比BM25、双编码器稠密检索与混合方法，评估领域微调拉丁编码器的迁移能力；主题建模方面，在FASTopic上引入种子引导损失与编辑摘要嵌入。

Result: 微调于圣经复用的拉丁编码器在双编码器中最佳（显式R@20=82%，隐含16%）；BM25在显式引用上优于稠密方法（94%）。BM25与稠密检索覆盖不相交的隐含引文集合，融合排序将隐含R@20提升至22%，接近交叉编码器重排的26%但推理成本更低。主题建模一致性增益极小，主导主题权重接近均匀分布，难以用于检索。

Conclusion: 首次给出布林格书信中教父引文检索与细粒度主题建模的方法框架；混合检索是隐含引文检测的高性价比方案，而主题建模需转向LLooM等LLM概念归纳方法以获得更好可解释性。

Abstract: The correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575), Swiss Reformer and Antistes of Zurich, documents the political and religious debates of the Reformation, including extensive appeals to the Church Fathers as authoritative sources. Detecting such references computationally remains largely unexplored. We investigate dense and sparse retrieval for patristic reference detection, and metadata-informed topic modelling as a proxy for the topics and ideas discussed. Prior work on Latin intertextuality has fine-tuned dense retrievers for their respective target domain, and we test how well these models transfer to Bullinger's correspondence. We compare lexical (BM25), dense (bi-encoders), and hybrid methods on explicit and implicit references. A Latin encoder fine-tuned on biblical reuse performs best among the bi-encoders (82%, Recall@20 on explicit and 16% on implicit references), while BM25 outperforms dense approaches on explicit citations (94%, R@20). Because BM25 and dense retrieval recover disjoint sets of implicit references, fusing their rankings raises implicit R@20 to 22%, close to cross-encoder reranking of the same candidates (26%) but without its inference cost. For topic modelling, we extend FASTopic with a seed-guided loss function and editorial abstract embeddings. Measurable coherence gains over the baseline remain minimal, but qualitative and temporal analysis shows that some topics track documented historical events. However, dominant topic weights barely exceed a uniform distribution, making topics unusable to inform retrieval. A pilot application of LLM-based concept induction (LLooM) further points towards a more human-interpretable alternative. Together, these results present a first approach to retrieving patristic references and modelling fine-grained topics in Bullinger's correspondence.

</details>


### [3] [LQ-Rec: Late Quantization for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25256)
*Satoshi Koide,Yueqi Wang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出非自回归的Late Quantization (LQ)范式，将离散化限制在输出（物品）侧，配合端到端训练的残差量化器，并用加性误差分解与成对排序定理界定量化导致的排序损失，实现语义ID长度与排序质量单调正相关、支持免训练随时压缩、泛化更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索（GR）用短语义ID压缩物品并训练自回归解码器生成下一物品码，利于冷启动和存储；但自回归GR存在结构性扩展瓶颈：更长的语义ID理论上降低量化误差，实际却损害排序质量，且显存开销随ID长度呈二次增长。

Method: 提出Late Quantization：编码器与打分规则均在连续空间运作，仅将离散化推迟到物品（输出）侧；物品以端到端训练的残差量化器产生的语义ID存储与匹配，从而形成非自回归范式。并据此给出加性误差分解与成对排序定理，联合界定量化引起的排序下降。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，LQ-Rec同时具备三项能力：排序质量随语义ID长度单调提升并优于现有基线；单一训练模型支持anytime compression，可在不重训的情况下权衡排序质量与推理成本；在记忆/泛化划分上表现出更优的泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过把量化移至输出侧的非自回归架构，回避了自回归GR的ID长度扩展瓶颈，并提供此前语义ID推荐器所缺乏的结构性误差保证，在质量、可调压缩与泛化上同时获益。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) for sequential recommendation compresses each item into a short semantic ID and trains an autoregressive decoder to generate next-item codes from a user history. While appealing for cold-start handling and storage, au- toregressive GR suffers a structural scaling limit: typically, longer semantic IDs reduce quantization error in principle but degrade ranking quality and quadratic memory usage against semantic ID length. We propose Late Quantization (LQ), a non-autoregressive paradigm that confines discretization to the output (item) side of the pipeline. The encoder and scoring rule operate in continuous space, while items are stored and matched as semantic IDs produced by an end-to-end-trained residual quantizer. This architectural shift permits an additive error decomposition and a pairwise ordering theorem that jointly bound the quantization- induced ranking drop, a structural guarantee absent in prior semantic-ID recommenders. Across four public benchmarks, the resulting recommender, LQ-Rec, delivers three capabilities simultaneously: ranking quality improves monotonically with the semantic-ID length and outperforms existing baselines; a single trained model supports anytime compression, tuning ranking quality against inference cost without retraining; and LQ-Rec shows superior generalization on a memorization/generalization partition.

</details>


### [4] [Learning Generative Ranking Policies for Whole-Page Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25527)
*Chen Qiu,Zhigang Hua,Qi Xu,Jizhe Zhang,Sha Hua,Ren Mao,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“整页策略改进”（whole-page policy improvement）方法，用一个局部的页面级目标，将整页优化分解为每个位置上的训练目标，使生成式重排序在每一步选择对整个页面最优的条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐页面需整体消费，条目相互竞争又互补，最优页面并非单个最优条目的简单拼接；页面组合数随长度组合爆炸，无法枚举，必须“生成”而非“穷举”。现有生成式重排序存在两大局限：(1) 对整个采样页面中每个选择都使用同一页面级训练信号，缺乏位置区分度；(2) 模仿训练前构建的目标页面，而这些目标在生成器改进过程中基本固定，成为瓶颈。

Method: 设计单一的局部页面级目标，反复将当前生成器朝更高分页面推进；该目标可精确分解为每个位置一个训练目标，且每个目标在同一“部分页面”上下文中比较候选，依据候选所导向的完整页面的得分来判断优劣。因此每个位置学到的是“对整页更有利”的条目，而非孤立看最优的条目。整个过程称为整页策略改进。

Result: 在两个公开数据集 KuaiRand-1K（多目标条目选择）与 RL4RS-SeqSlate（受约束排序）以及一个工业真实曝光页面数据集上，方法优于所对比的生成式重排序基线；在具有已知最优页面的合成基准上，最接近最优解，表明收益来源于更优的整页决策。

Conclusion: 以可精确分解为逐位置目标的局部页面级目标替代“共享信号”和“固定模仿目标”，可有效实现整页联合选择与排序优化，并在公开、工业与合成基准上验证其有效性。

Abstract: A recommendation page is consumed as a whole: the items on it compete for attention and complement one another, so the best page is not simply the individually best items placed side by side. We study whole-page optimization, which selects and orders the items on a page jointly to maximize the score of the page. The number of possible pages grows combinatorially, so good pages must be generated rather than enumerated. Some generative re-ranking methods use the same page-level training signal for every choice in a sampled page. Others imitate target pages built before training, which remain largely fixed as the generator improves. We address both limitations with one local page-level objective, which repeatedly moves the current generator toward pages with higher score. This objective splits exactly into one training target per position, and each target compares candidates at the same partial page by the scores of the complete pages they lead to. Every position therefore learns to choose the item that is better for the whole page, rather than the item that looks best on its own. We call this procedure whole-page policy improvement. On two public datasets, KuaiRand-1K and RL4RS-SeqSlate, which cover multi-objective item selection and constrained ordering, and on an industrial dataset of logged pages, our method outperforms the evaluated generative re-ranking baselines. On a synthetic benchmark with a known optimal page, it also comes closest to the optimum, showing that its improvements come from better whole-page decisions.

</details>


### [5] [Generative News Recommendation Reproduces Popularity: A Memorization-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25606)
*Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究以日本新闻服务约2000万次浏览会话持续训练指令微调的Llama 3 8B生成下一篇文章标题，发现生成标题分布比日志中观测分布更集中、多样性更低；高频目标标题的逐字与近似记忆更强，且在对更广泛提示下被生成，表明流行度经"行为日志→重复训练文本→优先记忆→生成复现"路径放大，成为生成式推荐流行度偏置的一个机制。目标级去重可降低记忆与输出集中度，但显著牺牲训练集规模与推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本生成式推荐器可直接生成标题等文本表示，突破仅对既有条目排序的局限；但在新闻场景下，模型可能反复输出已热门文章，从而收窄用户信息曝光的多样性。作者希望检验"训练数据记忆"是否是造成这种集中化的机制之一，即流行度偏置是否不仅源于用户偏好或排序目标，也源于模型复现高频字符串的倾向。

Method: 使用日本新闻服务约2000万条浏览会话，对指令微调的Llama 3 8B进行持续训练，任务是生成会话中的下一篇文章标题。比较生成标题分布与浏览日志中标题分布的集中度与多样性；按目标标题的出现频次分析逐字记忆与近似记忆程度；考察记忆强度与生成所响应提示范围的关系；并开展探索性的目标级去重条件实验。

Result: 生成标题分布比浏览日志中观测到的标题分布更集中、多样性更低。频繁重复的目标标题表现出更强的逐字与近似记忆；记忆越强的标题，能在更广范围的提示下被生成。目标级去重条件同时降低了记忆程度与输出集中度，但大幅缩减训练集规模并显著降低推荐准确率。

Conclusion: 结果支持一条机制路径：行为日志中的流行度转化为重复训练文本，被模型优先记忆，随后由生成式推荐器复现。生成式推荐中的流行度偏置可能不仅来自用户偏好或排序目标，还来自模型复现高频字符串的倾向，这对新闻平台的信息多样性具有重要影响；而去重虽能缓解记忆与集中化，却以训练数据规模与准确率为代价，凸显多样性—准确性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Text-based generative recommenders can go beyond ranking existing items by directly generating item titles or other textual representations. In the news domain, this raises a societal concern that models may repeatedly surface already popular articles and thereby narrow the diversity of information to which users are exposed. We investigate training-data memorization as one mechanism behind this concentration. Using approximately 20 million browsing sessions from a Japanese news service, we continually trained an instruction-tuned Llama 3 8B model to generate the next article title in a session. The generated-title distribution was more concentrated and less diverse than the distribution of titles observed in the browsing logs. Frequently repeated target titles exhibited greater verbatim and approximate memorization, and more strongly memorized titles were generated in response to a wider range of prompts. These findings support a pathway in which popularity in behavioral logs becomes repeated training text, is preferentially memorized, and is subsequently reproduced by a generative recommender. An exploratory target-level deduplication condition reduced both memorization and output concentration, while substantially reducing the training-set size and recommendation accuracy. Our results suggest that popularity bias in generative recommendation may arise not only from user preferences or ranking objectives, but also from the model's tendency to reproduce frequently encountered strings, with important implications for information diversity on news platforms.

</details>


### [6] [DNA: Unlocking Depth Scaling in CTR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25659)
*Caiyuan Li,Wenjie Wu,Yuheng Huang,Xiao Zhou,Silu Zhou,Ke Chen,Zinan Zhao,Xinrui Zeng,Hanmo Xu,Chaoqun Song,Hua Zong,Zongyuan Wu,Hongbin Zhou,Huimin Yi,Zhixuan Zhang,Dan Ou,Haihong Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DNA（双流枢纽注意力），让用户行为序列随网络深度随请求更新，解决交叉注意力堆叠收益饱和问题；离线AUC提升0.08–0.26pt，淘宝搜索上线后CTR+0.8%、GMV+0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉注意力使长行为序列建模的计算量随序列长度线性增长，但堆叠层数加深时增益迅速饱和（淘宝搜索16→28层仅提升0.02pt AUC）。作者认为瓶颈在key-value侧：每层都将同一行为序列投影为key和value，加深只是对固定序列的重复读取，而非引入新信息。

Method: 提出DNA（Dual-stream Nexus Attention，双流枢纽注意力）。每层中，请求表示（搜索query、用户特征、场景）先读取行为序列；随后每个行为token对更新后的请求表示做注意力、并用读取到的信息更新自身，使下一层读到的是被当前请求重塑过的序列。更新每个请求只执行一次，所有候选共享更新后的序列，读取与更新均为序列长度的线性复杂度。

Result: 在相近前向FLOPs下，DNA较交叉注意力堆叠AUC提升0.08–0.26pt，且在后者已饱和的深度区间仍持续提升。DNA-4已在淘宝搜索部署，线上A/B较生产模型CTR+0.8%、GMV+0.5%。

Conclusion: 将序列从"固定被读"转为"随深度与请求共同演化"，是突破交叉注意力深度收益饱和的有效方向，并在工业级搜索场景得到验证。

Abstract: Long user behavior sequences are among the strongest signals for click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Cross-attention makes these sequences practical for industrial ranking by keeping compute linear in sequence length. Yet the gains from stacking cross-attention layers taper off: on Taobao search, growing a cross-attention stack from 16 to 28 layers adds only 0.02 percentage points (pt) of AUC. We argue that the key-value side is the bottleneck. Each layer projects the same behavior sequence into keys and values, so adding depth gives the model more reads of a fixed sequence. We propose DNA (Dual-stream Nexus Attention), which lets the sequence itself evolve with depth. At each layer, the request representation (the search query, user features and scene) first reads the sequence. Each behavior token then attends to the updated request representation and uses what it reads to update itself, so the next layer reads a sequence shaped by the current request. The update runs once per request, all candidates share the resulting sequence, and both the reads and the update keep linear complexity in sequence length. At comparable forward FLOPs, DNA improves AUC over cross-attention stacks by 0.08 to 0.26 pt and keeps improving in the depth range where those stacks level off. DNA-4 is deployed in Taobao search. In an online A/B test against the production model, it improves CTR by 0.8% and GMV by 0.5%.

</details>


### [7] [Exploring Semantic Table Search in Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25691)
*Martin Pekár Christensen,Matteo Lissandrini,Katja Hose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Thetis++，将关键词搜索与语义（命名实体）表搜索融合，并系统研究排序融合与学习式方法，同时用HNSW替代LSH提升语义预过滤的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖中相关数据集发现问题：union/join搜索适用于扩展现有表，探索式搜索常用关键词搜索，而基于示例元组中命名实体的语义搜索可带来更多样的结果，二者各有局限，需融合多种信号与范式。

Method: 提出Thetis++，联合关键词与语义表搜索；系统比较多种排序组合技术（无监督与监督，含TOPSIS）；另研究面向数据湖表搜索的学习排序变体；在语义搜索预过滤的可扩展性上比较LSH索引与HNSW。

Result: 实验揭示许多标准融合方法的局限，并指出学习式方法的有前景方向；HNSW相较LSH使语义表搜索运行时间平均提升4.7倍，同时保持高质量结果表。

Conclusion: 为语义数据湖中高效且有效的数据发现技术给出了路线，兼顾融合效果与语义预过滤的可扩展性。

Abstract: Discovery of relevant datasets within data lakes remains a key challenge. Union and join search are effective techniques for discovering data when augmenting an existing table. In exploratory search, instead, keyword search is commonly adopted to find a relatively small set of highly relevant tables matching some search terms. In this context, semantic search is a new technique that exploits the presence of named entities in exemplar tuples used as queries and in target tables. Hence, keyword search and entity search are able to find a diverse set of tables. In this work, we propose \texttt{Thetis++}, which combines keyword and semantic table search to discover relevant tables that combine the semantic signal with the presence of exact keyword matches. Incorporating multiple paradigms and sources of information allow us to combine the best of multiple worlds. Therefore, we study many alternative techniques for combining rankings and evaluate these for the table search task in data lakes. We explore both unsupervised and supervised techniques, including TOPSIS. Finally, alternative to Thetis++ combining keyword and semantic relevance signals, we study a variant of learning-to-rank for the table search task in data lakes. Our evaluation highlights both the limitations of many standard approaches along with some promising directions offered by learned approaches. Furthermore, while indexes for keyword search are quite efficient, here we provide insights into how to improve the scalability of prefiltering techniques for semantic search, comparing LSH indexes with Hierarchical Navigable Small Worlds. Our experiments show that HNSW drastically improves the runtime of semantic table search by 4.7X on average whilst retaining the high-quality tables. Together, our findings plot the line for effective and efficient data discovery techniques for semantic data lakes.

</details>


### [8] [Can LLMs Resolve Entity Conflicts in Domain Specific NLU Pipelines?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25733)
*Akansha Bhardwaj,Mingbo Cui,Victor Guimaraes,Dominik Kohler,Diana Nicoleta Popa,Meghana M. Reddy,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究比较规则方法、多示例提示的Llama3.1-8B-Instruct与LoRA微调模型在领域NLU流水线实体冲突消解中的表现，发现微调模型在复杂歧义案例上更优，提示法更易适配，二者结合为最佳方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水线式NLU架构中多个抽取器输出存在重叠或冲突，传统基于规则的实体冲突消解方法缺乏灵活性且难以扩展，作者希望探究能否利用大模型的广博知识来消解领域特定NLU流水线中的实体冲突。

Method: 实验对比三种方法：传统规则式冲突消解、基于Llama3.1-8B-Instruct的多示例提示（multi-shot prompting）、以及LoRA微调的Llama3.1-8B-Instruct；评估设两种设置：(1) 宽泛评估LLM匹配现有NLU流水线输出的能力；(2) 在人工标注数据上评估LLM准确率的受控场景。

Result: 多示例提示易于适配、可快速改变行为；LoRA微调模型在复杂、歧义案例上表现更优。

Conclusion: 最佳方案是结合两种策略，以多示例提示的灵活性配合LoRA微调带来的整体准确率提升，从而获得更全面的性能。

Abstract: Resolving entity conflicts is a critical step in Pipeline-based NLU architectures, especially when multiple extractors provide overlapping or conflicting outputs. Traditional rule-based entity conflict resolution methods often lack flexibility and are unscalable. In this work, we aim to study if the broad knowledge of LLMs can be leveraged to resolve entity conflicts in domain specific NLU pipelines. We present an experimental comparison between a traditional rule-based conflict resolution method, and two LLM-based approaches: a multi-shot prompting method using the Llama3.1-8B-Instruct model and a LoRA fine-tuned Llama3.1-8B-Instruct model. The evaluation was conducted under two setups: (1) a broad assessment of LLM's capability to match existing NLU pipeline output, and (2) a more controlled scenario assessing the LLM accuracy on human-labeled data. Results show that while multi-shot prompting offers ease of adaptation and rapid behavioral changes, the fine-tuned model delivers superior performance on complex, ambiguous cases. Ultimately, the best approach appears to be a combination of both strategies, leveraging multi-shot prompting's flexibility alongside the broad accuracy gains of LoRA fine-tuning for a more comprehensive performance.

</details>


### [9] [CACO: Coverage-Aware Coordinated Optimization for Continuous Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25738)
*Pan Zhang,Nan Zhuang,Yongkang Zhang,Kun Xú,Zhongtao Jiang,Zhiwei Wang,Kun Xǔ,Youhuan Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CACO，一种面向流匹配/扩散检索器的轻量自监督后训练框架，将采样查询协调到互补的物品覆盖区域，在无额外推理开销下显著提升召回并降低查询间重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续生成式检索器通过流匹配与扩散在物品嵌入空间采样多个查询做并行ANN搜索，但优化分布多样性或目标级兴趣分离忽略了集合级检索几何：采样查询坍缩到重叠的ANN邻域；同时离散top-K查找与单目标监督阻碍了端到端覆盖优化。

Method: 以采样参考查询的经验检索频率作为相关性代理，构造无标签覆盖目标；用协调查询调整网络（查询间双向注意力+门控相对方向）避免生成大查询池的开销；通过有界切空间投影与球面回缩更新查询；训练时施加切向对偶扰动并以反事实单查询替换估计边际增益，得到非可微ANN搜索下平滑集合覆盖的无偏梯度估计。

Result: 在全部30个骨干-数据集设置中离线贪心选择实验均提升召回；在8个公开基准及自建24分区流式工业数据集上，Recall@100最高提升23.7%，Recall@400最高提升23.9%，查询间重叠降低53.3%–64.9%，推理延迟仅增加0.5%–3.0%。

Conclusion: CACO通过集合级覆盖协调有效缓解查询坍缩，以极低推理代价实现显著召回增益，为连续生成式检索器提供即插即用的后训练方案。

Abstract: Continuous generative retrievers powered by flow matching and diffusion sample multiple queries in item embedding space for parallel approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search under fixed retrieval budgets. However, optimizing distribution diversity or target-level interest separation ignores set-level retrieval geometry: sampled queries collapse onto overlapping ANN neighborhoods, while discrete top-K lookup and single-target supervision block end-to-end coverage optimization. We introduce CACO (Coverage-Aware Coordinated Optimization), a lightweight self-supervised post-training framework that coordinates queries from frozen flow-matching and diffusion retrievers toward complementary item coverage with negligible additional inference latency. CACO uses empirical retrieval frequencies across sampled reference queries as a relevance proxy, formulating a label-free coverage objective that improves recall across all 30 backbone-dataset settings in offline greedy selection experiments. To avoid the inference overhead of generating a large query pool, CACO employs a coordinated query adjustment network that combines inter-query bidirectional attention with gated relative directions, finally updating queries by bounded tangent-space projection and spherical retraction. During training, CACO applies tangential antithetic perturbations and measures each query's marginal gain via counterfactual single-query replacement, yielding an unbiased gradient estimator of smoothed set coverage across non-differentiable ANN search. On eight public benchmarks and an in-house 24-partition streaming industrial dataset, CACO improves Recall@100 by up to 23.7% and Recall@400 by up to 23.9% over the strongest baseline, cuts inter-query overlap by 53.3%-64.9%, and adds only 0.5%-3.0% inference latency.

</details>


### [10] [LINEAGE: A Benchmark for Empirical Lineage Retrieval in AI Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25845)
*Pangjing Wu,Xu Yuan,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lineage 基准，用于 AI 科学文献中的“实证谱系检索”，即以“某论文将前人方法作为实证对比基线”这一关系为监督，构建谱系图并为多跳邻域提供结构评估的真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科研文献检索基准主要用主题相关性或引用链作为监督信号，无法刻画“哪些前人方法被用作实证对比基线”这一关键信息，而这些信息对文献综合与实验规划类工作流至关重要。

Method: 构建流程：定义实证谱系关系（先前论文→明确以其方法为对比基线的源论文），由此组成谱系图，多跳邻域即检索真值；提出 Lineage-Constructor，将异构论文记录与全文实证基线证据转化为校准后的谱系图。数据覆盖 2018—2025 年 17 个顶级 AI 会议/期刊的 121,048 篇论文。

Result: 基准包含 3,950 个图连通分量、67,502 个论文节点、164,948 条谱系边与 80,000 条多面向检索查询。对 19 个代表性检索系统的实验显示：实证谱系检索仍具挑战，性能随谱系距离增大而急剧下降；结构指标表明孤立的检索命中往往无法还原紧密关联的实证对比邻域。

Conclusion: 主题相关性与引用链接不足以支撑实证对比层面的检索需求，需引入谱系图式监督与结构化评估；Lineage 提供了该方向的规模化基准，并揭示多跳结构召回仍是开放难题。

Abstract: Current research agents are moving beyond academic search toward literature synthesis and experiment planning. These workflows increasingly rely on literature retrieval systems, which serve as standalone systems or as components of research agents. However, existing scientific literature retrieval benchmarks mainly evaluate these methods using topical relevance or citation links as supervision, which cannot capture which prior methods are used for empirical comparison. To address this gap, we introduce Lineage, a benchmark for empirical lineage retrieval in AI scientific literature. An empirical lineage relation links a prior paper to a source paper that explicitly uses its method as an empirical comparison baseline. These relations form lineage graphs whose multi-hop neighborhoods provide retrieval ground truth for structural evaluation. To construct these graphs at scale, we develop Lineage-Constructor, which converts heterogeneous paper records and full-paper empirical baseline evidence into calibrated lineage graphs. Built from 121,048 papers across 17 top AI venues from 2018 to 2025, Lineage contains 3,950 graph components, 67,502 paper nodes, 164,948 lineage edges, and 80,000 multi-facet retrieval queries. Experiments with 19 representative retrieval systems show that empirical lineage retrieval remains challenging. Performance drops sharply with lineage distance, while structural metrics reveal that isolated retrieval hits often fail to recover closely connected empirical comparison neighborhoods.

</details>


### [11] [A Task-Level Bias Taxonomy for Historical Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26019)
*Omkar Gavali,Ratan Sebastian,Christoph Rippe,Ralph Ewerth,Anett Hoppe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文考察历史图像馆藏中偏见的不同形式，综述当前缓解方法，并分析偏见如何产生及影响AI支持的检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GLAM机构数字化了大量文化遗产图像，但历史图像元数据稀疏或缺失，检索发现困难；ML/AI虽可生成或丰富元数据，却可能复制或放大偏见（训练数据与历史图像域差异、图像质量退化、黑白照片、历史性偏见描述等），危及检索结果的公平性。

Method: 综述性研究：对历史图像馆藏中各类偏见进行形式化梳理，检视并归类当前的偏见缓解方法，并分析偏见的成因机制及其对AI检索系统的作用路径。

Result: 识别出导致性能下降与偏见引入的关键因素（领域偏移、图像质量退化、单色影像、历史偏见或不完整描述），并归纳相应缓解手段。

Conclusion: 历史图像AI检索中的偏见源于数据与模型多重因素，需系统性认识其成因并采用针对性缓解策略，以提升馆藏可检索性与公平性。

Abstract: In recent decades, galleries, libraries, archives, and museums (GLAM institutions) have digitised vast amounts of cultural heritage material, including historical imagery. A major challenge in making these collections publicly accessible is enabling effective search and discovery. Many historical images contain incomplete, sparse, or no descriptive metadata. Consequently, machine learning (ML) methods are increasingly being used to generate or enrich metadata to improve the searchability of such collections. However, artificial intelligence (AI)-based systems can reproduce or amplify existing biases, particularly when applied to historical imagery. Factors such as differences between the data used to train the models and the historical images on which they are applied, degraded image quality, monochrome photographs, and historically biased or incomplete descriptions can reduce model performance and introduce bias into search and retrieval results. In this paper, we examine different forms of bias in historical image collections and review current methods to mitigate them. We investigate how these biases arise and how they influence AI-supported search and retrieval systems.

</details>


### [12] [From Sessions to Serving: Integrating LLM-Based Embeddings into Retrieval for Production Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26096)
*Zhixiong Hu,Meng Li,Maggie Zhang,Deborshi Goswami,David Bailey,James Bradley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LSER：在检索阶段用户塔中引入基于预训练LLM的会话级与历史级嵌入作为辅助特征，并配合近线异步服务刷新用户向量，实验表明可提升多内容类型推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型市场站点需在异构候选内容（客户案例、产品页、行业页、学习内容）与稀疏且快速变化的用户行为之间做个性化推荐；现有工作多关注下游排序阶段，而检索阶段对用户兴趣表征的要求日益提高，需更好的用户侧表征。

Method: 提出面向autodesk.com的生产级内容推荐系统LSER：(1) 用预训练LLM生成会话级与历史级用户嵌入，与已有学习式序列编码器并列作为检索阶段用户塔的辅助特征；(2) 构建近线（nearline）服务路径，异步刷新主推荐服务的序列向量与LLM派生用户向量，以应对实时行为下的新鲜度问题；(3) 在全量多内容类型目录上做消融实验验证LLM特征的增量价值。

Result: 消融实验表明，将LLM派生嵌入作为额外检索信号并结合生产级近线服务在技术上是可行的，且可提升模型性能。

Conclusion: LLM嵌入可作为检索阶段的有效辅助用户信号，配合近线异步刷新机制具备生产可部署性，为异构内容推荐中的用户表征提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Personalized content recommendation on large marketplace websites must reconcile heterogeneous candidate content (e.g., customer stories, product pages, industry pages, and learning content) with sparse, fast-changing user behavior, placing increasing demands on the retrieval stage to represent user interest well, not just the downstream ranking stage. We present LSER (LLM Session Embedding Retrieval), a production content recommendation system for autodesk.com that derives session- and history-level embeddings from a pretrained large language model (LLM) and adds them as auxiliary user-side features in the retrieval-stage user tower alongside existing learned sequence encoders. To keep user-side embeddings fresh under real-time behavior, we build a nearline serving path that asynchronously refreshes sequential and LLM-derived user vectors for our main content recommendation server. We validate the incremental value of the LLM features through an ablation study across our full multi-content-type catalog. Our results indicate that integrating LLM-derived embeddings as additional retrieval signals, together with production-grade nearline serving, is technically feasible and can improve model performances.

</details>


### [13] [Democratizing Cross-Project Knowledge for Transforming Water Governance and Climate Resilience: an Agentic AI Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26398)
*Oriol Alàs,Ian Palacín,Iván Cester,Edgar Rubión*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一个Agentic AI生态系统，将欧盟资助项目的异构文档整合为领域感知知识库，并通过ReAct式智能体RAG流水线实现自适应检索，MRR相对提升26.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟资助项目产出大量交付物、报告与案例研究，但知识分散在不同项目与文档结构中，决策时难以定位相关且有上下文的信息，缺乏跨项目的知识整合手段。

Method: 将IMPETUS、NATALIE、InnWater三个项目的文档重建为层次化章节树并附结构化元数据索引；采用两个在水与气候领域数据上微调的领域嵌入模型；在其上构建ReAct式控制器，迭代编排检索动作，在多个领域集合间分配检索预算并累积证据，直至满足停止准则。

Result: 在语料检索基准上，相比单域嵌入基线，平均倒数排名（MRR）相对提升26.7%，表明能更一致地检索到相关证据。

Conclusion: 该生态可支撑跨项目知识综合与气候韧性决策，验证了领域微调嵌入与智能体式多轮检索编排相结合的有效性。

Abstract: EU-funded projects produce extensive evidence in the form of deliverables, reports, and case studies. However, this knowledge is typically scattered across projects and document structures, making it difficult to locate relevant, contextual information when making decisions. This paper introduces an Agentic AI Ecosystem that consolidates heterogeneous project documentation into a domain-aware knowledge base and provides adaptive access through an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline. Documents from three EU-funded projects (IMPETUS, NATALIE, and InnWater) are reconstructed into hierarchical section trees and indexed with structured metadata. Retrieval is supported by two domain-specific embedding models fine-tuned on domain data (water and climate). On top of this knowledge base, a ReAct-style controller iteratively orchestrates retrieval actions by allocating retrieval budget across domain collections and accumulating evidence until a stopping criterion is met. In a retrieval benchmark over the corpus, the proposed approach improves Mean Reciprocal Rank by 26.7% (relative) compared to a single-domain embedding baseline, indicating more consistent retrieval of relevant evidence to support cross-project knowledge synthesis and climate-resilient decision-making.

</details>


### [14] [Cost-Aware Agentic Feature Optimization System for Fleet-Scale Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26531)
*Chang Yang,Yao Xuan,Wendan Yan,Nhat Le,Jiang Liu,Longhao Jin,Zhen Peggy Yao,Alireza Vahdatpour,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出端到端LLM agent系统，在跨模型共享与单模型资源约束下自动化优化大规模Web平台排序模型的特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型平台维护大量排序模型，各自从数万特征的共享池中选特征，特征流水线成本（共享）与serving QPS（单模型）约束交织，特征优化高度人工化、组合空间巨大，需要可自动化且守住预算的方案。

Method: LLM agent提出假设、确定性工具执行并验证实验；每模型由LLM从组合候选空间生成并初筛交叉特征；用多种重要性方法对特征排序，经feature blending融合为单一选择（多任务模型用任务间Pareto前沿）；LLM Researcher agent在资源上限下以10–25次异步试验调优选择；trimming步骤控制流水线成本。

Result: 各生产模型在自身预算内归一化熵平均提升0.31%；trimming使流水线成本降低2.3%且退化不超过测量噪声；优化后模型已上线，在线A/B测试主指标提升0.20%。

Conclusion: LLM agent + 确定性工具的混合范式可在真实生产多模型fleet中可靠地做特征优化，兼顾效果提升与资源约束，并取得可测量的线上收益。

Abstract: Large Web platforms operate large fleets of ranking models, each selecting features from a shared pool of tens of thousands. We present an end-to-end agentic system that optimizes features across the fleet under resource constraints shared across models and those set per model, such as feature pipeline cost and serving throughput (QPS), respectively. In this system, LLM agents propose hypotheses, and deterministic tools execute experiments and verify them. For each model, an LLM generates and shortlists cross features from a combinatorial candidate space, and all features are then ranked by several importance methods. Feature blending mixes these rankings into a single selection, using Pareto fronts across tasks for multi-task models. An LLM Researcher agent then tunes the feature selection in 10--25 asynchronous trials under resource caps, and a trimming step keeps the pipeline cost within budget. Across production models, the system improves normalized entropy by 0.31% on average within each model's budget, and the trimming step cuts pipeline cost by 2.3% with no regression beyond measurement noise. The optimized models were launched, and in online A/B tests they improved a primary online metric by 0.20%.

</details>

### [15] [Asymmetric rhetorical strategies drive partisanship in television news](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25140)
*Upasana Dutta,Homa Hosseinmardi,Amir Ghasemian,Aaron Clauset,Duncan J. Watts*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究用大语言模型分析2013–2025年六家美国主要电视台逾40万条新闻节目，构建电视新闻党派偏见的多维测量，识别出8个可解释语言特征（解释73%党派性方差），揭示各台在修辞策略上的系统性不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电视仍是美国主要政治信息来源，但关于电视新闻中党派性如何被表达的系统性证据有限；既有研究多将党派性视为一维构念，难以刻画意识形态偏见背后的修辞策略。

Method: 基于LLM对40万余条新闻节目进行大规模文本分析，覆盖六家主要电视台2013–2025年内容；提取8个可解释的语言学特征构建多维党派偏见指标；以人工标注评分者进行效度验证，并用另外四个政治倾向不同的LLM进行复现。

Result: 8个语言特征解释电视新闻党派性方差的73%。MSNBC与CNN在共和党总统任期内左移、民主党任期内趋中；Fox News则在每次大选后无论结果如何都强化保守立场。MSNBC与CNN更多攻击共和党行为体而非强化民主党立场，Fox News则强化共和党观点远多于攻击民主党观点；Fox News中“内部赞扬—外部攻击”关联强度约为MSNBC的三倍。

Conclusion: 电视新闻的意识形态倾向通过系统性的、台际差异明显的修辞策略传达，而非单一维度；该多维测量框架为媒体效果、受众态度与政治传播研究提供新路径。

Abstract: Television remains a dominant source of political information in the United States, yet systematic evidence on how partisanship is expressed in televised news remains limited. Prior research has largely treated partisanship as a one-dimensional construct, offering limited insight into the rhetorical strategies that underlie ideological bias. Here, by analyzing over 400,000 news episodes using large language models (LLMs), we provide a multi-dimensional measure of partisanship bias in television news across six major U.S. news stations (2013--2025). We identify eight interpretable linguistic features that explain 73% of partisanship variance in television news.\ Our findings reveal systematic asymmetries across stations. While MSNBC and CNN shifted leftward during Republican presidencies and moderated under Democratic presidencies, Fox News increased its conservative alignment following each election regardless of the electoral outcome. Beyond aggregate trends, MSNBC and CNN emphasized attacks on Republican actors more than reinforcement of Democratic positions, whereas Fox News reinforced Republican viewpoints substantially more than it attacked Democratic viewpoints. The association between in-group praise and out-group attacks was also threefold stronger on Fox News than on MSNBC. These results are validated against trained human raters and replicated with four additional LLMs with differing political leanings. Together, our study reveals systematic station-level differences in the rhetorical strategies with which ideological slant is communicated in television news, opening new opportunities for future research on media effects, audience attitudes, and political communication.

</details>


### [16] [Crisis Governance at the Joints. Mapping the Ethical and Societal Territory of Crisis Management Technologies: The Case of Human-Swarm Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25202)
*Anthony Longo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"3i框架"（Interface界面、Infrastructure基础设施、Imaginary想象域）及其间四种"翻译"机制，用于系统梳理人-集群协同（HST）在危机治理中的伦理与社会问题，主张责任鸿沟与有意义人类控制侵蚀产生于层级交界处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机与灾害治理日益依赖感知、分类与行动速度超过人类的智能技术。无人机集群与操作员协同的HST有望提升灾情态势感知，但同时带来人类控制、责任归属、监控、分诊、两用风险与公共合法性等争议。既有讨论往往逐层孤立地处理这些问题，缺乏跨层整合视角，因此需要一个能刻画问题如何在技术、组织与社会规范之间生成的分析框架。

Method: 采用批判性文献综述方法，横跨技术哲学、政治理论、道德心理学、心智哲学、AI伦理以及批判性灾害研究与安全研究。在此基础上构建3i框架：Interface（操作员与集群协同交互层）、Infrastructure（组织分配任务、决策与责任的层）、Imaginary（法律、政策与社会共有假设界定"何谓危机"及何种干预被正当化）。框架核心在于四类"翻译"：习惯固化为规则、惯例转为叙事、原则转为程序、程序转为提示词（prompts）。并以两个具体案例加以阐释。

Result: 论文给出HST伦理社会议题的层级地图，指出责任鸿沟与有意义人类控制的侵蚀等最紧迫问题并非发生于单一层级，而是通过四种"翻译"在层级交界处生成；据此推导出对设计者、应急组织和政策制定者的设计与治理启示，并附带一组面向实践者的追问清单。

Conclusion: HST的伦理治理不能逐层分而治之，必须关注界面、基础设施与想象域之间的衔接与转译过程；治理与设计应针对这些"接缝"设置干预点，以维护有意义的人类控制、明晰责任分配并保障公共合法性。

Abstract: Crisis and disaster governance increasingly relies on technologies that sense, classify and act faster than humans can. Drone swarms coordinated with human operators, known as human-swarm teaming, provide a productive case to review the field of crisis governance. They promise better situational awareness in disaster response, but they also raise questions about human control, responsibility, surveillance, triage, dual use and public legitimacy. This report maps the ethical and societal territory that HST opens up. It unpacks this territory with a 3i-framework, the Interface, where operators and swarms work together, the Infrastructure, where organisations distribute tasks, decisions and blame, and the Imaginary, where law, policy and shared societal assumptions define what a crisis is and which interventions it justifies. The central claim is that these concerns cannot be addressed one level at a time. Many of the most pressing problems, such as responsibility gaps and the erosion of meaningful human control, arise at the joints between levels, through four translations, habits harden, routines become stories, principles become procedures, and procedures become prompts. Drawing on a critical review of literature across philosophy of technology, political theory, moral psychology, philosophy of mind, AI ethics, and critical disaster and security studies, the report develops this framework, illustrates it with two worked examples, and derives design and governance implications and a set of questions for developers, emergency organisations and policymakers.

</details>


### [17] [Whose Safety? Addressing Anti-Blackness in the Age of AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25302)
*Mírian Silva,Serena Oduro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对2020–2025年十大ML/NLP/AI伦理会议69,290篇录用主会论文的两阶段语言模型辅助内容分析，发现AI安全研究占比从0.5%升至6.4%并超越AI伦理，但其高度依赖自动化指标、极少直接涉及社会性危害；2,182篇安全论文中仅8篇提及种族、0篇以之为核心、0篇点名黑人/非洲侨民群体，由此论证不直面反黑人主义就无法实现真正的AI安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全借政治与资本影响力成为AI评估的主导框架，压过AI伦理，使关注焦点从当下影响公众的现实危害转向被框定为"存在性风险"的威胁。作者以Sylvia Wynter对"人（Man）"之"过度表征"的批判为理论基础，指出现代西方的人之概念建立在将黑人性臣属化与他者化之上，因此希望以实证方式检验：在AI安全话语兴起的时代，反黑人问题在学术生产中究竟被如何处理。

Method: 两阶段、语言模型辅助的内容分析；语料为2020–2025年十个顶尖机器学习、自然语言处理与AI伦理会议的69,290篇录用主会论文。统计安全类与伦理类论文占比变化，并比较二者对自动化指标依赖度与直接提及社会性危害的比例；再在安全论文与种族聚焦论文中检索种族、黑人及非洲侨民相关表述及其论述地位。

Result: 安全研究占比从2020年的0.5%增长至2025年的6.4%，超过伦理研究的份额。安全论文中94%依赖自动化指标，仅11%提及直接影响社会的危害，而伦理论文该比例为78%。2,182篇安全论文中仅8篇提及种族，无一以种族为核心议题，也无一明确点名黑人群体或非洲侨民社群。全部254篇被识别为以种族为主题的论文中，仅3篇以"安全"术语描述种族危害，且55%发表于三个伦理会议，而这些会议仅占语料总量的2.5%。

Conclusion: AI安全的主导地位伴随着对社会性危害与种族（尤其是反黑人主义）议题的系统性边缘化，相关研究高度集中在少数伦理场域。作者据此主张，公正的技术未来必须以直接面对并处理反黑人主义为前提，并以此提出跨学科审视AI安全的必要性。

Abstract: "AI safety" has grown from a niche contingent of actors into the dominant framing for artificial intelligence (AI) assessment and evaluation, overshadowing AI ethics. Through political and monetary influence, AI safety has shifted political focus within the US and abroad. The focus has shifted away from harms that currently affect the public toward threats framed as "existential risks". We argue that safety cannot be achieved while anti-Blackness is left unaddressed. We ground this argument in Sylvia Wynter's critique of the "overrepresentation" of Man: the modern, Western conception of the Human that is predicated on the subjugation and othering of Blackness. To analyze the state of addressing anti-Blackness in the age of AI safety, we conduct a two-stage, language model-assisted content analysis of 69,290 accepted main-track papers from ten leading machine learning (ML), natural language processing (NLP), and AI ethics conferences (2020--2025). Safety research grew from 0.5% of accepted papers in 2020 to 6.4% in 2025, overtaking ethics. Yet 94% of safety papers rely on automated metrics, and only 11% of safety papers mention harms that affect society directly, compared with 78% of ethics papers. Of the 2,182 safety papers, 8 mention race; none make it central, and none name Black or African-diaspora communities. Across all 254 identified as race-focused papers, only 3 describe racial harm in safety terms, and 55% appear at three ethics venues that publish 2.5% of the corpus. We offer this interdisciplinary examination of AI safety to argue that a just technological future depends on confronting anti-Blackness directly.

</details>


### [18] [AI-Driven Transformation of Digital Platform Work: A Task-Based Model for Measuring Labour Disruption and Vulnerability, with Early Evidence from Online Freelancing in Kenya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25585)
*Farhan Latif*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个任务导向框架，将AI驱动的"扰动"（工作方式改变程度）与"生计脆弱性"区分开，并应用于肯尼亚内罗毕在线自由职业者的四类平台工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI对劳动力影响的评估多依赖传统职业分类或宏观市场指标，无法反映同一职业内部任务在不同工作安排下的差异；在线自由职业者多为远程工作且大量时间用于无酬任务，这一盲区尤为突出。

Method: 构建任务层面的分析框架，将AI驱动的扰动（disruption）与相应的生计脆弱性（livelihood vulnerability）分离开来；将框架应用于写作、虚拟助理、转录、网络与软件开发四类平台工作，评估自动化、替代与增强的可能性；并与肯尼亚内罗毕52名在线自由职业者的调查及20次后续访谈进行对照。

Result: 模型估计四类工作均面临高扰动，但生计脆弱性得分较低且因职业而异；无酬任务上的效率提升在一定程度上抵消了有酬工作的潜在工资影响。访谈显示，工人报告任务执行方式发生变化、竞争加剧，并通过技能调整与策略性使用AI来塑造其对失业的担忧。

Conclusion: 任务导向建模可为评估平台工作中的AI驱动变化提供基线；工人实际体验与模型预测存在差异，这有助于解释这些变化如何威胁其生计。

Abstract: Assessments of AI's impact on labour often rely on conventional occupational categories or broad market indicators. This dominant trend, however, obscures how tasks within the same occupation vary in practice across work arrangements. This limitation is particularly important in online freelancing, where workers are typically remote and devote substantial time performing unpaid tasks. This article introduces a task-based framework that distinguishes AI-driven disruption, defined as the extent of change in how work is performed, from livelihood vulnerability associated with that change. The framework is subsequently applied to assess the potential for automation, substitution, and enhancement across four categories of platform work: writing, virtual assistance, transcription, and web and software development. The model estimates high disruption across all four categories, while livelihood vulnerability scores are lower and vary by occupation. Potential efficiency gains on unpaid tasks partly offset potential wage impact associated with paid work in the model. The article compares these estimates with evidence from a survey of 52 online freelancers and 20 follow-up interviews in Nairobi, Kenya. Workers report changes in how they perform tasks and intensifying competition, while their accounts show how adapting their skills and using AI strategically shape their concerns about job loss. The findings show how task-based modelling can provide a baseline for assessing AI-driven changes in platform work and how workers' experiences differ from projections and help explain how these changes may threaten their livelihoods.

</details>


### [19] [KeyMem: Temporal Occupancy Control for Robust Streaming VideoLLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25649)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Xiao Cheng,Christy Jie Liang,Xi Zheng,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出流式VideoLLM的视觉token缓存淘汰若仅依据分数，会让短暂显著片段垄断缓存、挤占后续所需证据，即"时间占用失控"；提出免训练的KeyMem：对基础分数做秩归一化、将视频流划分为固定时长时间桶并对每桶保留token数设硬上限，并可选地以密钥采样保留者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数的淘汰机制让视频流自身决定其时间占用，短时显著片段可长期占据缓存并替换掉后续需要的证据；作者将此不受控的时间占用识别为根本性局限。

Method: KeyMem（免训练包装器）：1）对任意基础重要性分数进行秩归一化；2）把视频流切分为固定时长的时间桶，限制每个桶可保留的视觉token数量（硬性配额）；3）可选地用密钥对存活token进行采样。另含"一次决策"（decide-once）变体。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，20秒烧录字幕占8秒记忆的84%，使全片段准确率由79.4%降至58.3%。在五种自然压力、三个VideoLLM、两种预算下，时间配额将较小预算下基于注意力的保留方法的最差准确率由42.2%提升至73.3–76.1%，并在三个模型中的两个上比InfiniPot-V高6.1–16.7%。自然压力下确定性与密钥配额表现相当；自适应对手下，秘密密钥比泄露密钥多保留4.4–9.5%的早段准确率，但不能稳定优于最佳确定性配额。decide-once变体可保留被后续采样侵蚀的旧证据。

Conclusion: 时间维度的配额分配是鲁棒性的首要控制手段；保密性仅用于对抗可预测性攻击场景。

Abstract: Streaming VideoLLMs retain a bounded cache of visual tokens. Yet score-based eviction lets the stream determine its own temporal occupancy, so a short salient segment can monopolize the cache and displace evidence needed later. We identify this uncontrolled temporal occupancy as a fundamental limitation and introduce KeyMem, a training-free wrapper that rank-normalizes any base score, divides the video stream into fixed-duration temporal buckets and limits how many visual tokens each bucket can retain, and optionally samples survivors with a secret key. This hard cap bounds a segment's memory share from its first chunk. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, 20 s of burned-in captions occupies 84% of an 8 s memory and lowers accuracy over all clips from 79.4% to 58.3%. Across five natural stressors, three VideoLLMs and two budgets, temporal quotas raise the worst accuracy of attention-based retention at the smaller budget from 42.2% to 73.3-76.1% and exceed that of InfiniPot-V by 6.1-16.7% on two of the three models. Deterministic and keyed quotas perform comparably under natural stress. Under an adaptive adversary, a secret key preserves 4.4-9.5% more early-clip accuracy than a leaked key, but does not consistently beat the best deterministic quota. A decide-once variant keeps old evidence that fresh draws erode. Temporal allocation is the primary robustness control, with secrecy reserved for adversarial predictability.

</details>


### [20] [Neighbourhood Walkability and Cardiometabolic Morbidity in Greater London: A Fine-Grained Spatial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26573)
*Benjamin T. Pike,Stephen Law,Sanja Šćepanović,Linus W. Dietz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在大伦敦4,994个普查单元中，步行性越高与8种心血管代谢疾病患病率越低相关，但该关联大部分可由城市宏观空间结构（中心—边缘梯度及空间自相关）解释，表明全市尺度系数不可直接解读为独立的邻里效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管代谢疾病是主要健康负担，支持日常步行的邻里环境可能降低其风险。已有研究多报告步行性高的邻里疾病患病率较低，但步行性作用于多个空间尺度，全市层面的关联可能把本地邻里形态与大都市整体结构及共变环境暴露混为一谈，因此需明确区分全局与局部空间结构。

Method: 构建开放的五组分步行性指数，覆盖大伦敦4,994个普查单元；从基层医疗登记获取8种心血管代谢疾病患病率；在调整人口构成与剥夺指数后估计关联；用径向模型刻画中心—边缘梯度；在自治市内部及中心—边缘调整后比较系数变化；进行空间误差调整；并以保留同等空间结构的替代步行性表面作为对照。

Result: 步行性每提高1个标准差，高血压患病率低1.43个百分点，8种结局均呈负相关；径向模型解释步行性变异的74.2%；系数在自治市内降低2–43%，中心—边缘调整后降低11–75%；空间误差调整后肥胖无明确关联，其余7种系数相对可比的log尺度模型降低83–99%；但观察关联仍比替代步行性表面更极端（主分析8种结局，NOx调整后5种）。

Conclusion: 全市尺度的步行性系数不应被解释为独立于大都市结构的邻里效应；研究邻里形态与健康时须显式分离全局与局部空间结构。

Abstract: Cardiometabolic diseases are leading causes of ill health, and neighbourhoods that support everyday walking may help reduce them. Studies often report lower disease prevalence in more walkable neighbourhoods. However, because walkability operates across multiple spatial scales, city-wide associations may conflate local neighbourhood form with broader metropolitan structure and co-varying environmental exposures. Here we show that while much of the walkability--morbidity association in Greater London operates through the city's macro-scale spatial structure, local alignment persists beyond it. Using an open five-component walkability index for 4,994 London census units and recorded prevalence of eight cardiometabolic conditions from primary-care registers, we found that higher walkability was inversely associated with all eight outcomes after adjusting for demographic composition and deprivation; one standard deviation higher walkability corresponded to 1.43 percentage points lower hypertension prevalence. Walkability followed a centre--periphery gradient, with a radial model explaining 74.2\% of its variation. Coefficients were reduced by 2--43\% within boroughs and 11--75\% after centre--periphery adjustment. After spatial-error adjustment, no clear association remained for obesity; coefficients for the other seven were reduced by 83--99\% relative to comparable log-scale models. Nevertheless, observed associations were more extreme than those from surrogate walkability surfaces preserving this spatial structure, for all eight outcomes in the primary analyses and for five after adjustment for nitrogen oxides. These findings caution against interpreting city-wide walkability coefficients as neighbourhood effects independent of wider metropolitan structure, and highlight the need for studies of neighbourhood form and health to disentangle global and local spatial structure explicitly.

</details>


### [21] [A Success Model at the End of Its Life Cycle: 30 Years of Regionalised Regional Rail in Germany](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26583)
*Lukas Iffländer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 德国区域化改革（1996年将区域客运铁路责任下放各州）使客运周转量翻倍、竞争性招标显著降低运营成本，但组织模式已到生命周期末端：平均仅1.3份投标、26个招标机构与约60个交通联盟结构重叠、车辆采购风险转嫁乘客、欧盟法院2026年推翻轨道使用费上限致资金无法覆盖订购运量。文章回顾动因与成效，识别三大结构性设计缺陷，主张第二轮改革：整合机构、标准化车辆、在"修复的市场模式"与"一体化公营"间抉择、并将资金与真实基础设施成本挂钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估德国铁路区域化（Regionalisierung）三十年成效并诊断其组织模式的可持续性问题，为第二轮改革提供依据。核心动机在于：尽管区域化被视为德国铁路改革最成功的部分，但其制度架构已出现招标竞争不足、机构重叠、风险分配失当与资金缺口等系统性失灵。

Method: 政策与制度分析（综述性研究）：梳理区域化的动因与结果，归纳出三项结构性设计缺陷，并据此提出制度再设计方案（机构整合、车辆标准化、市场模式与公营模式的路径选择、资金指数化）。

Result: 成效方面：客运周转量翻倍，竞争性招标大幅降低运营成本。问题方面：招标平均仅吸引1.3份投标；26个招标机构与约60个交通联盟存在结构重叠；车辆采购多次将风险转嫁给乘客；2026年欧盟法院裁定德国轨道使用费上限无效后，资金不再覆盖所订购的服务量。

Conclusion: 区域化的组织模式已走到生命周期终点，需要进行第二次改革：合并整合招标机构、推进车辆标准化、明确在"修复的市场竞争模式"与"一体化公营运营"之间作出选择，并将资金与基础设施实际成本发展指数化挂钩。

Abstract: On 1 January 1996, Germany transferred responsibility for regional passenger rail services from the federal government to the states. Three decades later, passenger-kilometres have doubled and competitive tendering has lowered operating costs substantially, making regionalisation the most successful element of the German rail reform. Its organisational model, however, has reached the end of its life cycle: tenders now attract on average only 1.3 bids, 26 contracting authorities and some 60 transport associations maintain overlapping structures, rolling-stock procurement has repeatedly shifted risk onto passengers, and since the Court of Justice of the European Union struck down the German track-access charge cap in 2026, funding no longer covers the ordered service volume. This article reviews the motives and results of regionalisation, identifies three structural design flaws, and argues for a second reform: consolidating authorities, standardising rolling stock, deciding between a repaired market model and integrated public operation, and indexing funding to real infrastructure cost development. The submission contains an English translation followed by the German original.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [22] [PsyWorld: Profile-Conditioned World Modeling for Human Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24999)
*Ziyuan Qin,Yang Qiao,Summer B. Frandsen,Michael T. Treadway,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"以心理画像为条件的状态世界模型"：用真实环境—行为状态转移做监督，先由后验编码器量化真实转移，再由先验从心理画像与转移前上下文预测离散码，从而以"转移监督"而非"画像直接映射动作"的方式实现个体泛化行为预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人化智能体直接把静态心理画像作为动作条件，未把已实现的状态转移当作显式监督信号，浪费了时间变化状态所蕴含的信息；本文希望同时建模稳定特质与动态状态，并以结构化转移监督提升对未见个体的行为预测泛化。

Method: 在训练阶段用后验编码器对已实现的状态转移进行量化得到离散码；先验网络仅由心理画像与转移前上下文预测该码。先验码与预测的下一状态共同条件化宏观行为预测与基于模拟器的执行。评测于VEET（受控行为模拟器，含人类轨迹）与AgentSociety（结构不同的合成环境），采用参与者不重叠划分为240训练/25验证/48测试。

Result: VEET上Top-1准确率0.904（95% CI [0.895, 0.913]），Macro KL 0.0033。消融显示Top-1提升主要来自连续下一状态建模，离散码与先验对齐主要改善Top-3与分布层面的一致性。

Conclusion: 转移信息驱动的世界模型监督对主体泛化的行为预测有效，但不意味着因果心理机制，也不保证校准的长时程状态动态。

Abstract: Understanding how stable psychometric traits and time-varying states jointly inform behavior is an important problem in computational behavioral science. Existing personalized agents commonly condition actions directly on a static profile, leaving realized state transitions unused as an explicit source of supervision. We introduce a profile-conditioned state world model that uses observed environment--behavior state transitions to learn transition-informed representations for behavioral prediction. During training, a posterior encoder quantizes realized transitions, while a prior predicts the resulting codes from the psychometric profile and pre-transition context. The prior code and predicted next state then condition macro-level behavior and simulator-grounded execution. Thus, the world model provides structured transition supervision instead of treating personality as a direct action descriptor. We evaluate the framework in VEET, a controlled behavioral simulator with human trajectories, and in AgentSociety as a structurally different synthetic environment. A participant-disjoint split of 240 training, 25 validation, and 48 test participants evaluates generalization to unseen people. On VEET, the model reaches 0.904 Top-1 accuracy (95\% CI: [0.895, 0.913]) and 0.0033 Macro KL. Ablations associate continuous next-state modeling with most of the Top-1 gain, while discrete codes and prior alignment mainly improve Top-3 and distributional alignment. These results establish transition-informed world-model supervision as useful for subject-generalized behavior prediction, without implying causal psychological mechanisms or calibrated long-horizon state dynamics.

</details>


### [23] [Watch Where You Linearize: Predicting and Correcting Model-Merging Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25000)
*Ido Nitzan Hidekel,Dan Raviv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析任务向量方向导数何时能预测合并干扰，提出在接收端模型处冻结局部响应构造仿射代理，进而用无标签凸教师匹配优化系数得到 LR-Merge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并通常需先构造并评估候选合并才能衡量干扰，系数搜索代价高昂，缺乏廉价可靠的干扰预测信号。

Method: 研究任务 A 输出沿任务 B 向量的方向导数：在接收端模型上评估可预测单任务/单样本的邻近合并；全多任务合并需沿合并路径两次评估。冻结接收端局部响应得到对合并系数仿射的代理，据此提出 LR-Merge——在无标签探针上做凸教师匹配优化系数，无需对合并网络反传。

Result: 接收端导数将无变化基线的精度误差减半，识别出多数预测翻转样本；共享预训练骨干处导数在视觉实验中近乎正交，在 LoRA 语言模型试点仅部分预测性，失配源于沿接收端自身任务向量的位移。LR-Merge 提升所有测试的闭式、各向同性与稀疏化基合并器，任务越多增益越大；用预训练响应替代接收端局部响应反而使基合并退化；与经合并网络的精确教师匹配收敛快约三倍、同算力下表现相当，代理可在秒级回答新系数与任务子集查询。

Conclusion: 干扰预测的导数须在接收端模型处评估才有效；接收端局部响应冻结所成的仿射代理使无标签、低成本的合并系数优化可行且可扩展。

Abstract: Model merging combines separately fine-tuned models by adding their task vectors to a shared backbone. Interference is typically assessed only after constructing and evaluating a candidate merge, making coefficient search expensive. We show that a first-order directional derivative of task A's outputs along task B's vector can predict interference, but only when evaluated at the appropriate model.
  At the receiver model, this derivative forecasts nearby merges per task and per example, halves the accuracy error of a no-change baseline, and identifies most examples whose predictions flip. For full multi-task merges, a single evaluation is insufficient; two evaluations along the merge path are needed. At the shared pretrained backbone, however, the derivative is nearly orthogonal to realized changes in our vision experiments and only partly predictive in LoRA language-model pilots. Controls link this failure to displacement along the receiver's own task vector, not simply parameter distance; the mismatch appears within the first few dozen fine-tuning steps.
  Freezing receiver-local responses produces a surrogate affine in the merge coefficients. We use it to construct LR-Merge, a label-free correction that optimizes coefficients through convex teacher matching on unlabeled probes, without backpropagation through merged networks. LR-Merge improves all tested closed-form, isotropic, and sparsified base mergers, with gains generally increasing as more tasks are merged. Using pretrained rather than receiver-local responses instead degrades the base merge. LR-Merge matches exact teacher matching through a merged network, which takes roughly three times longer to converge and performs similarly at an equal compute budget. Once built, the surrogate answers new coefficient and task-subset queries in seconds.

</details>


### [24] [Density Feedback Control for Transferability of Generative Sparse Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25001)
*Dongsu Song,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出密度反馈控制（DFC），在每轮训练结束时依据观测到的硬掩码密度与目标密度之差，动态收紧或放松稀疏系数，无需修改生成器架构或损失，即可在单次训练中将稀疏对抗扰动的密度精确控制在目标值附近并提升迁移攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成器的稀疏对抗攻击仅通过一个固定的稀疏系数间接控制扰动密度：系数到密度的映射未知且在训练过程中漂移，达到目标密度需反复调参；同时该固定压力在密度下降开始后几乎不再带来迁移性收益。

Method: DFC 在每个 epoch 结束时，根据观测到的硬掩码密度与目标密度之间的差距，对稀疏系数进行收紧或放松的反馈调节；不改变生成器架构与损失函数。并在 GPG 训练过程中追踪系数放松与迁移 ASR 的关系，另对将扰动限制在源模型特征重要性区域的 EGS-TSSA 做分析。

Result: 在 10% 目标密度下，DFC 在单次运行中将三种基攻击的平均密度控制在距目标 1 个百分点以内，并提升其中两种在 CNN 与 Transformer 目标模型上的迁移 ASR（包括逐图像 ℓ0 预算情形），且扰动像素更少。GPG 训练追踪显示：随 DFC 放松系数，迁移 ASR 随密度恢复而回升，并以更少像素超过固定系数基线。对 EGS-TSSA，DFC 未带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 以密度反馈替代固定稀疏系数可有效解决密度不可控与迁移性受损问题，是一种与架构、损失解耦的通用稀疏攻击控制手段；但当攻击自带空间约束（如限制于特征重要性区域）时，密度反馈与约束存在交互，收益不一致，需针对性分析。

Abstract: Generator-based sparse adversarial attacks train encoder--decoder networks to produce transferable sparse perturbations for black-box robustness evaluation. Their perturbation density, however, is set only indirectly by a fixed sparsity coefficient whose mapping to density is unknown and shifts during training, so reaching a target density requires re-tuning; this fixed pressure also yields little transferability gain once density reduction begins. We propose Density Feedback Control (DFC), which tightens or relaxes this coefficient at the end of each epoch according to the gap between the observed hard-mask density and the target, without changing the generator architecture or loss. At a 10\% target density, DFC brings the mean density of three base attacks within one percentage point of the target in a single run and improves the transfer attack success rate (ASR) of two of them on CNN and Transformer targets, including under per-image $\ell_0$ budgets, while perturbing fewer pixels. Tracing GPG's training, we observe that as DFC relaxes the coefficient, the transfer ASR recovers with the density and overtakes the fixed-coefficient baseline with fewer pixels. For EGS-TSSA, which restricts perturbations to source-model feature-importance regions, DFC yields no consistent gain; we analyze how this restriction interacts with density feedback.

</details>


### [25] [A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25003)
*Manuel Prada-Corral,Yahya Emara,Timothy J. O'Donnell,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将多样化生成形式化为从确定性点过程(DPP)中采样大小为K的完整字符串子集，提出PoolDPP：从易处理提议分布抽取有限候选池、对候选进行重要性加权、在诱导池核上运行K-DPP采样，并给出KL散度以O(1/N)收敛的有限样本界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型单样本质量高但集合质量差：重复样本聚集在同一语义模式上。需要一种能同时刻画质量与多样性的集合级生成框架。

Method: 以DPP定义目标分布：似然编码样本质量，嵌入几何编码相似输出间的排斥；由于全字符串空间上精确推断不可行，提出PoolDPP——由可处理提议分布抽取有限候选池，用重要性权重将候选拉向目标质量分布，在诱导的池核上运行K-DPP采样器，并给出贪心MAP变体以实现确定性选择。

Result: 证明带显式常数的有限样本界：采样器输出与目标K-DPP之间的KL散度随池规模N以O(1/N)衰减。实验上，PoolDPP在开放式生成上扩展质量—多样性前沿，在歧义text-to-SQL(Ambrosia)上扩展精度—召回前沿，在数学推理(GSM8K)上于近似相同的pass@K下提升多样性。

Conclusion: 通过重要性加权的候选池化，把不可行的全空间K-DPP推断转化为可行的池上采样，理论上保证渐近收敛，实践上在多类任务中改善质量—多样性权衡。

Abstract: Language models often produce high-quality individual samples but poor sets, with repeated samples clustering around the same semantic modes. We formalize diverse generation as sampling a size-$K$ subset of complete strings from a determinantal point process (DPP), where the likelihood encodes item quality, and the embedding geometry encodes repulsion between similar outputs. Exact inference over all strings is intractable, so we propose PoolDPP: draw a finite candidate pool from a tractable proposal, importance-weight the candidates toward the target quality, and run a $K$-DPP sampler on the induced pool kernel; a greedy MAP variant provides deterministic selection. We prove a finite-sample bound with an explicit constant: the KL divergence between the sampler's output and the target $K$-DPP decays as O(1/N) in the pool size. Empirically, PoolDPP extends the quality--diversity frontier on open-ended generation and the precision--recall frontier on ambiguous text-to-SQL (Ambrosia), and on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), it raises diversity at about the same pass@$K$.

</details>


### [26] [On the Impact of Feature Reduction Techniques on Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25004)
*Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析特征降维技术对多种机器学习模型在投毒、后门与通用对抗扰动攻击下对抗鲁棒性的影响，发现在APT数据集上，排列重要性排序与单变量互信息通常能提供最一致的鲁棒性提升，而PCA与递归特征消除的效果随分类器与攻击场景变化较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征降维被广泛用于降低数据维度、缩短训练时间、缓解维度灾难、防止过拟合及去除冗余特征，但其对不同分类器及不同攻击场景下对抗鲁棒性的影响尚缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在APT数据集上，针对支持向量机、决策树、随机森林、多层感知机与k近邻五类模型，比较主成分分析、排列重要性排序、递归特征消除与单变量互信息四种特征降维技术，在投毒攻击、后门攻击和通用对抗扰动攻击下的表现；以准确率、F1分数与缓解分数作为评价指标。

Result: 排列重要性排序与单变量互信息总体带来最一致的鲁棒性提升；PCA与递归特征消除的效果波动较大，依赖具体分类器与攻击类型；各降维技术对准确率与F1分数的影响也存在差异。

Conclusion: 特征降维技术并非普遍提升对抗鲁棒性，其效果具有明显的技术—模型—攻击场景依赖性；在实际防御设计中应优先考虑排列重要性排序与单变量互信息，并需针对具体分类器与威胁模型进行实证验证。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks have posed significant threats to machine learning models by exploiting their inherent vulnerabilities. Researchers have established an interdisciplinary field called adversarial machine learning to explore these vulnerabilities and develop corresponding defense strategies. Feature reduction strategies are used to decrease the dimensionality of the data in order to reduce training time, avoid the curse of dimensionality, prevent overfitting, and mitigate redundant features. However, their effect on adversarial robustness across different classifiers and attack scenarios remains insufficiently explored. This study aims to analyze the effect of feature reduction techniques on the adversarial robustness of a diverse set of machine learning models under poisoning, backdoor, and universal adversarial perturbation attacks on the APT dataset. These models include support vector machine, decision tree, random forest, multi-layer perceptron, and k-nearest neighbors. The attained results are compared with scenarios where different feature reduction techniques are applied, including principal component analysis, permutation importance ranking, recursive feature elimination, and univariate mutual information. Metrics such as accuracy, F1-score, and mitigation score are employed to evaluate the effect of these techniques on adversarial robustness. The results demonstrate that permutation importance ranking and univariate mutual information generally provide the most consistent robustness improvements, while other methods exhibit more variable effects depending on the classifier and attack scenario.

</details>


### [27] [Near-Quadratic Sample Compression for All VC Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25011)
*Yang Cai,Vineet Gupta,Yanchen Jiang,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Shay Moran,Grigoris Velegkas,Di Wang,Manfred K. Warmuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了样本压缩弱猜想：每个VC维为$d$的二元概念类都存在大小为$O(d^2\log^2 d)$的样本压缩方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本压缩猜想（Littlestone–Warmuth，1980s；Floyd–Warmuth 1995）断言VC维为$d$的二元概念类可压缩到$O(d)$，弱形式要求$d$的多项式大小。二者长期未解，此前仅知Moran–Yehudayoff (2016)的指数级上界。

Method: 构造性地给出一个样本压缩方案，其大小仅依赖于VC维$d$，达到$O(d^2\log^2 d)$。

Result: 得到大小$O(d^2\log^2 d)$的样本压缩方案，将上界从$d$的指数级改进为多项式级。

Conclusion: 弱样本压缩猜想得证，同时$O(d)$的强猜想仍然开放。

Abstract: The sample compression conjecture is a longstanding open problem in learning theory, originating in the work of Littlestone and Warmuth in the 1980s. At its heart is a connection between simplification and generalization: if a predictor consistent with a training sample is defined using only a small part of that sample, then it is likely to generalize. Sample compression schemes formalize this idea, and many natural learning algorithms can be analyzed this way.
  The sample compression conjecture of Floyd and Warmuth (1995) asserts that every binary concept class of VC dimension $d$ admits a sample compression scheme of size $O(d)$; a weaker conjecture asks only for size polynomial in $d$. Both have remained open: the first bound depending only on $d$, due to Moran and Yehudayoff (2016), is exponential in $d$. We prove the weaker conjecture by constructing a sample compression scheme of size $O(d^2 \log^2 d)$.

</details>


### [28] [HapBO: Harnessed $α$-$π$ Bayesian Optimization via Expert Natural Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25100)
*Jean-Sébastien Giroux,Rose Guay Hottin,Marco Bonizzato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HapBO将自然语言先验一次性编译为可复用信念函数π，通过有界正乘子作用于任意非负采集函数，在无需优化中调用LLM的前提下提升贝叶斯优化样本效率并保证EI的简单遗憾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中的先验知识多以自然语言（专家直觉、文档）形式存在，而非先验引导型BO所需的显式概率分布景观；有效利用它既需要从语言到先验的表达性映射，又需要对可能误导的信息具备鲁棒性。

Method: 在α-πBO基础上提出HapBO：从人类专家、LLM或二者一次性引出信念，确定性地将结构化规格编译为可复用的信念函数π: X→[0,1]，表示不局限于高斯族；再以有界正乘子将π施加于任意非负采集函数，保持正采集支撑且优化过程零LLM调用。

Result: 理论上证明对EI，HapBO即使在对抗性信念下仍以有界因子保持标准简单遗憾率。实验覆盖40个HPOBench任务、6–15维合成套件及皮层神经刺激与约束太阳能设计两个真实应用：取得最低聚合HPOBench遗憾与较强真实任务表现，12–20次查询预算下即改善优化结果，且墙钟与token开销仅为其他LLM类方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: HapBO提供了一种样本高效、鲁棒的先验引导BO框架：将语言先验一次性编译为可复用信念函数，兼顾表达性、理论遗憾保证与零在线推理成本，在基准与真实任务中均具竞争力。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) can be substantially more sample-efficient when informed by prior knowledge about the problem. In practice, however, such knowledge is often available only in natural language, e.g., expert intuitions or documentation, rather than as the explicit probability distribution landscape that prior-guided BO methods may require. Leveraging it effectively demands both an expressive mapping from language to priors and robustness to information that may be misleading. We propose HapBO (Harnessed $α$-$π$ Bayesian Optimization), which elicits beliefs once from a human expert, a large language model (LLM), or both, and deterministically compiles a structured specification into a reusable belief function ($π: \mathcal{X} \to[0,1]$), with a rich representation not restricted to a Gaussian family. Building on $α$-$π$BO, HapBO applies this belief function through a bounded positive multiplier on any nonnegative acquisition, preserving positive acquisition support and requiring no LLM calls during optimization. We prove that, for the Expected Improvement acquisition function (EI), HapBO preserves the standard simple-regret rate up to a bounded factor, even under adversarial beliefs. We evaluate HapBO on 40 HPOBench tasks, a synthetic suite in 6-15 dimensions and two real-world applications: cortical neurostimulation and constrained solar design. HapBO achieves the lowest aggregate HPOBench regret and strong real-world task performance, with zero in-loop token cost. In real-world applications it improves the optimization outcome even at budgets of 12-20 queries, and at a small fraction of the wall-clock and token costs of other LLM-based methods.

</details>


### [29] [Long-Range Video Presentation Attack Detection: A Three-Class Benchmark Across 1-100 m and Two Sensor Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25108)
*Lambert Igene,Mohammad Zahir Uddin Chowdhury,Afzal Hossain,Sandip Purnapatra,Peter Johnson,Stephanie Schuckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个面向远距离（1–100 m）人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）的视频数据集（374,000+帧、261个身份、室内外、DSLR与移动传感器），并标注live/spoof/props三类，基准测试四种CNN架构，揭示采集距离与传感器分辨率是PAD难度的关键但未被充分研究的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸PAD系统部署于边检与机场监控场景，却普遍假设受试者在1米以内近距离采集；缺乏系统涵盖多距离、多环境条件以及同时包含欺骗与遮挡（props）攻击的基准数据集，导致已部署模型在真实远距离场景下准备不足。

Method: 构建基于视频的PAD数据集：261个唯一身份、5个拍摄距离（1–100 m）、室内外条件、DSLR相机（C）与移动传感器（M）双设备，三类标签（live、spoof、props遮挡）；以视频片段而非单帧进行训练与评估；微调并基准测试四种CNN架构（MobileNetV3、MobileNet、EfficientNet、ResNet-50）；并在未见过的攻击数据集上做泛化测试。

Result: 高分辨率DSLR相机在各距离下保持近乎完美的欺骗分类率；低分辨率移动传感器在50 m以外性能急剧下降，MobileNetV3在75 m处props分类率降至55%。EfficientNet整体表现最佳（准确率98.48%，ACER 2.01%）。在未见攻击数据集上的泛化测试达到89–94%准确率，验证了跨域鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 采集距离与传感器分辨率是PAD难度中关键但研究不足的维度；该数据集将在最终用户许可协议（EULA）下公开，以支持可复现的远距离PAD研究。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) systems deployed in border control and airport surveillance are constrained by a critical assumption: subjects are captured at close range, typically under one meter. No existing benchmark systematically addresses multi-distance capture, diverse environmental conditions, and concurrent spoofing and occlusion-based attacks, leaving deployed models underprepared for real-world scenarios. We introduce a video-based PAD dataset of 374,000+ frames from 261 unique identities collected across five standoff distances (1 m-100 m) under indoor and outdoor conditions using a DSLR camera (C) and a mobile sensor (M), with three-class labels: live, spoof, and props (occlusions); models are trained and evaluated on video clips rather than single frames. We fine-tune and benchmark four CNN architectures: MobileNetV3, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and ResNet-50. The higher-resolution DSLR maintains near-perfect spoof classification rate across all distances, while the lower-resolution mobile sensor degrades sharply beyond 50 m, with props classification rate dropping to 55% at 75 m for MobileNetV3. EfficientNet achieves the best overall performance (98.48% accuracy, ACER 2.01%). Generalization tests on unseen attack datasets yield 89-94% accuracy, validating cross-domain robustness. These results establish capture distance and sensor resolution as critical but underexplored axes of PAD difficulty. The dataset will be publicly released under an End-User License Agreement to support reproducible long-range PAD research.

</details>


### [30] [Fast and Memory-Light Evaluation of Density-Based Clusterings with the DISCO Index in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25114)
*Amin Entezari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: discoCVI 0.2.0 借助互可达最小生成树的单链接合并树，将 DISCO 聚类有效性指数的计算改为线性内存、精确计算，显著提升速度并降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DISCO 是考虑噪声标签质量的内部聚类有效性指数，可用于无真值条件下比较聚类结果和选择 HDBSCAN 参数。但其定义基于所有点对之间的密度连通距离，直接实现需存储多个 n×n 矩阵，内存与可扩展性受限。

Method: 将 DISCO 所需的全部量改写为可从互可达最小生成树（mutual-reachability MST）的单链接合并树（single-linkage merge tree）读取；据此实现 R 包 discoCVI 0.2.0，使内存随点数线性增长，运行时间保持与原始定义相同的二次复杂度；并将其与旧版本及原始 Python 实现进行一致性测试与基准测试。

Result: 与原始 Python 实现误差在 3×10⁻¹³ 以内，并修正了单点簇（singleton cluster）的评分以符合发表定义；500–4,000 点时比旧版本快 23–138 倍，500–8,000 点时比 Python 参考实现快 3–9 倍，内存占用均更低；10 万点约 50 秒完成评分，内存不足 200 MB。

Conclusion: 通过合并树表述可精确且低内存地计算 DISCO，使该指数在大规模数据集上实用化，适用于无真值的聚类比较与 HDBSCAN 参数选择。

Abstract: DISCO is a recent internal cluster validity index for density-based clustering that, unlike earlier indices, also evaluates the quality of noise labels. The discoCVI package makes DISCO available in R. This article describes the design of the package and a typical workflow, in which DISCO is used to compare clusterings and to choose the parameters of HDBSCAN without ground truth, followed by the implementation introduced in version 0.2.0. DISCO is defined through density-connectivity distances between all pairs of points, so direct implementations store several n-by-n matrices. We show that every quantity DISCO needs can be read off the single-linkage merge tree of the mutual-reachability minimum spanning tree, so that the index can be computed exactly with memory that grows linearly with the number of points, while the running time remains quadratic as in the original definition. The implementation is tested against the previous version, agrees with the original Python implementation to within $3 \times 10^{-13}$, and corrects the score of singleton clusters to match the published definition. In benchmarks, discoCVI is 23 to 138 times faster than its previous version for 500 to 4,000 points and 3 to 9 times faster than the Python reference for 500 to 8,000 points, using a fraction of the memory of both, and it scores 100,000 points in about 50 seconds with less than 200 MB of memory.

</details>


### [31] [Auditing Learned Dynamics in Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25119)
*Skylar Gu,Christina Last*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过GraphCast的稀疏自编码器特征做消融与激活引导实验，发现因果相关性并不依赖物理语义；物理相关特征可被放大以降低极端事件预报误差（深对流特征放大使七个风暴强度误差中位数降低51%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保障场景部署的AI天气预报模型需要审计其是否依赖可靠机制，因此需回答"因果相关性是否必须伴随物理语义"，并检验物理相关潜方向能否被引导以改善极端事件预报。

Method: 提取GraphCast（DeepMind自回归天气预报模型）内部表征的稀疏自编码器特征；在推演（rollout）过程中对内部激活做特征消融与特征缩放放大/抑制，检验物理语义与因果效用的分离。

Result: 消融一个绑定于GraphCast计算网格、缺乏常规物理语义的特征反而增大全球位势高度误差；抑制对流与低层涡度相关特征削弱热带气旋加深（中心气压下降），放大则降低强度误差，对流特征缩放使七个风暴误差中位数下降51%。

Conclusion: 可解释的物理关联与因果效用可以分离；稀疏特征可作为审计与干预学习型天气动力学的实验坐标。

Abstract: Learned weather models are experimentally accessible computational systems whose internal representations can be inspected and manipulated. Using sparse autoencoder features from GraphCast, DeepMind's autoregressive weather forecasting model, we test whether causal relevance requires physical semantics, a question that matters for auditing whether AI forecasters used in high-assurance settings rely on reliable mechanisms. We also look at whether physically associated latent directions can be steered to improve extreme-event forecasts, where interventions are applied to the model's internal activations during the rollout. We find that ablating a feature locked to GraphCast's computational mesh increases global geopotential height error despite lacking conventional physical semantics. Second, suppressing convection- and low-level-vorticity-associated features weakens predicted tropical-cyclone deepening (the fall in central pressure during intensification), while amplifying them reduces intensity error, with amplification of convection features through feature scaling achieving a median of 51\% error reduction over seven storms. Together, these results separate interpretable, physical association from causal utility and establish sparse features as experimental coordinates for auditing and intervening on learned weather dynamics.

</details>


### [32] [Temporal Extrapolation of Elastic Seismic Wave Propagation in Layered Media Using Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25130)
*Dibyajyoti Nayak,Yuze Pu,Kami Mohammadi,Somdatta Goswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出耦合神经算子框架（TI-DeepONet + FNO残差修正）实现分层弹性介质中应力条件下的速度场时间外推预测，外推区间NRMSE为0.61%–2.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子模型在超出训练时间区间的速度场时间预测上仍具挑战性，需要一种能在参考应力条件下稳定外推的预测方法。

Method: TI-DeepONet内置四阶Runge-Kutta积分器推进速度场，FNO学习对预测空间场的残差修正，二者以预测-校正方式联合训练；以前两个时间层参考速度场初始化并自回归推演，全程（含训练区间之外）输入参考应力场与给定激励。算例为50 m与280 m总深四层土柱中的SH波，激励为0.5–6 Hz中心频率的三阶高斯导数小波，层厚与剪切波速随剖面变化。

Result: 测试剖面外推长度达训练区间时长的两倍；预测再现主要速度到时与随深度变化的响应形态，精度随剖面而异。四个算例全评估窗口平均NRMSE为0.53%–2.31%，外推区间为0.61%–2.82%，归一化因子为各剖面全评估窗口内参考速度峰值绝对值。

Conclusion: 结果验证了所测配置下参考应力条件驱动的速度场时间预测的有效性；速度-应力波场的自主连续推演尚待建立。

Abstract: Temporal prediction of seismic velocity fields beyond the time interval represented in training remains challenging for neural operator models. This study develops a coupled neural operator framework for stress-conditioned temporal prediction in layered elastic media. A time-integrator-embedded Deep Operator Network (TI-DeepONet) advances the velocity field through an embedded fourth-order Runge-Kutta (RK4) scheme, while a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learns a residual correction to the predicted spatial field. The two components are trained jointly in a predictor-corrector configuration. Prediction is initialized with reference velocity fields at the first two time levels and proceeds autoregressively, with reference stress fields and prescribed excitation supplied throughout rollout, including beyond the training interval. Four cases consider horizontally polarized shear (SH) waves in four-layer soil columns with fixed total depths of 50 and 280 m, excited by third-order Gaussian-derivative (DOG3) wavelets with central frequencies between 0.5 and 6 Hz. Layer thicknesses and shear-wave velocities vary across profiles. Evaluations on test profiles extend beyond the training interval by up to twice its duration. The predictions reproduce principal velocity arrivals and depth-dependent response patterns, with accuracy varying across profiles. Across the four cases, mean peak-normalized root-mean-square errors (NRMSEs) range from 0.53% to 2.31% over the full evaluation windows and from 0.61% to 2.82% over the extrapolation intervals. Both metrics use each profile's peak absolute reference velocity over the full evaluation window as the normalizer. These results demonstrate temporal velocity prediction under reference-stress conditioning for the tested configurations; autonomous continuation of the coupled velocity-stress wavefield remains to be established.

</details>


### [33] [Rate-distortion Assessment for Hierarchical Content Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25151)
*Yunye Gong,Indranil Sur,Jared Claypoole,Pritish Sahu,Ajay Divakaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受率失真理论启发，将分层内容摘要形式化为信息编码问题，构建语义层级框架以量化生成式内容操纵中的压缩效率（率）与信息损失（失真）权衡，并用长上下文问答实验对不同大模型进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言与视觉生成模型在内容生成、编辑等任务上已取得成功，但对其生成内容质量的评估仍以主观为主，缺乏对模型能力的科学化理解，因此需要可量化的分析框架。

Method: 借鉴Shannon信息压缩理论中的率失真理论，设计原则性框架提取源内容的语义层级，将其作为信息编码问题来刻画生成式内容操纵过程中压缩效率（率）与信息损失（失真）之间的权衡；并在长上下文问答的内容摘要任务上用当前最先进的大语言模型开展实证实验。

Result: 实验揭示了不同大模型在给定率预算下的性能差异，提供了关于其效率与理解能力的新洞见；进一步将语义层级用作检索增强生成（RAG）的引导，相比现有基线在率失真特性上取得改进。

Conclusion: 该语义层级率失真框架为生成式内容操纵质量提供了客观、可量化的评估手段，可对模型进行条件于率预算的基准比较，并能有效指导检索增强生成以提升效率-保真度权衡。

Abstract: Powerful generative models including large language and vision models have demonstrated success across a wide range of content generation, editing and manipulation tasks via end-to-end prompt-based generation. However, current assessment of the quality of generative content manipulation remains subjective, limiting scientific understanding of model capabilities. Inspired by rate-distortion theory from Shannon information compression theory, in this work we formalize hierarchical content summarization as an information encoding problem. We design a principled framework to extract the semantic hierarchy of the source content to support quantitative characterization of the rate-distortion trade-off between compression efficiency (rate) against information loss (distortion) during generative content manipulation, which allows us to benchmark various models with respect to performance conditional on rate budget of interest. We demonstrate the use of the framework with empirical experiments on content summarization for long-context question-answering with state-of-the-art large language models, offering new insights into their efficiency and comprehension capabilities. We further demonstrate the use of the semantic hierarchy as guidance for performing effective retrieval augmented generation, showing improved rate-distortion characteristics against current baselines.

</details>


### [34] [Bayesian $κ$-Explorer for Minimax Model Estimation in MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25160)
*Xihe Gu,Urbashi Mitra,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将frequentist κ-Explorer的置信集联合优化替换为Dirichlet先验下的后验方差信号，得到计算更简便且保持Õ(T^{-1/3})速率的Bayesian κ-Explorer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知MDP中转移核的minimax估计需要主动探索。已有frequentist κ-Explorer虽具良好理论保证，但依赖置信界构造并对可行转移核与占用测度做联合优化，计算负担重，限制实用性。

Method: 对转移分布施加Dirichlet先验；采用后验均值估计器时，后验期望平方ℓ2误差等于总后验方差，从而给出闭式探索信号；算法直接在后验均值模型下规划，避免置信集优化；并进一步将最大后验方差与最优渐近minimax分配基准进行对比界定。

Result: 理论上保持与frequentist κ-Explorer相同的Õ(T^{-1/3})代理收敛率；给出最大后验方差相对于最优渐近minimax分配基准的界；在Pendulum、Mountain Car、Taxi上取得比基线更低的最坏情况转移估计误差。

Conclusion: 以贝叶斯后验方差替代置信集优化，可在不损失收敛速率的前提下显著简化minimax转移估计的主动探索算法，并具实证优势。

Abstract: We study active exploration for minimax transition estimation in unknown Markov decision processes (MDPs). Frequentist $κ$-Explorer~\citep{gu2026kappa} addresses this problem through a parameterized family of exploration objectives, but requires confidence-bound constructions and joint optimization over plausible transition kernels and occupancy measures, introducing a computational burden that limits its practical applicability. We develop Bayesian $κ$-Explorer, which simplifies this procedure by placing Dirichlet priors on the transition distributions. Under the posterior mean estimator, the posterior expected squared $\ell_2$ error equals the total posterior variance, providing a closed-form exploration signal. The algorithm plans under the posterior mean model, eliminating confidence-set optimization.
