Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently established a new paradigm in machine learning, demonstrating exceptional zero-shot generalization and complex reasoning capabilities. Concurrently, neural network parameter generation has emerged as a promising avenue for rapid task adaptation and model synthesis. Existing weight generation techniques, however, struggle to jointly learn the distribution of diverse models, to encode heterogeneous architectures within a single generator, and to generalize out of distribution. We therefore ask whether pretrained parameters can be encoded into a discrete domain so that the zero-shot generalization LLMs exhibit in text generation can be transferred to weight generation. Following this view, we propose an LLM-inspired approach, Instruction-Guided Parameter Generation (IGPG), that formulates network parameter generation as an autoregressive modeling problem. We first quantize pretrained weights with a VQ-VAE, then train a prefix-conditioned autoregressive generator over the resulting tokens. At inference, the generated weight tokens are mapped back to parameter space by the VQ-VAE decoder. We evaluate the framework across in-distribution generation, cross-dataset transfer, and cross-architecture settings. The proposed method achieves competitive performance in in-distribution generation and provides useful parameter initializations for held-out datasets and architectural configurations, improving downstream performance over random initialization after adaptation. These results suggest that discrete autoregressive modeling provides a promising framework for learning and transferring structure across collections of pretrained neural network parameters.

</details>


### [69] [Is a Description Worth a Thousand Demonstrations? Specificity and Reliability of Task Descriptions in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25560)
*Bruce Nguyen,Bei Jiang,Linglong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从贝叶斯视角将ICL中的任务描述建模为具有"特异性"和"可靠性"的先验，借用有效样本量(ESS)量化描述的预测价值，并在上下文线性回归中分析其规律，进而实验检验元训练Transformer与预训练语言模型是否按贝叶斯最优方式使用描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示中的任务描述（说明任务而非仅给示例）在ICL中普遍使用，但其作用机制缺乏理论理解；已有贝叶斯分析主要针对示例(demonstrations)，对描述(descriptions)尚无对应理论刻画。

Method: 在ICL的贝叶斯框架下，把描述视为由特异性与可靠性刻画的信息性先验；引入贝叶斯统计中的有效样本量(ESS)，将描述的预测价值折算为等价的示例数量；在上下文线性回归设定下推导ESS随特异性、任务维度及描述可能无关情形的变化规律；并以实验比较元训练Transformer与预训练语言模型在无/有推理时的表现与贝叶斯最优预测器的ESS。

Result: 理论上：可靠描述的ESS随其特异性和任务维度增大；若描述可能无关，则在缺少示例时其特异性带来的收益很小。实验上：元训练Transformer在其训练分布上接近贝叶斯最优ESS；预训练语言模型在无推理直接作答时对高特异性描述利用不足，多数模型仅在启用推理时才接近贝叶斯最优预测器。

Conclusion: 研究将ICL的贝叶斯分析从示例扩展到描述，给出以ESS度量描述价值的理论框架，并揭示模型对描述的利用是否贝叶斯最优依赖于训练方式与是否推理，说明描述与示例在ICL中存在可量化、可比较的信息作用。

Abstract: A prompt for in-context learning (ICL) can describe a task in addition to, or instead of, demonstrating it through examples. Yet how task descriptions work remains poorly understood theoretically. Through the Bayesian view of ICL, we model descriptions as informative priors characterized by their specificity and reliability. We adapt the effective sample size (ESS) from Bayesian statistics, quantifying a description's predictive value as an equivalent number of demonstrations. For in-context linear regression, we show that a reliable description's ESS grows with its specificity and with the task's dimension. A description that may be irrelevant, however, gains little from its specificity unless demonstrations are given. In practice, do in-context learners use descriptions as a Bayes-optimal predictor would? Our experiments show that meta-trained Transformers come close to the Bayes-optimal ESS when tested on their training distribution. Pretrained language models, however, make too little use of highly specific descriptions when answering without reasoning; most of them closely match the Bayes-optimal predictor only when reasoning is enabled. These results extend the Bayesian analysis of ICL from demonstrations to descriptions.

</details>


### [70] [Reinforcement-Aligned Probabilistic Weather Forecasting with Discrete Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25570)
*Jinhan Li,Zhisheng Chen,Yuxuan Li,Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Hao Wu,Jinlong Du,Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WeatherCraft，用离散天气 token 统一概率集合生成与强化学习对齐，将 GPT 式"预训练—后训练"范式引入全球中期天气预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球中期天气预报既要高效生成集合以刻画未来状态不确定性，又要服务极端事件预警、跨圈层耦合模拟等下游应用；现有方法在生成效率与任务对齐上难以兼顾。

Method: 多尺度 tokenizer 将大气状态表示为粗到细的 token 层级；Transformer 建模跨时间与跨尺度的因果依赖，并在每个尺度内并行预测 token；直接采样产生多样化集合；利用精确 token 对数概率实施 GRPO，在无需对评估器或耦合模型求导的情况下最大化下游任务奖励。

Result: ERA5 基准上，八成员集合平均使第 15 天归一化 RMSE 较最强确定性基线降低 17.6%；结合 KV 缓存较 GenCast 推理加速约 18 倍。公平 CRPS 适配提升集合概率技巧；基于奖励的后训练改善持续性陆地温度极端与海洋热极端耦合预报。

Conclusion: 确立离散自回归建模作为可扩展、可任务对齐的地球系统基础模型的通用基础。

Abstract: Global medium-range weather forecasting must not only generate ensembles efficiently to characterize uncertainty in future states, but also serve downstream applications such as extreme-event warnings and cross-sphere coupled simulations. We introduce WeatherCraft, a framework that unifies probabilistic generation and reinforcement learning alignment through discrete weather tokens, bringing the GPT-style pretraining--post-training paradigm to global medium-range weather forecasting. A multiscale tokenizer represents atmospheric states as a coarse-to-fine token hierarchy, while a Transformer models causal dependencies across time and scale and predicts tokens within each scale in parallel. Direct sampling generates diverse ensembles, and exact token log-probabilities enable Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to maximize downstream task rewards without differentiating through evaluators or coupled models. On the ERA5 benchmark, WeatherCraft's eight-member ensemble mean reduces day-15 normalized RMSE by 17.6% relative to the strongest evaluated deterministic baseline and, with KV caching, achieves an approximately $18\times$ inference speedup over GenCast. Direct fair-CRPS adaptation improves ensemble probabilistic skill, while reward-based post-training improves forecasts of persistent land temperature extremes and coupled ocean heat extremes. This work establishes discrete autoregressive modeling as a general foundation for scalable, task-alignable Earth system foundation models.

</details>


### [71] [Design and Evaluation of Epira: An Automated Interview System for Case Investigation and Contact Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25577)
*Ishita Asnani,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Zachary Ryan,Kriyoshig Prasai,Chirag Kumar,Indra Gonzalez Ojeda,Marc Lipsitch,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了 Epira——一个由协议规则控制的 LLM 访谈员系统，用于多病种的病例调查与接触者追踪；研究表明协议控制比模型选择更能提升信息采集完整性，本地与云端 LLM 表现相近，且用户可用性与信任度评价良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病例调查与接触者追踪高度依赖人力；传统网络表单在受访者需要澄清问题时难以应对，而开放式聊天机器人又可能偏离既定协议。因此需要一种既保持对话灵活性、又能严格遵循标准流程的访谈系统。

Method: Epira 将 LLM 对话与基于规则的控制器结合，强制执行必填问题、安全转交（safety handoffs）与结构化病例记录，并支持通过上传问卷适配新疾病。测试采用本地（Qwen2.5）与云端（GPT-5.4）两种后端。研究一：108 个模拟病例（麻疹与淋病，含真值字段与真值接触者），在 Epira 与开放式条件下共 864 次会话。研究二：72 名志愿者参与 2×2（疾病×模型）访谈，评估完成率、可用性、可接受性与信任度。

Result: 研究一：本地 LLM 的 Epira 采集到 88.9% 的必填字段与 60.1% 的真值接触者；相同模型在开放式条件下采集的必填字段少 31.1–41.9 个百分点。研究二：完成率 100%；改编版 SUS 均值为 82.95±16.17（0–100 分），信任度均值 3.85±0.77（1–5 分）。

Conclusion: Epira 能在多样化披露条件下还原必填字段与接触者，兼容本地与云端 LLM，并获得良好的可用性与信任评分。讨论指出信息完整性主要由协议控制而非模型选择驱动，本地与云端部署产出相似记录，支持在人工审核被转交访谈与漏报接触者的前提下采用隐私保护的本地部署。

Abstract: Objective: Case investigation and contact tracing are labor-intensive. Web forms struggle when respondents need clarification before disclosing contacts, and open-ended chatbots may deviate from protocols. We developed and evaluated Epira, a protocol-controlled large language model (LLM) interviewer for multi-disease case investigation and contact tracing.
  Materials and Methods: Epira pairs LLM dialogue with a rule-based controller enforcing required questions, safety handoffs, and a structured case record, and adapts to new diseases by questionnaire upload. Local (Qwen2.5) and cloud (GPT-5.4) backends were tested. Study 1 used 108 simulated cases with ground-truth fields and contacts for measles and gonorrhea under Epira and open-ended conditions (864 sessions). Study 2 enrolled 72 volunteers in a 2$\times$2 disease-by-model interview assessing completion, usability, acceptability, and trust.
  Results: Across Study 1, Epira with a local LLM captured 88.9% of required fields and 60.1% of ground-truth contacts. Open-ended interviewers recovered 31.1-41.9 percentage points fewer required fields than the same models in Epira. In Study 2, completion was 100%; mean adapted System Usability Scale (SUS) was 82.95 $\pm$ 16.17 on a 0-100 scale and mean trust was 3.85 $\pm$ 0.77 on a 1-5 scale.
  Discussion: Protocol control drove completeness more than model choice. Local and cloud deployments produced similar records, supporting privacy-preserving local use with human review of routed interviews and missed contacts.
  Conclusion: Epira recovers required fields and contacts under diverse disclosure conditions, works with local and cloud LLMs, and received favorable usability and trust ratings.

</details>


### [72] [From Data Alignment to Parameter Generation: Few-Shot Cross-City Traffic Prediction with Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25592)
*Shengchun Wang,Yuhang Lin,Tong Li,Pengyuan Zhou,Andy Chi Lok Tai,Wenjie Chen,Zhu Xiao,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CDPG框架，用条件扩散模型为数据稀缺城市直接生成节点级预测器参数，以多模态时空上下文为条件，实现跨城市少样本流量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多城市/区域数据稀缺（新部署或监测不足），使移动流量预测成为少样本学习问题；现有跨城市方案依赖数据空间对齐，在真实分布漂移、测量噪声和城市特有偏差下易失效。

Method: CDPG不进行原始数据对齐，而是学习源城市高性能预测器参数的条件分布并直接为目标城市生成节点特定参数。参数生成以多模态时空上下文为条件：结合卫星影像与LLM语义描述刻画空间环境线索，并通过无监督模式聚类概括复杂时间行为，从而为各基站生成契合其运行上下文的参数。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集上，CDPG在标准预测指标上较SOTA跨城市少样本基线最高提升9.72%，并在少样本多步预测中保持稳健。

Conclusion: 以参数生成替代数据对齐可更鲁棒地应对跨城市/跨区域分布偏移，多模态上下文条件有效提升少样本流量预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate mobile traffic forecasting is crucial for network service management, supporting proactive capacity provisioning, congestion mitigation, and energy-efficient operation. However, many cities and regions are data-scarce (e.g., newly deployed or poorly instrumented areas), thus making traffic prediction a few-shot learning problem and limiting the practicality of standard data-driven models. Existing cross-city solutions primarily rely on data-space alignment to match traffic distributions between data-rich and data-scarce cities, but such alignment is often brittle under real-world distribution shifts, measurement noise, and city-specific bias. To address these limitations, we propose a Conditional Diffusion-based Parameter Generation (CDPG) framework. Instead of aligning raw data, CDPG learns the conditional distribution of high-performing predictor parameters for source cities and directly generates node-specific model parameters for the target city. To support reliable adaptation for network traffic prediction, CDPG conditions parameter generation on multi-modal spatio-temporal context. Specifically, CDPG captures spatial environment cues by combining satellite imagery with large language model (LLM)-derived semantic descriptions, and summarizes complex temporal behaviors via unsupervised pattern clustering. These contextual components guide CDPG in producing parameters tailored to each base station's operational context, thereby improving robustness under cross-city and cross-region shifts. Extensive experiments on three large-scale datasets show that CDPG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-city few-shot baselines by up to 9.72% on standard forecasting metrics and remains robust for few-shot multi-step prediction. The code is available at https://github.com/LingoodBoy/CDPG.

</details>


### [73] [Bradley-Terry Training Can Distort Outcome Probabilities of Initially Accurate Language World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25594)
*Xinze Shi,Litian Zhang,Zhenjie Su,Zelong Yu,Yuting Zhang,Qihua Feng,Zifeng Kang,Chaozhuo Li,Zhengxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于Bradley-Terry目标的比较式反馈训练世界模型，不必学到真实结果概率，反而会把模型原本准确的概率质量转移到枚举分支未产生的结果上（33–43 pp），且这一偏移由目标函数本身最优解预先决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较式反馈（判官判断两个候选结果哪个更接近真实发生情况）是训练动作结果概率世界模型的实用手段，但作者质疑其能否教会真实结果概率，并系统检验该训练方式是否破坏原本准确的概率。

Method: 在模拟机器人推动任务上使用可枚举的结果分布；以Bradley-Terry目标拟合比较数据，通过训练前计算目标最优解预测模型最终落点，并对比一个知晓判官规则的似然目标作为对照；在未见布局/条件、五种候选规律、三倍更密枚举以及剔除物理不可能事件等设置下做稳健性检验，并进行读出现、响应与决策轨迹审计。

Result: 原模型在未见布局上准确（hazard误差2.4 pp），经该目标训练后有33–43 pp概率质量转移到任何枚举分支都不产生的结果上；该偏移可被目标最优解预先预测，且知晓判官规则的似然可从同样比较中学习而不出现偏移；偏移在未见条件、五种规律、更密枚举下重现，剔除物理不可能事件后仍残留约29 pp；代价具条件性：含这些事件时hazard概率被高估，沿用反馈前模型的阈值导致已训练条件约3 pp/决策、未见条件1–2 pp的效用损失（源于误拒），剔除后六种子平均损失低于1 pp，但动作选择仍有少量regret。

Conclusion: 比较式反馈的Bradley-Terry拟合问题是目标函数而非比较数据本身：它不必恢复真实结果概率并可扭曲准确概率，其失效模式可由最优解预先预测并可被规则一致的似然替代；由此造成的效用损失依赖于是否存在物理不可能事件，需通过对概率、响应与决策的审计加以诊断。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, in which a judge says which of two candidate outcomes is closer to what happened, is a practical way to train language world models that assign probabilities to an action's outcomes. We show that, fitted with a Bradley-Terry objective, it need not teach the true outcome probabilities and can distort accurate ones. On a simulated robot push with enumerated outcome distributions, this objective takes a model that is accurate on unseen layouts (hazard error 2.4 percentage points, pp) and moves 33 to 43 pp of its probability onto outcomes that none of a condition's enumerated branches produces. The cause is the objective, not the comparisons: its optimum, computed before training, predicts where the model ends up, and a likelihood that knows the judge's rule learns from the same comparisons without the shift. The shift is robust: it recurs on unseen conditions, under five candidate laws and a three-fold denser enumeration, and about 29 pp remain without the physically impossible events. Its cost is conditional: with those events it inflates hazard probabilities, and a threshold inherited from the pre-feedback model costs about 3 pp of utility per decision on trained and 1 to 2 pp on unseen conditions, through false rejects; without them the utility loss averages below 1 pp over six seeds, though a small regret in action choice remains. Audits of readout, response and decision trace the effect from probabilities to actions.

</details>


### [74] [Decision-Focused Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25597)
*Haotian Zhai,Leqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出决策聚焦强化学习（DFRL）框架，用下游决策后悔作为奖励对LLM后训练，使其预测参数直接最小化决策成本，并设计两种降低昂贵奖励评估的手段，在多个优化任务与库存规划上优于预测损失基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为预测器时，预测精度提高并不必然降低下游优化问题的决策成本，因为预测是作为优化参数使用的；需要直接以决策质量为目标进行训练。

Method: DFRL：以负的下游决策后悔为奖励，对LLM预测器做RL后训练，无需下游问题凸性或可微，可直接嵌入现有RL后训练框架。为缓解奖励评估昂贵，提出（1）启发式热启动——初期用廉价近似奖励，再切换到精确奖励精修；（2）奖励校正——将廉价估计与随机化的精确评估结合，在保持期望策略梯度不变的同时降低每次更新的精确求解次数。

Result: 在三个合成优化问题上优于预测损失基线；较大规模背包问题在短训练预算下早期提升更快；H&M交易与产品描述的库存规划中，"先推理再预测"的最佳配置相对SFT基线降低后悔31.4%，相对最强常规预测基线降低12.4%。

Conclusion: 以决策后悔为奖励的后训练可有效将LLM预测与下游决策目标对齐，且通过近似奖励与精确奖励的混合策略显著降低计算开销，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as predictors across various tasks, benefiting from their ability to use information from unstructured context. However, when predictions serve as parameters of downstream optimization problems, higher prediction accuracy does not necessarily translate into lower decision costs. We propose decision-focused reinforcement learning (DFRL), a framework for post-training LLMs to predict optimization parameters using negative downstream decision regret as reward. DFRL trains LLM predictors to minimize the costs of the decisions induced by their predictions, without requiring downstream convexity or differentiability, and integrates readily into existing RL post-training frameworks. To address expensive reward evaluation, we develop two approaches. Heuristic warm-starting uses inexpensive approximate rewards during an initial phase before switching to refinement with exact rewards. Reward correction combines inexpensive estimates with randomized exact evaluations, preserving the expected policy gradient while reducing the expected number of exact solves per update. Experiments on three synthetic optimization problems demonstrate improvements over prediction-loss baselines, while a larger knapsack experiment shows faster early improvement under short training budgets. On inventory planning with H&M transactions and product descriptions, our best configuration, in which the LLM reasons before predicting, reduces regret by 31.4% relative to the SFT baseline and 12.4% relative to the strongest evaluated conventional forecasting baseline.

</details>


### [75] [On the Informativeness of Structural Stability Bounds for GNNs: GINs versus GATs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25603)
*Alan Gany,Paul Landrier,Bogdan Cautis,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为GIN与单头GATv1推导了边扰动下的结构性稳定性界，并与一个与扰动无关的输出直径界进行比较，指出GATv1的稳定性界从不占优，需转向注意力质量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络的稳定性界用于刻画图变化时节点表示的漂移幅度，但很少与最简单的替代方案——不依赖图结构、仅约束任意两输出间距离的直径界——进行比较，导致稳定性界的实际有效性缺乏参照。

Method: 针对确定性（非微小）边扰动，分别推导GIN与单头GATv1的结构性稳定性界，并将其与扰动无关的直径界逐一对比；对GIN给出稳定性界更紧的精确条件，该条件关联扰动规模、网络深度与最大度。

Result: GIN在Cora上该条件以较大裕度成立，而在低度图MUTAG上超过几层即失效；GATv1的稳定性界在任何情形下都不比直径界更紧。

Conclusion: 稳定性界相对平凡直径界并非普遍占优，其有效性依赖于扰动规模、深度与度分布；对GATv1需发展基于注意力质量的专门分析。

Abstract: Stability bounds for graph neural networks limit how much node representations can drift when the graph changes, but they are rarely compared with the simplest alternative: a bound on the distance between any two outputs, whatever the graph. We derive structural stability bounds for Graph Isomorphism Networks (GINs) and single-head GATv1 networks under deterministic edge perturbations that need not be small, and compare each with such a perturbation-independent diameter bound. For GINs, we characterize exactly when the stability bound is tighter, through a condition linking perturbation size, depth and maximum degree; it holds with a wide margin on Cora, but fails beyond a few layers on the low-degree MUTAG graphs. For GATv1, the bound is never tighter, which calls for analyses based on attention masses.

</details>


### [76] [PSTO: A PDE-structured transport operator with shared implicit updates for multiscale CDR systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25607)
*Kaiwen Li,Dezhi Zhou,Kun Wu,Xingcai Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PDE-Structured Transport Operator (PSTO)，仅用无反应的对流-扩散数据学习输运动力学，推理时无需重训练即可泛化到完整CDR系统，参数与输运变量数解耦，100步耦合预测误差较最强基线降低≥50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度CDR系统将空间输运与局部刚性反应耦合，数值模拟代价高；常规神经代理需昂贵的耦合CDR训练数据，且参数量随输运变量数增长，难以扩展到高维CDR系统。

Method: 将PDE结构嵌入神经架构，构建PSTO：(1)仅用无反应（纯对流-扩散）数据训练；(2)单一输运算子块在所有输运变量间共享；(3)通过松弛不动点迭代在潜空间实现类隐式修正，提升递归多步预测稳定性且不增加可训练参数。评测于2D CDR、多尺度Robertson对向流火焰、多尺度氢平面射流火焰三个基准，并做不动点迭代消融、误差分解与时间步敏感性分析。

Result: 三个基准上，100步耦合预测的平均相对L2误差较最强神经基线至少降低50%；消融与误差分解表明其兼具高单步精度与长期滚动稳定性；时间步放大十倍仍保持精度，而基线精度随步长增大迅速衰减。

Conclusion: 通过将输运学习与局部反应动力学处理解耦，PSTO实现了多尺度CDR动力学的高效、模块化建模，并具备良好的参数可扩展性与时间步鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multiscale convection-diffusion-reaction (CDR) systems couple spatial transport with local stiff reaction kinetics, making their numerical simulation computationally demanding. Conventional neural surrogates require costly coupled CDR training data, and their parameter counts scale with the number of transported variables, limiting scalability to high-dimensional CDR systems. Here we propose a PDE-Structured Transport Operator (PSTO) that learns convection--diffusion dynamics solely from reaction-free data. By embedding the PDE structure into the neural architecture, PSTO generalizes to full CDR systems at inference without retraining. A single transport operator block is shared across all transported variables and refined through relaxed fixed-point iterations, performing an implicit-like correction in latent space. This improves stability during recursive multi-step prediction without adding trainable parameters. We evaluate PSTO on three benchmarks: a canonical 2D CDR system, a multiscale Robertson counterflow flame, and a multiscale hydrogen planar jet flame. In every case, PSTO reduces the mean relative $L_2$ error after 100 coupled steps by at least 50% compared to the strongest neural baseline. Ablation studies on the fixed-point iterations and detailed error decomposition further confirm that PSTO achieves both high one-step accuracy and long-term stability in coupled rollouts. Time-step sensitivity analysis shows that PSTO maintains accuracy even with a tenfold larger time step, whereas baseline accuracies decay rapidly as the time step increases. This framework enables efficient, modular modeling of multiscale CDR dynamics by separating transport learning from the treatment of local kinetics.

</details>


### [77] [Deviations from the NTK Regime in CNNs under Joint Depth-Width Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25624)
*Maria Matveev,Pascal Esser,Gitta Kutyniok,Mariia Seleznova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在CNN中，NTK色散随L/C（层数/通道数）在临界与混沌初始区域增大，有序区域较小；临界初始化下色散曲线近似按L/C塌缩；训练中核旋转随L/C增大，即使初始色散很小的有序区域亦然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTK的集中性与训练期恒定性依赖宽度、深度与初始化的相互作用。联合的深度-宽度缩放已在全连接、残差与循环网络中被研究，但在CNN中的效应理解不足，尤其缺乏对NTK色散与核旋转随深度-宽度联合缩放的实证刻画。

Method: 数值研究CNN中NTK：以L为卷积层数、C为每层通道数作为深度-宽度联合缩放变量；测量初始化时的NTK色散与训练过程中的核旋转（kernel rotation），覆盖有序、临界与混沌三种初始化区域，并考察色散对L/C的依赖与塌缩行为。

Result: 临界与混沌初始化区域中NTK色散随L/C增大，有序区域色散保持较小；在临界初始化下，色散曲线作为L/C的函数近似塌缩，暗示存在非平凡的联合缩放区域；训练中核旋转总体随L/C增大，且在初始色散较小的有序区域同样显著。

Conclusion: L/C可能在某些区域内控制NTK对无限宽度极限的偏离；NTK在初始化时的集中性与训练中的稳定性表现出不同的缩放行为，二者不能相互推断。

Abstract: The concentration and training-time constancy of the neural tangent kernel (NTK) depend on the interplay between network width, depth, and initialization. While joint depth-width scaling has been studied in fully-connected, residual, and recurrent networks, its effects in convolutional neural networks (CNNs) remain less understood. We investigate these effects numerically in CNNs, examining NTK dispersion at initialization and kernel rotation during training. Let depth $L$ be the number of convolutional layers, and width $C$ the number of channels per layer. We find that NTK dispersion increases with $L/C$ in the critical and chaotic initialization regimes, but remains small in the ordered regime. At critical initialization, dispersion curves approximately collapse as a function of $L/C$, suggesting a nontrivial joint scaling regime. During training, kernel rotation generally increases with $L/C$, even in the ordered regime where initial dispersion is small. Together, these results suggest that $L/C$ may govern NTK deviations from the infinite-width limit in certain regimes, and that NTK concentration at initialization and stability during training exhibit different scaling behaviors.

</details>


### [78] [QUBO-based feature selection for MRI undersampling pattern design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25625)
*Xiaoxin Huang,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将MRI欠采样掩膜设计建模为特征子集选择问题：以二值特征表示采样线，估计互信息与条件互信息并加采样数惩罚构成QUBO模型，用模拟退火与量子退火求解，在fastMRI膝关节数据上零填充重建性能优于四种传统方法与单变量MI排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MRI欠采样掩膜多依赖经验或固定模式（如变密度、随机等），缺乏以重建信息量为目标的系统性设计方法；作者希望用信息论特征选择视角为掩膜设计提供可优化的建模范式。

Method: 把每条采样线视为二值特征（是否被纳入候选掩膜）；利用候选掩膜及其训练集评估结果估计互信息（MI）与条件互信息（CMI），结合采样计数惩罚构建二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）模型；用模拟退火与量子退火求解得到特征子集即采样掩膜；在fastMRI膝关节幅值参考图像上以患者不重叠的训练/测试划分、傅里叶欠采样仿真与零填充重建进行评估，测试15%–40%共六个采样率。

Result: 在全部六个目标采样率（15%–40%）下，所得重建的平均均方根误差低于四种传统采样方法，平均结构相似度高于单变量互信息排序方法。

Conclusion: 结果支持信息论特征选择可作为零填充仿真设定下MRI欠采样掩膜设计的一种有效途径；结论限于所测试的仿真条件，未涉及迭代重建等更复杂场景。

Abstract: We formulate undersampling mask design for Cartesian magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) as a feature subset selection problem. Each sampling line is represented by a binary feature indicating whether it is included in each candidate mask. Using candidate masks and their training-set evaluations, we estimate mutual information (MI) and conditional mutual information (CMI). These estimates and a sampling-count penalty define a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. The model is solved using simulated annealing and quantum annealing to determine a feature subset and its corresponding sampling mask. We assess the selected subsets on the test set through zero-filled reconstruction in Fourier undersampling simulations using fastMRI knee magnitude reference images, with patient-disjoint training and test sets. At all six tested target sampling rates from 15% to 40%, the resulting reconstructions have lower mean root mean square error than those obtained with four conventional sampling methods. They also have higher mean structural similarity than those obtained using univariate mutual-information ranking at all tested rates. These results support information-theoretic feature selection as an approach to MRI undersampling mask design under the tested zero-filled simulation settings.

</details>


### [79] [NIMIS: A Structure-Agnostic, Chemically Informed Slot-Attention Framework for Demixing 1D $^1$H-NMR Mixture Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25629)
*Anna Marija Sumrova,Jonathan M. Goodman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NIMIS：一种结构无关的注意力深度学习框架，将一维1H-NMR混合物谱的峰按所属组分分组，结合J耦合信息与slot attention，在合成混合物上取得约82%的峰-组分归属准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应产物常为复杂混合物，需先分离组分才能获得可解释的1H-NMR谱；直接分析粗混合物谱可加速反应分析并拓展化学空间探索，但混合物谱峰重叠、归属困难。

Method: 提出NIMIS框架：结构无关、基于注意力；将J耦合信息直接引入多头Transformer编码器的注意力机制，获得情境化峰嵌入，再用slot attention将峰嵌入分离为各组分集合。训练集为NMRexp数据库实验谱构建的158,005个合成混合物，分子至多20个重原子，含C、H、N、O、S、F、Cl、Br、I。

Result: 在留出分子上，无噪输入峰-组分归属准确率82.28%，10%积分噪声下77.34%；正确归属的平均NIMIS得分为0.83。优于常规Transformer与简单基线，且在骨架不重叠（scaffold-disjoint）评估中性能保持。

Conclusion: 通过将峰分组为组分级集合，NIMIS为更稳健的混合物分析方法（包括处理重叠信号的方法）奠定基础。

Abstract: The products of chemical reactions are usually complex mixtures that must be separated into their components before interpretable $^1$H-NMR spectra can be obtained. Direct analysis of the spectra of crude mixtures would accelerate reaction analysis and facilitate the exploration of new chemical space. We present NIMIS, a structure-agnostic, attention-based deep learning framework that groups the peaks of a 1D $^1$H-NMR mixture spectrum according to the component from which they originate. J-coupling information is introduced directly into the attention mechanism of a multi-head Transformer encoder, and the resulting contextualised peak embeddings are separated into components by slot attention. NIMIS was trained on 158,005 synthetic mixtures constructed from experimental spectra in the NMRexp database, covering molecules of up to 20 heavy atoms composed of C, H, N, O, S, F, Cl, Br and I. On held-out molecules it reaches a peak-to-component assignment accuracy of 82.28% for noise-free input and 77.34% with 10% integral noise, with a mean NIMIS score of 0.83 for correct assignments. NIMIS outperforms a conventional Transformer and simple baselines, and its performance carries over to a scaffold-disjoint evaluation. By grouping peaks into component-level sets, NIMIS provides a foundation for more robust mixture analysis methods, including methods capable of handling overlapping signals.

</details>


### [80] [Extending Rademacher Complexity-Based Generalization Theory to Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25650)
*Xi Wang,Liang Bai,Xian Yang,Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将域空间形式化为表示空间上的Wasserstein空间，借助CD(K,∞)曲率理论推导出依赖几何的域泛化误差界，从形式上分离样本级与分布级泛化效应，并实验验证曲率对域泛化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域泛化理论通过引入带元分布的域空间来推广经典泛化分析，但把域空间视为无几何结构，忽略了其作为概率分布构成的无穷维非线性抽象空间的复杂几何性质，因而无法刻画分布级泛化与样本级泛化的本质区别，也难以解释跨域泛化的内在困难。

Method: 将域空间建模为表示空间上的Wasserstein空间，利用该空间的CD(K,∞)曲率几何性质，引入额外的曲率相关置信项，推导出新的、依赖几何的域泛化误差界。

Result: 所得误差界形式上解耦了两级泛化效应，揭示域泛化同时受表示空间的不变性与曲率影响；实验验证了曲率对域泛化性能的作用。

Conclusion: 域空间的几何结构是理解域泛化的关键，几何感知的理论框架弥补了无几何理论的根本缺陷，为跨域泛化难度提供形式化解释。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to train models on multiple source domains with distinct distributions to ensure robust performance on unseen target domains. In doing so, it elevates the concept of generalization from the traditional sample level to the distribution level. Existing theoretical frameworks introduce a domain space endowed with a meta-distribution to extend classical generalization analysis to this new setting. However, these approaches typically treat the domain space as devoid of geometry. In reality, the domain space is an inherently infinite-dimensional, nonlinear abstract space composed of probability distributions, and its complex geometric properties fundamentally determine generalization behavior. Consequently, existing geometry-free theories of domain generalization have a fundamental limitation. They lack a formal characterization of what distinguishes distribution-level generalization from sample-level generalization, leaving the inherent difficulty of generalizing across domains largely unexplained. To bridge this gap, we formalize the domain space as the Wasserstein space over the representation space. Building on the geometric properties of this space (specifically the $\mathrm{CD}(K,\infty)$ curvature theory) we derive a novel, geometry-dependent DG error bound. By introducing an additional curvature-dependent confidence term, this bound formally disentangles the two levels of generalization effects and reveals the essential nature that domain generalization is affected by both the invariance and the curvature of the representation space. Finally, we experimentally validate the effect of curvature on domain generalization.

</details>


### [81] [Explainability of genotype embedding models through observed cohort paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25681)
*Amashi Niwarthana,Aloysius Tan,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出沿嵌入空间中连接训练队列观测基因型路标的分段线性路径进行归因积分，以解决基因型-神经影像模型解释中积分梯度违反完备性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合基因型与神经影像的深度学习模型预测神经疾病更准确，但临床落地需识别预测所依据的具体遗传变异。积分梯度等路径归因方法要求模型在基线到输入的整条路径上均可取值、可求梯度；而基因型模型通过仅在离散等位基因剂量{0,1,2}处定义的学习嵌入映射为向量，直线路径会经过无嵌入、无梯度的分数基因型。归因须在嵌入空间进行，而路径选择会影响归因结果。此外常用的逐变异估计器一般不具备完备性。

Method: 证明加性可分离性可保证逐变异估计器完备，但变异间交互通常破坏该性质；改在嵌入空间中沿连接训练队列观测基因型路标的分段线性路径积分，路径整体满足完备性（尽管分段内部未必被观测）。

Result: 在两种扰动协议下忠实性均优于积分梯度与离散化积分梯度；在保支撑扰动下帕金森病分类取得最佳结果；在逐变异扰动下与支撑背景Shapley基线相当；种子稳定性更高，并在合成基准中更好地恢复植入的非加性效应。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间分段线性路标路径归因是基因型-影像模型可解释性的可行方案，能兼顾完备性、忠实性与稳定性。

Abstract: Deep learning models that combine genotypes with neuroimaging predict neurological diseases more accurately than either modality alone. Their clinical use, however, requires identifying which genetic variants a prediction is based on. Path-based attribution methods like Integrated Gradients address this by integrating the model's gradient from a baseline to the input. These methods assume the model accepts any point along that path as a valid input. Genotype models violate this assumption. They map the three discrete allele dosages \{0,1,2\} to vectors through a learned embedding defined only at those values, so a straight line passes through fractional genotypes at which no embedding, and therefore no gradient, exists. Attribution must therefore proceed in embedding space where the choice of path can affect the resulting attribution. We show that the commonly used per-variant estimator does not generally satisfy completeness. Additive separability guarantees completeness for this estimator, but interactions between variants generally break that property. Instead, we integrate along piecewise-linear embedding-space paths connecting genotype waypoints observed in the training cohort. Each path satisfies completeness in the continuum although the interiors of its segments are not necessarily observed. Our method improves faithfulness over Integrated Gradients and discretized Integrated Gradients under both perturbation protocols, achieves the strongest results for Parkinson's disease classification under support-preserving perturbations, and is competitive with a support-background Shapley baseline under per-variant perturbations. It also shows higher seed stability and better recovery of planted non-additive effects in a synthetic benchmark.

</details>


### [82] [Measurable Does Not Imply Transportable: A Prospective Audit of Model Explanations in Multi-Year Field Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25692)
*Rayhaneh Shabani Nia,Ali Karkehabadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验"前瞻性解释可迁移性"（prospective explanation transportability），利用多年Genomes-to-Fields玉米试验，发现粗粒度环境级解释能可靠迁移（中位余弦0.83，2024锁定值0.811在预设区间内），而细粒度基因型条件解释迁移性差（历史中位0.30，年份间0.006–0.822），且测量可靠性无法决定解释迁移结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型解释日益被当作科学证据使用，但当解释未能在新数据上复现时，人们常将其归因于目标测量噪声。作者试图回答一个根本性问题：可靠测量是否蕴含可复现的解释？即解释能否从历史数据迁移到真正的未来情境，这一性质此前未被明确形式化。

Method: 将"前瞻性解释可迁移性"形式化为解释在真实未来语境中被恢复的程度，并明确其区别于特征重要性与测量可靠性。使用Genomes-to-Fields多年玉米试验（每个新年份为真实未来语境而非随机重采样），评估16个农艺可解释的环境概念，采用五个前向切分与一个前瞻锁定的2024测试年，并辅以split-half可靠性、上下文内重采样及非时序验证的对比分析。

Result: 粗粒度环境级解释迁移稳健（中位余弦0.83），锁定2024值0.811落入预设80% bootstrap百分位区间。粮食产量上基因型条件解释历史中位余弦仅0.30，跨年0.006–0.822。可靠性无法决定迁移：split-half可靠性最高的年份迁移性最低。上下文内重采样显示GxE解释在固定语境可复现（0.77–0.95），但跨2020–2023迁移对齐低（0.01–0.36）。第二个表型在两种分辨率下均强迁移，非时序验证将交互级迁移高估+0.22。

Conclusion: 目标可靠性与语境内解释可复现性本身不足以确定模型解释何时能支撑可复现的科学主张；解释的细粒度、目标表型及验证设计（时序vs非时序）共同决定其前瞻可迁移性，需以真实未来语境进行评估。

Abstract: Model explanations are increasingly used as scientific evidence, yet it remains unclear what they license scientists to conclude beyond the data on which they were constructed. When a fine-grained explanation fails to replicate, the failure may be attributed to noisy measurement of its target, raising a fundamental question: does reliable measurement imply a reproducible explanation? We test this directly. We formalize prospective explanation transportability: the extent to which an explanation learned from past data is recovered in a genuinely future context. This property is distinct from feature importance and measurement reliability. Using multi-year Genomes-to-Fields maize trials, where each new year is a real future context rather than a random resample, we evaluate 16 agronomically interpretable environmental concepts across five forward splits and one prospectively locked test year. Coarse environment-level explanations transport reliably (median cosine 0.83); their locked 2024 value, 0.811, fell within a prespecified 80% bootstrap percentile interval. For grain yield, genotype-conditioned explanations have a historical median cosine of 0.30 and range from 0.006 to 0.822 across years. Reliability does not order these outcomes: the year with the highest split-half reliability had the lowest explanation transport. Within-context resampling showed that GxE explanations were reproducible within a fixed context (0.77-0.95) while exhibiting lower prospective alignment across the 2020-2023 transitions (0.01-0.36). A second phenotype transports strongly at both resolutions, and non-temporal validation inflates apparent interaction-level transport by +0.22. These results show that target reliability and within-context explanation reproducibility do not by themselves establish when a model explanation can support a reproducible scientific claim.

</details>


### [83] [PosteriorBench: Evaluating Behavior Posterior Sampling in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25735)
*Zhiyang Xun,Daniel D. Johnson,Sarah Schwettmann,Neil Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PosteriorBench 基准，用于系统评测"行为后验采样"问题：给定语言模型与目标行为，如何高效地从表现出该行为的文本分布中采样，并以计算成本与 KL 散度衡量算法优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有语言模型行为通过普通采样难以获得（需极大量样本），因此需要"从模型的行为后验分布中高效采样"的算法，但此前缺乏统一、可量化的评测手段。

Method: 构建 PosteriorBench：在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上设置 21 个任务，行为概率覆盖 10^-5 至 10^-1，每个任务均基于十亿条带标签回复语料提供精确后验样本；以计算成本及前向/反向 KL 散度作为评价指标，比较提示、微调及其他已有训练方法。

Result: 所评测方法无一同时具备高效性与保真性：提示类方法成本低但与真实后验 KL 散度居高不下；基于后验样本的微调常因成本过高而不可行；若干已有训练方法也未表现更优。

Conclusion: 能够低成本逼近真实后验的采样器仍是开放问题，PosteriorBench 旨在为这一目标提供可度量的进展基准。

Abstract: Rare language model behaviors can be difficult to study because obtaining them through ordinary sampling can require a very large number of samples. This motivates the behavior posterior sampling problem: given a language model and a behavior of interest, is there an algorithm that efficiently samples from the model's distribution over transcripts that exhibit this behavior? We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for comparing algorithms by their compute cost and by how close their samples are to the true posterior in forward and reverse KL divergence. PosteriorBench contains 21 tasks on Qwen3.5-0.8B with behavior probabilities from $10^{-5}$ to $10^{-1}$, each with exact posterior samples from a corpus of one billion labeled responses. We find that no method we evaluate is both efficient and faithful: simple methods like prompting are cheap but plateau at a high KL divergence from the posterior, fine-tuning on posterior samples is often cost-prohibitive, and several training methods from prior work do not perform better. Building a posterior sampler that can cheaply approximate the true posterior remains an open problem, and we hope PosteriorBench can measure progress towards this objective. Code and data are available at https://github.com/TransluceAI/PosteriorBench.

</details>


### [84] [Goal-Guided Reinforcement Learning via Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25742)
*Yajie Qi,Wei Wei,Lin Li,Lijun Zhang,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态目标偏好数据集CCGP-2M与VLM引导的两阶段RL框架（离线VLM微调+在线目标引导RL），在Craftax-Classic上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Craftax类环境奖励稀疏、决策周期长，探索是RL智能体的主要难点；用LLM生成高层目标引导探索时，未经任务适配的模型依赖精心设计的提示词，生成的目标常不一致或与情境不符。

Method: 构建Craftax-Classic交互轨迹的多模态目标偏好数据集CCGP-2M；框架分两阶段：离线阶段用KLTDPO（token级KL正则+分布鲁棒重加权）将VLM与目标偏好对齐；在线阶段进行目标引导的RL训练。

Result: Craftax-Classic实验中，方法比SOTA基线更早采集到钻石，取得最高分数与奖励，并在多数成就上成功率最高。

Conclusion: 通过数据集驱动的VLM任务适配，可为稀疏奖励长周期环境提供一致有效的目标引导，显著提升探索效率与整体表现。

Abstract: Exploration remains a major challenge for reinforcement learning (RL) agents in Craftax-style environments due to sparse rewards and long horizons. Large language models (LLMs) can guide exploration by generating high-level goals, but methods using models without explicit task adaptation often rely on carefully designed prompts and may produce inconsistent or contextually inappropriate goals. We introduce CCGP-2M, a multimodal goal-preference dataset constructed from interaction trajectories in Craftax-Classic, and propose a vision-language model (VLM)-guided RL framework that learns environment-specific goal guidance from this dataset. The framework consists of two stages: offline VLM fine-tuning and online goal-guided RL. In the offline stage, we align the VLM with goal preferences using Kullback-Leibler Token-level Direct Preference Optimization (KLTDPO), which combines token-level KL regularization with distributionally robust reweighting. Experiments on Craftax-Classic show that our method collects diamonds earlier than state-of-the-art baselines, achieves the highest score and reward, and attains the highest success rates on most achievements.

</details>


### [85] [Disentangling Monitor Hardware Artifacts and Smoothing Lag for Shift-Robust Intraoperative EEG and Hemodynamic Risk Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25765)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果信号调理+移位鲁棒共形校准框架，处理商用EEG/血压监护仪的阻抗脉冲、平滑延迟、DC漂移与袖带保持伪差，在5,439例麻醉过程中实现可靠的术中风险实时界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用术中EEG与血压监护仪引入循环阻抗脉冲、平滑延迟、直流漂移和袖带保持等伪差，干扰风险预测与不确定性校准，导致误报与可靠性下降。

Method: 构建因果信号调理流水线：流式滤波剔除10.13分钟、64 Hz阻抗检测段，非截断陷波后调理，分离63秒抑制比滞后，区分三通道袖带充气与脱落；采用去阻抗皮层树集成与深度序列模型对比，配以患者簇分组Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted Conformal Risk Control（CRC）及滞后缓冲在线共形PID跟踪。数据涵盖TIVA（N=3,214）、挥发性麻醉（N=2,046）、欧洲内镜镇静（N=171）、DC耦合手术（N=8）共5,439例。

Result: 预滤波剔除消除97.90%与97.60%的假平线冲突，同时保留99.23%与98.96%的深度抑制；未见TIVA手术（N=525）上皮层特征AUPRC 0.3135（患病率1.94%）与0.1627（无滞后0.82%），与深度编码器无显著差异（p≥0.36），低血压无皮层增益（0.3432 vs 0.3437，p=0.784）；零重训挥发性麻醉迁移（N=1,048）与呼气末气体先知在无滞后抑制上无显著差异（0.0820 vs 0.0736，p=0.472）；Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted CRC将全部70个TIVA移位单元界限控制在≤4.38%（α=5.0%），每30秒epoch耗时66.32 ms。

Conclusion: 因果伪差解耦与移位感知共形校准可在明确生理可观测性限度内支撑可靠的实时术中风险界限。

Abstract: Commercial intraoperative electroencephalography (EEG) and blood pressure monitors introduce cyclic impedance pulses, smoothing delays, direct-current (DC) drift, and cuff holds that confound risk forecasting and uncertainty calibration. We developed a causal signal-conditioning and shift-robust conformal calibration framework across 5,439 public anesthesia procedures. Streaming filters blank 10.13-min 64-Hz impedance checks, apply unclipped post-notch conditioning, separate 63-s suppression-ratio lag, and distinguish three-channel cuff inflations from dropouts across propofol-remifentanil total intravenous anesthesia (TIVA; N = 3,214), volatile anesthesia (N = 2,046), European endoscopy sedation (N = 171), and DC-coupled surgery (N = 8). De-impedanced cortical tree ensembles were compared against deep sequence models, paired with patient-cluster Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted Conformal Risk Control (CRC) and lag-buffered online Conformal Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) tracking. Pre-filter blanking removed 97.90% and 97.60% of false flatline conflicts while preserving 99.23% and 98.96% of deep suppression. On unseen TIVA surgeries (N = 525), cortical features achieved area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) 0.3135 (1.94% prevalence) and 0.1627 (0.82% lag-free prevalence), with no detectable difference from deep encoders (p >= 0.36) and no cortical gain for hypotension (0.3432 vs. 0.3437, p = 0.784). Zero-retraining volatile transfer (N = 1,048) showed no detectable difference from an end-tidal gas oracle on lag-free suppression (0.0820 vs. 0.0736, p = 0.472). Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted CRC bounded all 70 TIVA shift cells at <= 4.38% (alpha = 5.0%) in 66.32 ms per 30-s epoch. Causal artifact disentangling and shift-aware conformal calibration support reliable real-time intraoperative risk bounds within explicit physiological observability limits.

</details>


### [86] [RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25780)
*Yucheng Du,Ryan A. Rossi,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 路由状态能读出推理步骤的对错，且替换路由会移动验证判断，但判断的移动基本不跟随"源步骤对错"，即路由器编码与验证判断之间存在因果缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中路由状态可区分正确与错误推理步，若改变路由也改变步骤级验证判断，容易被推断"判断追随路由所编码的信息"。作者要检验这一推断是否成立，即路由干预对判断的影响是否依赖源步骤的正确性。

Method: 提出RouteVerify控制干预研究：在保持待评输入不变的前提下，用另一条解答轨迹的内部激活替换选定激活。跨9个MoE检查点比较三种干预接口（router logits、MoE输出、MoE后残差状态），并变化替换所在层与token位置；以"偏好INCORRECT胜过CORRECT"的验证margin为度量，再将来自正确步与错误步的激活分别替换，检验判断是否依赖源步骤正确性，并以norm-matched随机替换作为对照。

Result: 四项关联结果：(1) router logits探针在8/9检查点可分离正确与错误步（AUROC 0.80–0.88）；(2) 全路由层替换router logits在9/9检查点改变判断，且幅度大于norm-matched随机替换；(3) 该改变极少依赖源步正确性——单层下错误步与正确步路由给出不可区分的margin（9/9），全层下差异仅在1/9为显著为正；(4) 同一指标对局部残差替换在8/9检查点给出显著为正差异，说明度量本身能检出真实存在的依赖。

Conclusion: 正确性可从路由中读出，路由替换也确能移动验证判断，但该移动很少由源步骤的正确性决定；因此"判断追随路由编码"的推断不成立，路由与验证判断之间存在因果脱耦，需区分可解码性与因果依赖性。

Abstract: Routing states in mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can distinguish correct from erroneous reasoning steps. If changing routing also changes a model's step-level verification judgment, it is tempting to conclude that the judgment follows what routing encodes. We test this inference with RouteVerify, a controlled intervention study that replaces selected internal activations from one solution trace with activations from another, while keeping the evaluated input unchanged. Across nine MoE checkpoints, we compare three intervention interfaces: router logits, MoE outputs, and post-MoE residual states. We vary the layers and token positions at which activations are replaced. We measure changes in a verification margin that quantifies the model's preference for INCORRECT over CORRECT. We also evaluate the same input with activations from correct versus erroneous steps to test whether the resulting judgment depends on the source step's correctness. We report four linked results. First, probes on router logits separate correct from erroneous steps in eight checkpoints (AUROC 0.80-0.88). Second, replacing router logits across all routed layers changes the judgment in all nine checkpoints, and by more than a norm-matched random replacement. Third, this change rarely depends on whether the source step is correct. At one layer, routing from erroneous steps and from correct steps produces indistinguishable margins in all nine checkpoints. Under all-layer routing, their difference is resolved and positive in only one. Fourth, the same difference is positive for local residual replacements in eight of nine checkpoints, so the measure does detect such dependence where it exists. Correctness is therefore readable from routing, and routing replacements move the judgment, but the movement rarely follows correctness.

</details>


### [87] [How Reliable Are Explanations of Language Models That Detect Insider Threats?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25796)
*Suramya R. Angdembay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"临界半径"（tipping radius）指标，用于审计语言模型内部威胁检测器解释的稳健性：量化控制组度量（metric）需移动多远才能使"移除特征"与"对照"之间的差异消失，从而暴露解释结论对度量选择的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织用不透明的神经网络检测员工离职前拷贝机密文件等内部威胁，其解释指向内部神经元特征。现有审计常以"移除特征后分数变化"验证解释，但需构造一个"尽可能相似但不真正移除"的对照，而"相似"依赖度量选择，结论可能对度量的任意选取高度敏感，这一脆弱性此前未被量化。

Method: 在CERT模拟办公日志上训练七种语言模型检测器；对每条解释，比较"模型内移除该特征"与"尽可能相似但未移除的对照"两种情形下分数的差异；提出一阶预测的临界半径——度量需移动多远差异才消失；并与等幅度随机扰动、以及两种标准度量间距离作对照，另在十四次预注册主要运行与开源模型上复现。

Result: 34次运行中33次，临界半径不超过两种标准度量距离的18%；移动到该处至少消除58%的差异或使差异反转，而同等幅度随机移动几乎不改变差异。预注册的14次主要运行中13次复现，但在自训练检测器上差异常小到无法测量。另发现：某日分数部分反映"是谁"而非"做了什么"；被指名特征取决于比较对象。

Conclusion: 内部特征解释对度量选择脆弱，审计应预先固定对照度量、报告其临界半径，并披露所采用的比较对象；同时需警惕分数中的身份泄漏与比较对象的偏置效应。

Abstract: Organizations monitor their workers' activity for insider threats, in which workers misuse their legitimate access, by copying confidential files before leaving. Detectors score how unusual each day of activity is, flagging the most unusual for an analyst to review. Prominent current approaches use opaque neural networks, so explanations of a flag point to internal features: patterns of neuron activity most active on days of misuse. We audit such explanations for language-model detectors trained on CERT's simulated workplace logs. To test an explanation, we see what happens to the detector's score when we remove its features inside the model. We compare this to a control: a change that is as similar as possible to the removal, but without actually removing the features. Because defining "similar" requires choosing a metric, our central contribution is the tipping radius: a first-order prediction of how far that metric must move before the difference between removal and control disappears, a measure of how fragile the result is to that choice. In 33 of 34 runs of seven language models, the tipping radius is at most 18% of the distance between two standard choices of metric. Moving that far removes at least 58% of the difference or reverses it, while random moves of the same size leave it largely intact. The result replicates in thirteen of fourteen pre-declared primary runs on openly released models, though on our trained detectors the difference is often too small to measure. We also find that a flag's score partly reflects who the worker is, not what they did, and that the features named depend on whom they are compared against. Audits should therefore fix the control's metric in advance, report its tipping radius, and disclose that comparison.

</details>


### [88] [A Sparse Low-Rank Biclique Decomposition for Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25826)
*Antoine Vialle,Sergei Gerasimov,Aref Einizade,Antonio Ortega,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Jhony H. Giraldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出符号双团分解（SBD），将整数权重图的低秩部分表示为稀疏符号二值秩一因子之和，保持离散、可解释与稀疏性，并支持高效SpMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低秩加稀疏分解（如鲁棒PCA）假定低秩分量稠密、稀疏分量为噪声，这与图邻接矩阵的稀疏结构性不符：稠密低秩因子会破坏图的组合与计算性质。

Method: 将结构化分量建模为稀疏符号二值秩一因子之和，外加稀疏符号残差；给出固定秩与贪心SBD目标，并推导基于增益的贪心算法及理论保证。

Result: 在含噪图上可恢复块结构；真实图中压缩因子超过10，SpMM相对cuSPARSE加速最高6倍。

Conclusion: SBD是稠密低秩分解在图数据上的实用替代方案，兼顾可解释稀疏表示与计算效率。

Abstract: Low-rank plus sparse decompositions, such as robust PCA, typically model the low-rank component as dense and the sparse component as corruption. This viewpoint is poorly suited to graph adjacency matrices, where sparsity is structural and dense low-rank factors destroy the combinatorial and computational properties of the graph. We propose signed biclique decomposition (SBD), a sparse low-rank decomposition for integer-weighted graphs that represents the structured component as a sum of sparse signed binary rank-one factors, together with a sparse signed residual. The resulting representation remains discrete, interpretable, sparse, and compatible with standard linear algebra. We formulate fixed-rank and greedy SBD objectives and derive a gain-based greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Empirically, SBD recovers block structure in noisy graphs, while its factorized representation enables efficient sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpMM). On real graphs with strong shared-neighborhood structure, SBD achieves compression factors above $10$ and SpMM speedups up to $6\times$ over cuSPARSE, suggesting a practical alternative to dense low-rank decompositions for graphs. Code is available at https://github.com/IAntoineV/sbd.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Alignment: Whitening Geometry, Profile Synthesis, and Heterogeneous Ensembles for Cross-Environment WiFi Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25831)
*Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 跨环境部署是WiFi感知的核心障碍：LODO下仅K-shot标签时top-1从0.89+跌至0.408；作者通过消融证明"域对齐"类方法无效甚至有害，转而提出Dec-H（类内白化+profile混合增强+交替白化重拟合的少样本适配+异质决策集成），K10达0.560（+15.2pp），K40达0.812。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决WiFi感知在未见环境中的泛化崩塌问题，并检验"性能下降源于域未对齐"这一主流假设是否成立。

Method: 先做上限分析（信号强度、环境可解码性、失效定位在编码器外推）与预注册的反证链条，系统否证训练期对齐、readout层原型对齐、对抗混淆、CORAL、子空间对齐等做法；再提出Dec-H，含四个非对齐手段：闭式源域类内白化（等价于重几何化readout的L2正则）、profile-mixing增强、交替重拟合白化的少样本适配、异质决策集成。

Result: 对齐类方法普遍变差（CORAL -3.8pp，子空间对齐 -8.3pp），且源域统计量与跨环境贡献反相关；Dec-H各组件增益为+8.6pp（白化，零成本）、+1.0pp（增强）、+1.5pp（少样本适配，固定预条件器20/20为负）、+1.7pp（异质集成）；整体K10=0.560、K40=0.812，较基线+15.2pp，四个环境与多随机种子一致。

Conclusion: 跨环境WiFi感知的瓶颈不是表征对齐而是编码器外推；去除对齐偏置、改用闭式白化、增强、自适应少样本适配与异质集成可稳定获得显著增益；全部十三个配置经预注册与冻结判据验证，结论可信度高。

Abstract: Cross-environment deployment is the central obstacle to practical WiFi sensing: a pipeline reaching 0.89+ top-1 in its training environments collapses to 0.408 under Leave-One-Domain-Out (LODO) evaluation with only K-shot labels. A ceiling analysis shows the collapse is not an alignment problem: the action signal is strong across seen environments (about 0.89 K10), environment identity is decodable from the encoder (0.965), and the failure concentrates in the encoder's extrapolation to the novel environment. A pre-registered falsification chain confirms that "align the domains" methods--training-time alignment, readout-level prototype alignment, adversarial confusion, and covariance/subspace alignment (CORAL -3.8pp, subspace -8.3pp)--degrade performance, and that source-domain statistics anti-correlate with cross-environment contribution. The effective levers lie elsewhere; we combine them into Dec-H: (i) closed-form source within-class whitening, provably re-geometrizing the readout's L2 regularization (+8.6pp, zero cost); (ii) profile-mixing augmentation (+1.0pp); (iii) few-shot adaptation with alternating whitening refit (+1.5pp; fixed preconditioners are 20/20 negative); (iv) heterogeneous decision ensembles (+1.7pp); homogeneous scaling and source-weighted stacking are saturated or harmful. Dec-H reaches 0.560 K10 (0.812 K40), +15.2pp over the baseline, consistent across all four environments and seeds; all thirteen configurations were pre-registered with frozen criteria.

</details>


### [90] [Interpretable Prototype-Conditioned Adaptation for Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25848)
*Zhixuan Li,Hao Jiang,Chen Hui,Kai Geng,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ProtoCast，用分组原型库为数据驱动天气预报提供显式、可解释的局部模式参考，在 GlobalForecast 与 TyphoonTracking 上提升精度并可通过删除原型验证局部特征贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动天气预报中，神经预测器学到的模式是隐式的，难以解释；若不能区分局地状态的时间演变，会对演变方式不同的地点给出相似预报，增大误差。因此需要显式的模式参考来区分并解释局地模式。已有原型方法仅做样本级或任务级条件化，难以适配不同地点与变量组。

Method: 1) 采用分组特定的原型库，结合各组历史特征、时间变化量与跨变量注意力提供的预报上下文，进行演变感知的原型匹配；2) 用稀疏混合的原型特定低秩算子为每组与各位置定制修正，再经门控注意力块在各组间交换修正后的特征后预测，且每组修正可分解为显式原型贡献；3) 从零联合训练预测网络与算子，并在每个训练轮次前重拟合各组原型，通过槽位跟踪保持原型—算子关联。

Result: 在 GlobalForecast 与 TyphoonTracking 上预报精度提升；原型删除实验可将局部特征贡献与预测误差变化联系起来。

Conclusion: ProtoCast 在保持可解释性的同时提升数据驱动天气预报精度，为更准确、可解释的天气预报模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Data-driven weather forecasting requires distinguishing local states and their temporal evolution. However, learned patterns remain implicit in neural forecasters, making their influence difficult to interpret. Failing to distinguish local evolution can lead to similar forecasts for locations that develop differently, increasing prediction error. This motivates explicit references for distinguishing and interpreting local patterns. Prototypes provide explicit pattern references, but sample- or task-level conditioning leaves open how to adapt to distinct locations and variable groups. To deal with this problem, we design ProtoCast, which uses group-specific prototype banks to adapt local features while accounting for cross-variable dependencies. Specifically, (1) we combine each group's historical features and temporal change with forecast context from cross-variable attention for evolution-aware prototype matching. (2) Sparse mixtures of prototype-specific low-rank operators tailor corrections to each group and location. A gated attention block then exchanges the corrected features across groups before prediction. Each group's correction decomposes into explicit prototype contributions before this fusion. (3) We jointly train the forecasting network and operators from scratch and refit each group's prototypes before every training epoch. Slot tracking preserves prototype-operator associations, making each prototype's contribution to local corrections more interpretable. Experiments on GlobalForecast and TyphoonTracking show improved forecasting accuracy, while prototype deletion links local feature contributions to changes in prediction error. This work advances the development of more accurate and interpretable data-driven weather forecasting models. Project page: https://zhixuanli.github.io/projects/li2026protocast

</details>


### [91] [X Hedging: towards an eXplainable artificial intelligence option Hedging framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25870)
*Bjørn André Aaslund,Johannes Berge,Ayoub Haida,Ying Ni,Rita Pimentel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X Hedging，用LightGBM树集成替代神经网络作为Deep Hedging的计算高效、可解释替代方案，在保持对冲性能的同时提升效率与数据受限下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep Hedging基于神经网络学习灵活对冲策略，但模型不透明、可解释性差，且通常需要大规模训练数据，在数据受限场景下效果受限，训练与解释成本高。

Method: 以树集成方法（LightGBM）替代神经网络来估计对冲策略；为将其嵌入强化学习式对冲框架，提出一种顺序训练树集成模型的优化方法，并通过随机训练顺序引入随机性。

Result: 在受控实验中，X Hedging的对冲性能与Deep Hedging相当，同时具备更强的以用户为中心的可解释性、更高的计算效率，并在训练数据有限时稳健性显著更高。

Conclusion: 数值结果表明，树集成方法可为数据驱动的动态对冲提供有效的计算替代方案，是神经网络之外可行的选择。

Abstract: We propose X Hedging, an alternative to the Deep Hedging framework that addresses computational and explainability limitations of neural-network-based hedging strategies. Deep Hedging leverages neural networks to learn flexible hedging policies but relies on opaque models and typically requires large training datasets, which can complicate interpretation and limit its effectiveness in data-constrained settings. X Hedging takes a step toward addressing these limitations by replacing neural networks with tree ensemble methods, specifically LightGBM, to estimate hedging strategies. This design provides a computationally efficient alternative while preserving strong hedging performance and moving toward more user-centered explainability. To integrate LightGBM into a reinforcement-learning-style hedging framework, we introduce an optimization approach that sequentially trains tree-ensemble models while introducing stochasticity through random training orders. In controlled experiments, X Hedging achieves performance comparable to Deep Hedging while offering greater user-centered explainability, improved computational efficiency, and substantially higher robustness when training data are limited. The numerical results demonstrate that tree-ensemble methods provide an effective computational alternative to neural networks for data-driven dynamic hedging.

</details>


### [92] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Self-supervised Vision Backbones Survives Distribution Shift in Radar Micro-Doppler HAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25874)
*Keryan Chelouche,Petr Dobias,Nistor Grozavu,Olivier Romain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统评估微多普勒雷达人体活动识别（HAR）的部署鲁棒性，比较全微调、冻结探测与PEFT三种迁移策略，并提出DINOv3+PiSSA方案，在各类分布偏移下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微多普勒雷达支持非接触、隐私保护的HAR，但现有工作在部署鲁棒性（跨受试者、跨年龄、跨朝向、跨数据集）方面研究不足；同时缺乏对迁移策略选择（全微调、冻结特征、参数高效微调）的系统比较。

Method: 采用同分布跨受试者交叉验证与少样本跨年龄、跨朝向、跨数据集迁移的统一协议，比较全微调、冻结探测和PEFT；将自监督图像基础模型DINOv3与全层主奇异方向适配（PiSSA）结合，首次用于雷达微多普勒HAR，并进行消融分析（预训练贡献 vs. 适配器设计）。

Result: 全微调出现跨数据集负迁移，冻结特征在跨朝向下适配不足，仅PEFT同时规避两类失效；DINOv3-PiSSA在所有PEFT方法中各设置排名第一；其Small变体以仅3%可训练参数、总计4.5倍更少参数匹配此前Base规模PEFT SOTA的同分布精度；相较同规模基线在所有设置均更优，迁移增益随偏移严重程度递增，分别为2.1、6.6、8.9个百分点。

Conclusion: PEFT是雷达微多普勒HAR部署中最稳健的迁移范式；DINOv3-PiSSA在参数效率与跨域鲁棒性上均领先，增益主要来自自监督预训练与适配器配方，复合偏移下预训练贡献最大。代码与协议已开源。

Abstract: Micro-Doppler radar enables contactless, privacy-preserving human activity recognition (HAR), but deployment robustness remains underexplored. We compared full fine-tuning, frozen probing, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) using subject-disjoint in-distribution cross-validation and few-shot cross-age, cross-orientation, and cross-dataset transfer. Full fine-tuning suffered negative cross-dataset transfer, while frozen features under-adapted on cross-orientation. Only PEFT avoided both failures. We paired DINOv3 with all-layer principal-singular-direction adaptation (PiSSA), to our knowledge the first application of a self-supervised image foundation backbone to radar micro-Doppler HAR. DINOv3-PiSSA ranked first among PEFT methods in every evaluated setting. Under our protocol, its Small variant matched the in-distribution accuracy of the prior Base-size PEFT state of the art using 4.5x fewer total parameters, with only 3% trainable. It surpassed a size-matched variant of that baseline in every evaluated setting, with transfer gains increasing with shift severity: 2.1, 6.6, and 8.9 percentage points, respectively. Ablations attributed improvements to both pretraining and adapter recipe, with the pretraining contributing most under compound shift. Code and protocol are available at https://github.com/KeryanChelouche/udoppler-ssl.

</details>


### [93] [LIFT: Latent Flow Matching for Probabilistic Cell Fate Dynamics with Single Cell Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25917)
*Bowen Yang,Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIFT：基于谱系信息耦合的潜变量流框架，实现从初始细胞状态出发的概率性细胞命运预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱系追踪只能通过克隆祖先连通细胞状态，一般无法识别唯一的"源—后代"配对，因此仅学习终末群体分布不足，需要建模条件结果（一对多关系）。

Method: 构建潜变量流（latent flow）框架：变分推断在潜空间编码多种可能结果；条件先验支持仅由初始状态采样；Mean Flow 解码器直接预测未来细胞状态，每次采样仅需一次解码器评估，便于大规模蒙特卡洛采样；并给出条件预测误差分析。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，LIFT 既能恢复群体分布，也能恢复细胞命运概率。

Conclusion: 将一对多关系表示、高效采样与条件预测误差分析相结合，使 LIFT 成为从谱系信息耦合中进行概率性细胞命运预测的有效工具。

Abstract: Lineage tracing links cell states through clonal ancestry, but does not generally identify unique source-descendant pairings. Learning from such data therefore requires a model of conditional outcomes, rather than only the terminal population distribution. We introduce LIFT, a latent flow framework for probabilistic cell-state generation from lineage-informed couplings. Variational inference encodes alternative outcomes in a latent space, while a conditional prior enables sampling from the initial state alone. A Mean Flow decoder predicts future cell state directly, requiring one decoder evaluation per sampling, which makes it convenient to predict cell fate through large scale Monte-Carlo sampling. This construction combines a representation of one-to-many relationships with efficient sampling and an analysis of conditional prediction error. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate LIFT's ability to recover both population distribution and cell fate probabilities.

</details>


### [94] [A Lightweight Hybrid Approach for Milling Force Reconstruction from Internal Machine Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25921)
*Eric Hirsch,Christian Friedrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外加传感器的混合方法：用机床内部信号和加工元数据，先由监督学习预测平均铣削力，再用其参数化静态铣削力模型以重构一个主轴旋转周期内的力行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于铣削力的数据驱动监测（过程优化、状态监测）受制于昂贵且侵入性的硬件与大量训练数据需求，难以在现有的老旧机床（brownfield）上落地；且常用机床内部信号采样率低，无法直接表征高频铣削力变化。

Method: 不直接学习完整力信号（以免增加模型复杂度、数据需求与过拟合风险），而是仅从选取的机床信号特征与加工参数出发，用监督机器学习（岭回归）预测平均铣削力；该低频估计量用于参数化静态铣削力模型，从而重构一个主轴旋转周期内的力行为。测力仪数据仅用于开发与验证。

Result: 在一台铣床上跨不同工艺参数、啮合条件、刀具及两种工件材料评估：平均力预测测试集 R² 为 0.984（F̄x）与 0.991（F̄y）；以实测力特征重构为基准，单转内重构力行为 R² 为 0.962（Fx）与 0.982（Fy）。

Conclusion: 静态模型虽不能复现全部动态效应，但能捕捉力分布、载荷水平与最大刀具啮合的角向区间；该方法为现有机床上的过程监测、刀具状态评估与工艺优化提供了低成本、计算高效的基础。

Abstract: Data-driven monitoring based on milling force can enable advanced manufacturing services including process optimization and condition monitoring. However, industrial implementation is often hindered by costly and intrusive hardware and large training data requirements. This paper therefore presents a hybrid approach using machine-internal signals and machining metadata to enable force-based monitoring on brownfield machines without additional sensors. Commonly available machine signals have relatively low sampling rates and cannot directly represent high-frequency milling-force variations, especially at higher spindle speeds. Instead of learning the complete force signal, which would increase model complexity, data requirements, and overfitting risk, a supervised machine-learning model predicts only the average milling force from selected machine-signal features and machining parameters. This low-frequency estimation parameterizes a static milling force model that reconstructs the force behavior over one spindle revolution. Although the static model does not reproduce all dynamic effects, it captures the force distribution, load levels, and angular regions of highest tool engagement. The approach is evaluated on one milling machine across varying process parameters, engagement conditions, cutting tools, and two workpiece materials. Dynamometer measurements are required only for development and validation. Ridge regression achieves an $R^2$ of $0.984$ and $0.991$ for average-force prediction on the test data ($\bar{F}_x$ and $\bar{F}_y$). The reconstructed force behavior over one spindle revolution achieves an $R^2$ of $0.962$ and $0.982$ ($F_x$ and $F_y$) compared with the reconstruction based on measured force features. The method provides a cost and computationally efficient basis for process monitoring, tool condition assessment, and process optimization on existing machines.

</details>


### [95] [DTS: Delta-Based Tile Scoring for Efficient Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25938)
*Emre Uğur,Jiawen Qi,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DTS 是一种免训练、内容自适应的分块打分方法，用“delta”锚点选择降低块稀疏注意力的打分开销，在大幅加速长上下文 prefill 的同时保持接近全注意力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文场景下注意力 prefill 成为主要瓶颈；块稀疏注意力虽能跳过不重要块，但准确打分本身开销高昂，需要一种既便宜又准确的块打分机制。

Method: 提出 Delta-Based Tile Scoring (DTS)：依据局部向量变化选取 query/key 锚点，在冗余区域用更少代表、在异质区域保留更多细节；并用 parallel delta packing 将序列切分为 tile 对齐的块并行做有界局部扫描以加速锚点选择；选中的 tile 仍用原始 Q/K/V 计算。

Result: LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct 上 RULER（至 128K）与 LongBench 平均分与全注意力差距在 0.10 个百分点内；128K 时打分流水线延迟较 XAttention 降低 38%；4×H100 上 512K token 相对 FlashAttention-2 全注意力 PyTorch 基线取得 3.3× prefill 加速。

Conclusion: 内容自适应的 delta 锚点打分可在免训练条件下兼顾精度与效率，为长上下文块稀疏注意力提供实用的打分加速方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly need to process long documents and codebases, making long-context attention a major prefill bottleneck. Block sparse attention reduces computation by skipping unimportant blocks of the attention matrix, but scoring blocks accurately can itself be expensive. We propose Delta-Based Tile Scoring (DTS), a training-free, content-adaptive tile scoring method for sparse block attention. DTS selects query and key anchors according to local vector changes, using fewer representatives in redundant regions while retaining more detail in heterogeneous regions. To accelerate anchor selection, parallel delta packing partitions the query and key sequences into tile-aligned chunks and performs bounded local scans over these chunks concurrently. The selected tiles are then computed using the original query, key, and value vectors. On LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, DTS keeps average RULER and LongBench scores within 0.10 percentage points of full attention, with RULER evaluated up to 128K tokens. At 128K tokens, DTS reduces measured scoring-pipeline latency by 38% compared with XAttention. On four NVIDIA H100 GPUs, it achieves a 3.3$\times$ prefill speedup over a PyTorch full-attention baseline using FlashAttention-2 at 512K tokens.

</details>


### [96] [Gradient Boosting with Kolmogorov-Arnold Units for Interpretable Regression beyond Pairwise Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25942)
*Minjong Cheon,Hanseon Joo,Eunji Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MJBoost：弱学习器为单个Kolmogorov-Arnold单元g(Σφ_p(x_p))的梯度提升方法，用惩罚样条与交替惩罚最小二乘闭式拟合，可精确分解为ANOVA主效应/二阶效应/高阶余项，在高阶交互合成目标与OpenML-CTR23/CC18上兼具精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释表格回归模型（如EBM）被限制在主效应加两两交互，当目标依赖三个及以上变量联合作用时精度下降，只能退回到黑箱模型加事后解释。作者希望在保留逐部分可绘图的可解释性前提下，不牺牲高阶交互建模能力。

Method: MJBoost以单个Kolmogorov-Arnold单元g(Σ_p φ_p(x_p))为弱学习器，内、外函数均为惩罚样条；每个单元从残差的主Hessian方向初始化，通过交替惩罚最小二乘以闭式解拟合；类别与缺失输入直接进入单元。因每个单元对每个输入仅通过一个内函数作用，模型具有精确的泛函ANOVA分解，主效应、二阶效应与高阶余项可用一维卷积计算。分类时引入Newton步。

Result: 在三至六阶交互的合成目标上，测试误差最低比EBM低13倍，分解对各阶真实方差占比的恢复误差在0.04以内；OpenML-CTR23的35个回归任务中，21个任务测试RMSE优于EBM、仅2个更差，几何平均RMSE降低29.1%，主效应与二阶效应中位数解释拟合模型方差的0.992；OpenML-CC18的32个二分类任务上测试log-loss与EBM相当，11个多分类任务中平均log-loss在8个任务上优于EBM、7个任务上优于调参LightGBM。

Conclusion: MJBoost在数据允许时以主效应/二阶效应图呈现模型，在数据不允许时用方差分解量化高阶部分，实现了高阶交互建模与可解释性（可诊断的高阶余项）的统一。

Abstract: Interpretable models for tabular regression, such as the explainable boosting machine (EBM), restrict a prediction to main effects and pairwise interactions so that every part of it can be plotted. When the target depends on three or more inputs jointly, this restriction costs accuracy, and a black-box model with post hoc explanations becomes the usual fallback. Here we present MJBoost, a gradient boosting method whose weak learner is a single Kolmogorov-Arnold unit $g(\sum_p φ_p(x_p))$ with penalized spline inner and outer functions. Each unit is fitted in closed form by alternating penalized least squares, starting from principal Hessian directions of the residual, and categorical and missing inputs enter the units directly. Because a unit depends on each input only through one inner function, the fitted model has an exact functional ANOVA decomposition into main effects, pair effects and a higher-order remainder, which one-dimensional convolutions compute. On synthetic targets of interaction order three to six, the test error of MJBoost is up to 13 times lower than that of EBM, and its decomposition recovers the true variance share of every order to within 0.04. On the 35 regression tasks of the OpenML-CTR23 benchmark, it has a lower test RMSE than EBM on 21 tasks and a higher one on 2 (geometric-mean RMSE -29.1%). On these tasks the main and pair effects of the fitted model explain a median 0.992 of its variance, so the model reads as plots where the data allow, and the decomposition measures the higher-order part where they do not. With Newton steps the same algorithm classifies: on 32 binary tasks of OpenML-CC18 its test log-loss is on par with that of EBM, and on 11 multiclass tasks its mean log-loss is lower than that of EBM on 8 and than that of tuned LightGBM on 7.

</details>


### [97] [Power Setpoint Tracking for Large-Scale EV Charging: Transformer-Constrained Charging Policy Learning with Self-Tuning Constraint Prices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25965)
*Daniyal Sajedi-Hosseini,Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Shubham Singh,Lei Zhou,Katsuki Fujisawa,Sebastien Gros,Mazaher Karimi,Xun Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TC-CPL：将充电功率设定点跟踪建模为带过载与用户满意度约束的CMDP，用PI-Lagrange自适应与Transformer感知编码器学习策略；在600端口城区把日过载从3.59降至0.0017 kWh、跟踪误差降27%、满意度升至0.989。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营商需按已购充电曲线进行功率设定点跟踪，同时不能使共享变压器过载、也不能让车辆充电不足；现有调度器（如轮询）在两侧难以兼顾。

Method: 将PST表述为最小化跟踪误差、受变压器过载与平均用户满意度约束的约束马尔可夫决策过程；TC-CPL在soft actor-critic基础上扩展：以实测违约量驱动比例-积分式Lagrange乘子更新，在现有调度器上加残差并以其实例示范热启动，采用所有端口共享的变压器感知编码器；观测中不含离站时间与荷电状态。

Result: 五种子、100对配对日仿真。600端口、122变电站城区：日过载由3.59降至0.0017 kWh（过载种子日从77%降至0.4%），绝对跟踪误差降27%，输送能量增16%，满意度由0.959升至0.989。42 kW变压器带家庭负荷：过载由1.86降至0.0014 kWh/日，满意度由0.974升至0.985，但绝对跟踪误差高22%。与手工固定乘子相比，城区统计上无差异；单变压器上以相近过载换来满意度+0.011，但短扫描找到更优固定乘子对；过载乘子始终低于上限。

Conclusion: 约束强化学习可在大规模配电网中几乎消除过载并改善跟踪与满意度；自适应乘子相对固定乘子在城区等效，在单变压器上略优但不及细致调参的固定解，说明自适应机制稳健而非占优。

Abstract: In power setpoint tracking (PST), an operator follows a purchased charging profile without overloading shared transformers or undercharging vehicles. PST is cast as a constrained Markov decision process that minimizes tracking error subject to overload and mean user-satisfaction constraints. Transformer-Constrained Charging Policy Learning (TC-CPL) extends soft actor-critic with proportional-integral Lagrange-multiplier updates from measured violations, a residual on the incumbent dispatcher that is warm-started from its demonstrations, and a transformer-aware encoder shared by all ports. It observes no departure times or states of charge. Simulations (five seeds, 100 paired days) compare it with the incumbent, Round-Robin. On a 600-port, 122-substation district, raw overload falls from 3.59 to 0.0017 kWh/day (overloaded seed-days from 77% to 0.4%), and absolute tracking error falls by 27%. Delivered energy rises by 16%, and satisfaction rises from 0.959 to 0.989. On a 42-kW transformer with household load, overload falls from 1.86 to 0.0014 kWh/day and satisfaction rises from 0.974 to 0.985, but absolute tracking error is 22% higher. Against hand-set fixed multipliers, self-tuning is statistically indistinguishable on the district. On the single transformer, it raises satisfaction by 0.011 at similar overload, but a short sweep found a better-serving fixed pair. The overload multipliers stay below their cap.

</details>


### [98] [Seeing Beyond the Obvious: Complementary Semantic Guidance for Salient Single-Positive Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25986)
*Tong Jin,Meng-Long Wang,Xingyu Zhao,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoST框架，用覆盖率原型补充显著原型，通过排序选择性细化软标签，缓解SalSPML中正监督不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SalSPML方法由已观测正样本构建类级"显著原型"来指导未观测类识别，但显著标注偏向视觉突出实例，原型难以覆盖隐正样本的视觉多样性，导致正监督不足。

Method: 从未知类中借助任务学习的类别信息与图文语义对应关系筛选语义相关候选，聚合成类级"覆盖率原型"以补充显著原型的语义表征；再引入基于排序的选择性细化策略，对获得更强语义支持的类别增强其软标签，其余类别保留基础软标签。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上实验验证了CoST的有效性，相较现有基线取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 以覆盖率原型补充显著原型并结合排序式软标签选择性细化，可有效弥补SalSPML的正监督不足，提升未观测类的识别性能。

Abstract: Salient Single-Positive Multi-Label Learning (SalSPML) assigns each image its most salient positive label, leaving other classes unlabeled. Existing methods construct class-level Salient Prototypes from observed positives to guide the recognition of unobserved classes. However, since salient annotations favor visually prominent instances, these prototypes may not fully capture the visual diversity of hidden positives, resulting in insufficient positive supervision. To address this limitation, we propose CoST, a Coverage-guided Complementary Soft-Target Refinement framework that supplements Salient Prototypes with complementary class-semantic information. Specifically, CoST identifies semantically relevant candidates from unknown classes by leveraging task-learned category information and image-text semantic correspondence. The selected candidates are then aggregated into class-wise Coverage Prototypes to complement the semantic representations provided by Salient Prototypes. Furthermore, we introduce a ranking-based selective refinement strategy that identifies classes receiving stronger semantic support from Coverage Prototypes and selectively enhances their soft targets while preserving the base soft targets for the rest. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of CoST, with strong performance gains over existing baselines.

</details>


### [99] [Policy Improvement-Guided Replay for Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25989)
*Qin-Wen Luo,Haoxuan Gao,Sheng Ren,Xiang Chen,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从强化学习"策略改进"视角重新审视视觉-语言模型的持续强化学习（CRL），提出对比似然回放（CoLiR）方法，通过对比最终策略对正确回答的似然增益与初始策略对错误回答的似然降低来打分并选取历史提示进行回放，在MRCL基准上取得最高的平均最终准确率与平均任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习以"稳定性"为导向，强调知识保持与干扰抑制；本文补充"策略改进"视角，旨在保留已获得的性能增益，并在有限回放内存下高效选择历史数据进行回放。

Method: 利用性能差异引理，将历史性能变化刻画为当前策略在历史任务分布上的期望优势，从而论证用当前策略回放历史提示的必要性。提出CoLiR：以"最终任务策略在正确回答上的似然增益"对比"初始策略在错误回答上的似然降低"作为评分，从有限训练轨迹与缓存的对数似然中估计分数，在任务边界处选择提示，后续训练中均匀回放，且KL正则仅作用于被回放的提示；内存构建只需少量跨策略打分，无需额外生成回答。

Result: 在MRCL基准上，针对三种规模的Qwen3-VL模型，CoLiR在对比方法中取得最高的平均最终准确率与平均任务准确率，同时保持有竞争力的新任务性能。

Conclusion: 理论分析将正确回答似然增益与理想KL正则最优更新下的参考策略优势相联系，并为一般策略更新提供内生奖励解释；实验表明基于策略改进视角的对比似然回放能有效兼顾历史性能保留与新任务学习。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) requires models to acquire new capabilities while retaining performance on previous tasks. A stability-oriented perspective common in conventional continual learning emphasizes knowledge preservation and interference mitigation. Complementing this perspective, we revisit CRL for vision-language models through policy improvement, a fundamental principle of RL, to preserve acquired performance gains. Using the performance difference lemma, we characterize historical performance changes through the current policy's expected advantages on historical task distributions, motivating replay of historical prompts with the current policy. To allocate limited replay memory, we propose Contrastive Likelihood Replay (CoLiR), which contrasts likelihood gains on correct responses from the final task policy with likelihood reductions on incorrect responses from the initial policy. Our analysis links correct-response likelihood gains to reference-policy advantages under an idealized KL-regularized optimal update and provides an endogenous-reward interpretation for general policy updates. CoLiR estimates scores from finite training rollouts and cached log-likelihoods, selects prompts at task boundaries, and replays them uniformly during subsequent training, with KL regularization restricted to replayed prompts. Memory construction only requires a small amount of additional cross-policy scoring but no additional response generation. Across three Qwen3-VL model sizes on MRCL, CoLiR achieves the highest mean final accuracy and mean task accuracy among the compared methods while maintaining competitive new-task performance.

</details>


### [100] [Reinforcing Few-Step Diffusion Models via Shadow Posterior Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25998)
*Jingqiao Xiu,Yicong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Shadow Posterior Matching (SPM)，通过对生成样本再噪化获得“影子后验”来为少步扩散生成器分配奖励信用，兼顾教师锚定与训练/部署输入对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散模型部署广泛但效率导向，需用黑盒奖励强化；相对多步模型存在两大结构性问题：(1) 少步生成器是采样器而非打分模型，其速度不是生成分布的前向速度，难以正确归因奖励信用；(2) 需锚定与之无共享转移的未蒸馏教师。已有少步 RL 方法仅用对教师的边际 KL 处理第二点，却在奖励信用上为第一点付出代价。

Method: SPM 将生成样本再噪化为影子输入，并从其影子后验中获取信用；由 KL 正则化奖励目标推导出一个回归损失，将奖励值转化为奖励方向，同时把生成器输出锚定到教师，并使影子输入与部署输入对齐。

Result: 在 4 步生成器上，SPM 相比已有少步 RL 方法获得更高奖励且奖励作弊更少。

Conclusion: 影子后验匹配为少步扩散的强化微调提供了一种同时解决奖励归因与教师锚定的统一范式，在效率与对齐质量上表现更优。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion models are widely deployed for their efficiency. However, reinforcing them with black-box rewards, as is routine for multi-step models, faces two structural challenges. First, a few-step generator is a sampler rather than a score model, so its velocity is not the forward velocity of the generated distribution. Second, it must be anchored to the undistilled teacher, which shares no transitions with it. Existing few-step RL methods handle the latter with a marginal KL to the teacher, but each pays for the former in its reward credit. We propose Shadow Posterior Matching (SPM), which renoises generated samples into shadow inputs and obtains the credit from their shadow posterior. From the KL-regularized reward objective, we derive a regression loss that turns reward values into a reward direction while anchoring the generator's outputs to the teacher and aligning the shadow inputs with the deployed inputs. Applied to 4-step generators, SPM achieves higher rewards with less reward hacking than prior few-step RL methods.

</details>


### [101] [Does the Workspace Do the Work? A Causal Protocol for Verifying Global-Workspace Computation in a Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26018)
*Diaoxuan He,Linqian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种因果协议，通过"移植消息"测试判定模块通信中究竟由谁完成计算，在多种workspace网络上区分出忽略、中继与重组三种情形，并在AFGW语言模型中97%情形下验证为重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论（GWT）认为信息被广播到专门过程即成为意识内容，但仅凭任务准确率与消融实验无法区分：接收模块是把广播消息与自身知识结合计算，还是仅仅转发别处算好的答案。二者行为难以区分，故需因果层面的判定方法。

Method: 设计因果测试协议：从另一输入移植一条消息，使"忽略""中继""重组"三种假设分别预测不同答案。先在按中继或重组构造的网络（应为对照/验证集）上检验判定正确性，再用于训练得到的workspace模型（含仅训练数据不同的模型对）及AFGW——一个模块仅通过学得workspace通信的语言模型，并推广至其他通信设计与预训练Transformer的已知接口。

Result: 在构造网络上每一种情形判定均正确；训练模型中出现全部三种结果：一个模型几乎未使用广播消息，两个仅训练数据不同的模型分别表现为中继与重组。AFGW中移植消息有97%情形给出重组预测的答案，且其内容通过重定向接收方从自身记忆中检索的内容起作用。

Conclusion: 该因果协议可识别模块通信中的计算归属，把一个易被准确率掩盖的问题变为可检验性质，并将GWT的核心主张转化为训练模型上可测试的属性，可迁移到其他通信设计与预训练模型接口。

Abstract: Global Workspace Theory (GWT), a leading theory of consciousness, holds that information becomes conscious when it is broadcast to specialized processes that use it. Building a workspace into a network does not reveal what the trained network computes with it: a module that reports an answer may have combined a broadcast message with its own knowledge, or merely forwarded an answer computed elsewhere, and task accuracy and ablation cannot tell these apart. We introduce a causal protocol that identifies who computes when modules communicate. Its central test transplants a message from another input, chosen so that ignoring, relaying and recombining it predict three different answers. On networks built to relay or to recombine, the test assigns the right computation in every case. Among trained workspace models it finds all three outcomes: one leaves its broadcast nearly unused, and of two models that differ only in their training data, one relays and the other recombines. In AFGW, a language model whose modules communicate only through a learned workspace, a transplanted message yields the answer that recombination predicts in 97% of cases, and its content acts by redirecting what the receiver retrieves from its own memory. The tests carry over to other communication designs and to a known interface of a pretrained Transformer, turning GWT's central claim into a testable property of trained models.

</details>


### [102] [FrontierReplay: Split-Free Exact Prefix Caching for Hybrid Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26020)
*Zekun Wang,Yizhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FrontierReplay：在不切分 prefill 的前提下，在可预测的结构边界处为混合（注意力+循环）模型精确物化检查点，用跨层、仅状态的回放核低成本重建循环状态，并配合前沿感知保留策略，显著降低 TTFT、提升吞吐与 goodput。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语言模型的自注意力+循环层采用原地状态更新，导致前缀复用只能对齐到已保存的精确位置。粗粒度网格保存浪费可复用前缀；细粒度精确缓存需在每个中途检查点切分 prefill 前向，引入的调度开销在高负载下超过复用收益。
