Method: 观察到多轮对话、智能体循环、文档问答与树搜索中，前缀复用几乎总是终止于可预先预测的结构边界（轮次结束、已知分支点）。FrontierReplay 在这些边界处物化精确检查点而不切分 prefill：前向不切分地进行，同时记录一小段循环层输入的窗口，调用跨层、仅状态的回放核重建目标状态（每 token 比全量重算便宜 6–37 倍，且无需主机同步）；前沿感知保留策略优先保留活跃锚点与已验证分支，并将失效检查点槽位借给注意力 KV 缓存。

Result: 在多种混合架构、模型规模与负载下，相较现有精确缓存：中位 TTFT 降低 16–89%（最高 8.9x），80% SLO 容量提升至 1.73x，goodput 提升最高 4.26x（更大规模下 3.67x）。相较近似回放方法，goodput 最高高 1.54x 且保持精确任务准确率，避免近似方法长上下文下最高 17% 的精度下降。

Conclusion: 利用结构边界的可预测性，可在不牺牲 prefill 连续性的情况下实现精确且低开销的前缀状态复用，兼顾效率（TTFT、SLO 容量、goodput）与准确性，优于粗/细粒度精确缓存与近似回放方案。

Abstract: Hybrid language models combine self-attention with recurrent layers whose in-place state updates restrict prefix reuse to exact saved positions. Saving states on a coarse grid wastes reusable prefixes, whereas existing fine-grained exact caches must cut the prefill forward pass at each mid-prompt checkpoint, adding scheduler steps that outweigh the reuse gains under load.
  We observe that across multi-turn chat, agent loops, document question answering, and tree search, prefix reuse almost always ends at structural boundaries predictable in advance, such as turn ends and known branch points. We present FrontierReplay, a prefix caching system that materializes exact checkpoints at these boundaries without cutting the prefill. While the forward pass runs uncut, FrontierReplay logs a short window of recurrent-layer inputs and invokes a cross-layer, state-only replay kernel to build the target state 6-37x cheaper per token than full recomputation without host synchronization. A frontier-aware retention policy prioritizes active anchors and proven branches while lending dead checkpoint slots to the attention KV cache.
  Across diverse hybrid architectures, model scales, and workloads, FrontierReplay delivers clear gains over existing exact caches, lowering median time to first token (TTFT) by 16-89% (up to 8.9x), raising 80% service-level objective (SLO) capacity by up to 1.73x, and improving goodput by up to 4.26x (3.67x at larger scales). Compared with approximate replay methods, FrontierReplay delivers up to 1.54x higher goodput while preserving exact task accuracy, avoiding the up to 17% accuracy drop suffered by approximation on long contexts.

</details>


### [103] [The Delayed Societal Cost of Unfairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26030)
*Giovani Valdrighi,Isabel Valera,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究了在决策引发群体资质分布变化的动态环境下，静态数据上Pareto最优的策略何时会被更公平的策略在部署后种群上超越，并给出Rawlsian与egalitarian公平下的成立条件与警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平机器学习在静态数据上评估决策者损失与群体间成本分配，二者常此消彼长；而决策会改变其所作用的人群，静态数据上设计的公平干预在长期可能失效甚至伤害弱势群体。作者追问：静态部署数据上Pareto最优的策略，何时会在各自诱导的种群上被更公平的策略在双方成本上同时占优。

Method: 构建资质分数随二值决策及其结果动态演化的模型，刻画出策略诱导的部署后种群；在该设定下分别分析Rawlsian公平（不降低任何群体接受率）与egalitarian公平（可能降低优势群体接受率）两种约束，推导“更公平策略在不增加一方成本的前提下降低另一方成本”的充分条件。

Result: 在Rawlsian公平下，当在决策者盈亏平衡点接受弱势个体后其状态对决策者的成本低于拒绝时，赋予公平足够高权重即可降低社会总成本；egalitarian公平下还需弱势群体上决策者损失的下降超过优势群体上的上升。两种公平概念下，当决策者损失基于准确率时，部署后种群的改善可能源于弱势群体资质的下降，即决策者获益并不必然带来决策对象境况改善。

Conclusion: 策略诱导的种群动态可逆转表面上明显的性能—公平权衡，使更公平的策略降低延迟的社会成本；但需警惕以准确率为基础的损失度量可能将弱势群体的资质下滑误读为整体改善。

Abstract: Fair machine learning typically evaluates the societal costs of a decision policy -- the loss incurred by the decision-maker (DM) and the unfair distribution of costs among demographic groups of decision-subjects (DS) -- on static data, where reducing one often comes at the expense of the other. Yet decisions change the populations on which they act, and prior work has shown that fairness interventions designed on static data may fail to benefit, or even harm, the disadvantaged group over time. We investigate a complementary question: When can a policy that is Pareto-optimal on pre-deployment data be outperformed by a fairer policy once each is evaluated on the population it induces, reducing costs for one stakeholder without increasing them for the other? In a setting where qualification scores evolve in response to binary decisions and their outcomes, we show that under Rawlsian fairness, which never lowers any group's acceptance rate, placing sufficiently high weight on fairness reduces societal costs whenever accepting disadvantaged DS at the DM's break-even point leaves them in a state less costly to the DM than rejecting them. Under egalitarian fairness, which may lower the advantaged group's acceptance rate, this additionally requires the reduction in DM loss on the disadvantaged group to outweigh the increase on the advantaged group. Under both fairness notions, however, when DM loss is accuracy-based, improvements in the post-deployment population may arise from declining DS qualification, revealing that gains for the DM do not necessarily translate into better outcomes for DS. Our results show that policy-induced population dynamics can overturn apparent performance-fairness trade-offs, allowing fairer policies to reduce the delayed societal costs of decision-making.

</details>


### [104] [When Do Rankings Beat Signs? Gradient Direction Recovery under Even Nuisance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26055)
*Zhuangzhuang Pan,Yan Xia,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究对一批对称查询对进行排序，能否比仅用对内向符号更少评估地恢复梯度方向；作者分离评价次数与反馈轮数，提出信息层级并给出多轮代价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有成对比较/排序反馈的黑箱优化中，符号信息（对内向顺序）似乎是自然的最小信息量。作者追问：充分利用批内跨对、反向交叉对及全部排序不等式，是否能突破符号法的信息瓶颈、减少函数评估与轮数。

Method: 研究固定中心恢复：线性信号叠加未知可微偶函数扰动（nuisance）的 d 维问题，分别统计函数评估与反馈轮数。构造共享偏移的查询对，形成三档信息层级：Mirror 仅保留对内向符号；Reverse 将反向的跨对顺序耦合以抵消偏移；Full 强制满足所有排序不等式。以“相对壳层变化”度量扰动幅度（查询球面扰动范围除以线性信号尺度）。对正定二次类分析 R 轮极小极大代价，并将有效性扩展到非严格偶函数、在给定误差界下成立；辅以匹配端点实验与两个 ridge 任务上的联合评估—轮数上限实验。

Result: 当相对壳层变化不超过 cε/√d 时，Reverse 可在单轮内以 Õ(d+√(d/ε)) 次评估把所有一致方向定位到角度精度 ε（固定置信度），且解码器无需已知变化界；该结果在半对数因子内匹配下界，而同一类上一轮纯符号法的极小极大代价为 Θ(d/ε)。在无共同相对曲率界的良态正定二次类上，R 轮极小极大代价为 Θ(dR(ε^{-1/R}-1))，即使允许累积排序亦然。匹配端点实验证实存在超越符号与短环的信息；两个 ridge 任务中，短环恢复在联合评估与轮数上限下取得比所测批处理对照更低的终止平均间隙；拟合完整排序的额外优化收益尚未确立。

Conclusion: 批量对称对排序所携带的跨对信息可显著降低梯度方向恢复的评估复杂度，并在多轮设定下给出紧的极小极大刻画；但将完整排序用于实际优化能否带来额外收益仍待证实。

Abstract: Can ranking a batch of antipodal query pairs recover a gradient direction with fewer evaluations than using within-pair signs alone? We study fixed-center recovery in $d$ dimensions for a linear signal plus an unknown differentiable even nuisance, counting function evaluations and feedback rounds separately. Shared pair offsets create an information hierarchy: Mirror retains within-pair signs, Reverse couples reversed cross-pair orders to cancel offsets, and Full enforces all ranking inequalities. Relative shell variation is the nuisance range over the query sphere normalized by the linear-signal scale. When it is at most $c\varepsilon/\sqrt{d}$, Reverse localizes every consistent direction to angular accuracy $\varepsilon$ using $\widetilde{O}(d+\sqrt{d/\varepsilon})$ evaluations in one round at fixed confidence, without a variation bound supplied to the decoder. This matches a lower bound up to logarithmic factors, versus the $Θ(d/\varepsilon)$ one-round sign-only minimax cost on the same class. Over well-conditioned positive-definite quadratics with no common relative-curvature bound, the $R$-round minimax cost is instead $Θ(dR(\varepsilon^{-1/R}-1))$, even with cumulative rankings. We extend validity beyond exact evenness under supplied error bounds. Matched-endpoint experiments certify information beyond signs and short cycles. On two ridge tasks under joint evaluation and round caps, short-cycle recovery attains lower terminal mean gaps than the tested batch-capable controls. An additional optimization benefit from fitting the complete ranking remains unestablished.

</details>


### [105] [Complete-SVD: A Functional Norm Approach to Low-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26059)
*Alper Kalle,Theo Rudkiewicz,Mohamed-Oumar Ouerfelli,Mohamed Tamaazousti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Complete-SVD 是一种面向大语言模型的训练后低秩压缩方法，它通过最小化原模型与压缩模型 logits 之间的 L2 距离（对所有 logit 变化等权）来保持模型的输入—输出行为；借助网络线性化与 Kronecker 分解近似，将目标化为带闭式解的加权 SVD，并用随机探针无偏估计难以计算的输出侧因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后低秩压缩不仅要逼近原始权重，还需保持模型的输入—输出行为。已有输出感知方法虽在模型输出端度量压缩误差，但以观测到的下一个 token 或模型预测概率对 logit 变化加权，隐含地对不同 logit 施加了不等权重；本文希望改为对所有 logit 变化等权处理。

Method: 在原始权重附近对网络做线性化，并结合 Kronecker 因子分解近似，把最小化 logits 的 L2 距离这一目标转化为加权 SVD 问题，从而获得闭式解。其输出侧因子难以解析计算，因此用随机探针进行无偏估计，每条序列仅需 K 次反向传播，成本与词表大小和序列长度无关。此外还提出以 KL 散度替代 L2 距离的 KL-SVD 变体，沿用同一估计框架。

Result: 在均匀秩与 WikiText-2 校准的匹配设置下，Complete-SVD 在每种设置中均取得最低的 C4 与 PTB 困惑度，并在 LEMS 秩分配、压缩比 0.7 时同样最优。KL-SVD 在多数设置下降低校准困惑度并提升下游准确率，但跨语料困惑度上升。Complete-SVD 在 IFEval 指令遵循准确率上始终优于最强的既有输出感知方法。

Conclusion: 等权度量全部 logit 变化的完整输出误差并在闭式加权 SVD 框架下求解，是一种有效且成本可控的低秩压缩准则，在困惑度与指令遵循等指标上优于既有输出感知方法；以 KL 散度替代 L2 则在分布内校准与下游任务上更优，但牺牲跨语料泛化。

Abstract: Post-training low-rank compression of neural networks must not only approximate the original weights but also preserve the model's input-output behavior. Recent output-aware methods measure the compression error beyond the compressed layer, at the model output, weighting logit changes by the observed next token or by the model's predicted probabilities. We propose Complete-SVD, a method for large language models that minimizes the $L_2$ distance between the logits of the original and compressed models, weighting all logit changes equally. Linearizing the network around the original weights, together with a Kronecker-factored approximation, reduces this objective to a weighted SVD with a closed-form solution. Its output-side factor is intractable to compute; we estimate it without bias from random probes, at a cost of $K$ backward passes per sequence, independent of vocabulary size and sequence length. At matched settings with uniform ranks and WikiText-2 calibration, Complete-SVD achieves the lowest C4 and PTB perplexity in every setting, and at ratio 0.7 under LEMS rank allocation. Replacing the $L_2$ distance by the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL-SVD), with the same estimation framework, lowers calibration perplexity in most settings and raises downstream accuracy, but raises cross-corpus perplexity. Complete-SVD also improves IFEval instruction-following accuracy over the strongest prior output-aware methods throughout.

</details>


### [106] [Iterative Scarcity-Guided Exploration: Bootstrapping Generative Auto-bidding from Narrow Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26060)
*Qingmao Yao,Guangzheng Hu,Yusen Huo,Zhilin Zhang,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Xu Chu,Bo Zheng,Xiaotie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代稀缺性引导探索（ISGE），通过"稀缺性信号"在回报信号衰减时接管探索引导，打破扩散式AIGB在冷启动迭代训练中的性能停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告自动出价在冷启动场景依赖迭代训练弥补历史数据质量低、支撑范围窄的问题，但代表性的扩散式AI生成出价（AIGB）方法在迭代训练中无法维持对窄数据支撑之外的外推，停滞于次优水平。

Method: 理论上将停滞归因于信噪比（SNR）崩塌：微弱的径向回报信号被曲率诱导的惩罚项压制。为此提出ISGE，引入"引导交接"机制——当回报信号崩塌时由稀缺性接管作为探索信号，将SNR提升至临界阈值之上。ISGE在"评判器"（为轨迹分配稀缺性分数）与"探索器"（引导生成高稀缺性、高回报轨迹）之间迭代交替，用自生成轨迹从窄支撑中自举。

Result: 在工业级AuctionNet基准上的大量实验表明，ISGE从冷启动数据集出发，在三次迭代内即有效超越全数据集（Full-Dataset）基线的性能。

Conclusion: 以稀缺性作为回报信号衰减后的替代探索信号，可有效缓解扩散式生成策略在迭代训练中的SNR崩塌与外推停滞，实现仅凭冷启动数据快速超越全量数据训练的效果。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a core algorithmic component in online advertising auctions. In practical cold-start settings, iterative training is commonly used to compensate for low-quality, narrowly supported historical data. Unfortunately, in iterative training, representative diffusion-based AI-Generated Bidding (AIGB) methods fail to sustain extrapolation beyond the narrow data support and stagnate at a suboptimal level. In this paper, we theoretically attribute this stagnation to Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) collapse: the weak radial return signal is overwhelmed by a curvature-induced penalty. To break this stagnation, we propose \textbf{Iterative Scarcity-Guided Exploration (ISGE)}, which introduces a guidance handoff: as the return signal collapses, scarcity subsequently takes over as an exploratory signal to elevate the SNR above a critical threshold. Specifically, ISGE iteratively alternates between a \emph{Judger} that assigns scarcity scores to trajectories and an \emph{Explorer} that performs guided generation of high-scarcity and high-return trajectories, thereby bootstrapping from the narrow support with such self-generated trajectories. Extensive experiments on the industrial AuctionNet benchmark demonstrate that ISGE, starting from cold-start datasets, effectively surpasses the performance of the Full-Dataset baseline within three iterations. Code is available at https://github.com/alimama-tech/ISGE

</details>


### [107] [Learning to Compare Neural Representations from Parameterized Computation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26081)
*Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Daniel Herbst,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于图元网络(graph metanetwork)的框架，可直接从模型参数中学习跨独立训练模型的内部表示相似性，从而在无需推理前向采集激活的情况下预测层间对应关系并辅助模型复用组件选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同训练模型的内部表示对识别对应层、选择可复用的模型组件很重要，但直接比较需在推理时用已知参考输入评估候选模型并收集内部激活，代价高且难以针对未见模型预先进行。作者希望探讨跨模型测得的表示相似性能否从参数化计算中被"学习"出来，并泛化到新模型。

Method: 构建一个框架：对每个内部位置(internal site)，用图元网络从其上游计算导出嵌入表示，并显式处理隐藏坐标置换(hidden-coordinate permutation)与内部注意力基变换(attention-basis transformation)等对称性问题；理论上分析估计器的结构性质，并给出层检索与拼接候选选择的条件性保证。

Result: 学习到的预测器能支持表示关系估计并实现低遗憾(low-regret)的层检索；在未用拼接结果训练的情况下，也能筛选出有用的拼接连接。

Conclusion: 跨一组训练模型学到的表示关系可泛化到新计算，预测的关系可在直接测量或适配器拟合之前指导候选选择，为直接从训练好的神经网络研究表示相似性开辟新途径。

Abstract: Comparing internal representations across trained models is important for identifying corresponding layers and selecting components for model reuse. Yet, direct comparisons at inference require evaluating candidate models on known reference inputs and collecting their internal activations. In this paper, we study whether internal representation similarity measured across independently trained models can instead be learned to predict in unseen models from their parameterized computations. We introduce a framework that applies a graph metanetwork for mapping each internal site to an embedding derived from its upstream computations while accounting for hidden-coordinate permutation and internal attention-basis transformations. We analyze the structural properties of our estimators and derive conditional guarantees for layer retrieval and stitching candidate selection. Empirically, we find that the learned predictors support relation estimation and low-regret layer retrieval. They also select useful stitching connections while not being trained on stitching outcomes. This opens up a new way to study representation similarity directly from trained neural networks: representational relations learned across a collection of trained models can generalize to new computations, and that the predicted relations can guide candidate selection before direct measurement or adapter fitting for functional evaluations.

</details>


### [108] [RL Can Only Reinforce What It Sees Succeed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26084)
*Ruben Sanchez-Corcuera,Unai Zulaika,Aritz Bilbao-Jayo,Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在"熟悉规则失效"的情境下，结果奖励强化学习（RL）如何改变模型在预训练先验与当前观察之间的权衡。结论：RL只能强化其观测到成功的动作；正确动作罕见时，单次训练要么处处学会、要么完全学不会，而成败可由训练前的一个计数（动作获胜的尝试组期望数）预测，约35组为均等胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当先验知识（预训练所学）与新观察冲突时，基于结果奖励的RL如何调整二者的权重；并希望在一个先验与正确答案都已知的可控环境中精确量化这一过程。

Method: 构建"puzzle world"：由贝叶斯玩家进行游戏，从零训练小型语言模型（1.3M–85M参数），使先验分布与正确答案均为精确已知。在此环境上做结果奖励RL，考察训练前后模型的权衡方式，并分析正确动作出现频率对学习结果的影响，检验学习率、优化器、GRPO变体、模型规模及4–256次尝试分组等条件。

Result: 基础模型像其语料一样"对冲"（hedge），RL将这种对冲转化为决策，方向由奖励决定；但RL只能强化其观测到的成功。当正确动作罕见时，每次训练要么在所有地方学会该动作，要么完全学不会，且哪种情况会出现可由训练前的单一计数——动作获胜的尝试组期望数——预测，约35组对应均等胜率。该计数在重复与全新问题、4–256次尝试分组、不同学习率/优化器/GRPO变体/模型规模下均成立；这些因素只移动阈值而不改变被计数的对象。

Conclusion: 结果奖励RL将预训练先验转化为决策，但其学习受"可观测成功"的限制；稀有正确动作的习得呈全有或全无（all-or-none）现象，且可由训练前的期望成功组数精确预测（约35组为临界）。阈值随超参数与模型规模变化，但预测变量本身稳健。

Abstract: When a familiar rule stops working, an agent has to weigh what pre-training taught it against what it now observes. We ask how outcome-reward reinforcement learning (RL) changes this balance, in a puzzle world where both sides are known: small language models trained from scratch on games played by a Bayesian player, so that the prior and the right answer are exact. Base models hedge as their corpus did, and RL turns that hedging into decisions, in whichever direction the reward favours. But RL can only reinforce what it sees succeed. When the right action is rare, each run either learns it everywhere or not at all, and which runs take off is predicted before training by one count: the expected number of groups of attempts in which the action wins. About 35 such groups give even odds. The count holds for new and repeated problems and for groups of 4 to 256 attempts, and across learning rates, optimizers, GRPO variants and model sizes from 1.3M to 85M parameters, which move the threshold but not what is counted.

</details>


### [109] [Approximate Adjoint Models for Memory and Compute-Efficient Online Learning of Hybrid Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26095)
*Emilio González-Zamora,Pierre Tandeo,Laurent Debreu,Said Ouala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一类梯度近似方法：在全轨迹上保持伴随递推，仅近似其中的pullback算子，anchor状态用自动微分精确计算，anchor之间用恒等映射(Zero-order)或复用冻结的pullback(Pullback Reuse)，使块内参数pullback恒定从而把梯度求和因子化，将每步一次评估降为每块一次；内存降一个量级、运行时间降四倍，混合模型训练精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微编程通过最小化轨迹累积误差实现在线校准，但伴随法/BPTT的计算与内存开销随训练窗口长度增长，限制了混合模型训练时长与分辨率；截断BPTT虽省内存却丢弃跨截断边界的敏感性，引入梯度偏差并忽略长程依赖。

Method: 在全轨迹上保留伴随递推，只近似其中的pullback算子：仅在稀疏anchor状态用自动微分计算精确pullback，anchor之间敏感性经恒等映射(Zero-order)或复用的冻结pullback(Pullback Reuse)传播；该近似使每个chunk内参数pullback为常数，梯度求和可因子化，每chunk一次评估替代每时间步一次。在三个复杂度递增的系统上验证：不完整Lorenz-63、二维准地转湍流的大涡闭合、多层准地转海洋模式偏差校正。

Result: 峰值内存最多下降一个数量级，运行时间最多降至四分之一，其中Pullback Reuse的梯度方向最接近精确梯度；三个案例中近似梯度均支持精度相当的混合模型训练。

Conclusion: 有效的在线学习不要求精确梯度；在保持伴随递推完整性的前提下近似pullback算子，可在显著降低内存与时间成本的同时维持混合模型训练精度。

Abstract: Differentiable programming enables online calibration of numerical models by minimizing errors accumulated along simulated trajectories. This includes estimating physical parameters and training neural sub-models in hybrid configurations. Gradients are typically computed using adjoint methods, or backpropagation through time (BPTT), whose computational and memory costs grow with the length of the training window. This limits the duration and resolution of hybrid-model training, a major bottleneck for realistic geophysical applications. Approaches like truncated BPTT reduces memory use by discarding sensitivities across truncation boundaries, introducing gradient bias and neglecting long-range dependencies. We propose a class of gradient approximation methods that preserve the adjoint recurrence over the full trajectory while approximating only the pullback operators within it. Exact pullbacks are computed by automatic differentiation at sparse anchor states only. Between anchors, sensitivities propagate either through the identity (Zero-order) or through a reused, frozen pullback (Pullback Reuse). This approximation makes the parameter pullback constant within each chunk, allowing the gradient sum to factorize so that a single evaluation per chunk replaces one per time step. We evaluate both on three systems of increasing complexity, an incomplete Lorenz-63 model, a large-eddy closure for two-dimensional quasi-geostrophic turbulence, and bias correction in a multilayer quasi-geostrophic ocean model. Peak memory falls by up to an order of magnitude and runtime up to fourfold, with Pullback Reuse staying closest to the exact gradient direction. In all three cases, approximate gradients support hybrid-model training at comparable accuracy, showing that effective online learning does not require exact gradients.

</details>


### [110] [Revealing Pathway Interactions in Network Computations with Complete Path Contribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26101)
*Shenghua Liu,Youssef Faragalla,Surya Ganguli,Stephen A. Baccus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Complete Path Contribution（路径贡献）方法，将网络输出完整分解为单条因果路径的贡献，并支持任意粒度电路的完备归因；配合仅需O(k)时间的Top-k强路径搜索算法，可在规模上刻画兴奋-抑制路径交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法多只分析单元、层或注意力头之间的成对交互，即便事后拼成电路，也无法刻画从输入到输出的完整因果链（路径）上的高阶交互以及指数级路径间的相互作用，导致"抑制通路是否否决兴奋通路、二者如何共同决定网络决策"等问题无法回答。

Method: 提出Complete Path Contribution：将网络输出分解为各条单独路径的数学完备贡献，直接研究路径级交互；通过对贡献做部分求和，为任意粒度、任意电路给出贡献定义，并在每一层级保证完备性。另给出高效算法，无需枚举全部路径即可识别k条最强路径，运行时间经验上随k线性而非随深度指数增长。

Result: 在基准图像分类网络上量化了兴奋-抑制交互，并以前所未有的细节展示二者如何共同构成网络决策；在小鼠视网膜可解释模型中，揭示了在时变自然刺激下，不同细胞类型间通路上的兴奋-抑制交互模体如何产生动态感受野。

Conclusion: Path Contribution在规模上揭示了网络因果通路如何交互并将输入变换为输出，为机制可解释性提供了完备、可扩展的路径级分析框架。

Abstract: Networks pass signals through many layers of interconnected units, so explaining what they compute means tracing how information travels from input to output and how pathways interact. Most existing approaches analyze only pairwise interactions between components such as units, layers or attention heads. Even when these measurements are combined post hoc into a circuit, they do not capture higher-order interactions along full causal chains (paths) from input to output, and so cannot address how exponentially many paths interact to construct network computation. This leaves a large set of mechanistic questions unanswerable, for example whether inhibitory pathways veto excitatory ones, and how the two combine to yield network decisions. To address this gap, we introduce Complete Path Contribution, a method to decompose the network output into mathematically complete contributions of individual paths to study path-level interactions directly and at scale. Partial summation then induces definitions of contributions for any circuit at any granularity level, with completeness guaranteed at every level. We further present an efficient algorithm that identifies the $k$ strongest paths without examining all of them, with runtime empirically linear in $k$ rather than exponential in depth. By applying Path Contribution to benchmark image classification networks, we quantify excitatory-inhibitory interactions and show how they collectively construct network decisions at a level of detail previously unmeasurable. Furthermore, in an interpretable model of the mouse retina, it reveals how dynamic receptive fields emerge from excitatory-inhibitory interaction motifs among pathways between different cell types in response to time-varying natural stimuli. Overall, Path Contribution reveals how a network's causal pathways interact at scale to transform input to output.

</details>


### [111] [How Network Topology and Data Heterogeneity Shape Decentralized Learning: A Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26104)
*Luigi Palmieri,Lorenzo Valerio,Chiara Boldrini,Andrea Passarella,Marco Conti,János Kertész*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱框架，将去中心化联邦学习中网络拓扑的作用分解为占优特征模态决定共识轨迹与节点影响力、非占优特征模态控制分歧传播与衰减两种互补机制，并揭示均匀邻域平均下稳态影响力正比于节点度数加一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析多聚焦谱混合性质，且普遍假设双随机聚合，忽略了不规则通信网络下拓扑相关的节点影响力差异，难以刻画非IID数据下局部信息的相对贡献。

Method: 构建谱分解框架，分离占优与非占优特征模态两种机制；在均匀邻域平均下推导稳态影响力与节点度数的关系，导出模型分歧与谱混合、梯度异质性之间的界，并分析数据分布与两种机制的交互。

Result: 在规则与异构拓扑上的实验表明：IID数据下学习动力学几乎一致；非IID数据下网络规模与连通性影响不均分布信息的学习方式，并可刻画数据分布对局部信息相对贡献的塑造作用。

Conclusion: 拓扑影响可通过谱机制统一解释，稳态节点影响力由度数决定，非IID场景下网络结构与数据分布共同决定共识动力学中局部信息的权重。

Abstract: Network topology influences decentralized federated learning through information mixing and the relative contribution of individual nodes. While existing analyses primarily focus on spectral mixing properties, they often assume doubly stochastic aggregation, overlooking topology-dependent influence under irregular communication networks. We develop a spectral framework that separates two complementary mechanisms: the dominant eigenmode determines the consensus trajectory and node influence, while non-dominant eigenmodes govern the propagation and attenuation of disagreement. Under uniform neighborhood averaging, we show that the stationary influence is proportional to node degree plus one and derive a bound relating model disagreement to spectral mixing and gradient heterogeneity. We then investigate how these mechanisms interact with the data distribution across nodes. Experiments across regular and heterogeneous network topologies reveal nearly identical learning dynamics under IID data, while network size and connectivity affect how unevenly distributed information is learned under non-IID data. For non-IID settings, we characterize how the distribution of data across nodes shapes the relative contribution of local information to consensus dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [Loss-Guided Tsetlin Machines: Making the Learning Objective Explicit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26114)
*Mayur Shende,Runar Helin,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Vladimir I. Zadorozhny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Loss-Guided TM，用可替换的显式损失函数替代Tsetlin Machine固定的隐式margin更新规则(MBUR)，在保持Tsetlin Automaton与clause逻辑不变的前提下实现任务定制化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Tsetlin Machine没有显式损失函数概念，其学习信号固定为隐式、基于margin的更新规则(MBUR)，无法针对特定任务选择合适的目标函数，限制了其在损失函数设计方面的拓展。

Method: 将MBUR替换为显式且可交换的损失函数；由损失驱动clause反馈与权重更新，同时保持Tsetlin Automaton与clause逻辑不变；引入交叉熵(CE)、对称交叉熵(SCE)与非对称损失(ASL)三种损失；在6个涵盖多分类、多标签与二分类的数据集上评估。

Result: 在合适损失下，Loss-Guided TM在6个数据集中的5个上匹配或超过MBUR的准确率与F1；在不平衡多标签数据集上提升最大，ASL使macro F1最高提升9个百分点；使用CE时在全部6个数据集上校准显著优于MBUR，校准误差降低3至11倍；定性分析表明clause级全局可解释性(在图像与文本数据的每类层面考察)得以保留。

Conclusion: 显式TM学习目标可直接针对不确定性校准、数据不平衡与任务结构进行优化，使TM能够接入更广泛的损失函数研究体系。

Abstract: The Tsetlin Machine (TM) has no explicit notion of a loss function. Its learning signal is fixed to an implicit, margin-based update rule (MBUR), with no way to choose an objective function suited to a specific task. This paper introduces Loss-Guided TM, which replaces MBUR with an explicit and swappable loss function. In our proposed method, clause feedback and weight updates are driven by the loss, while keeping the Tsetlin Automaton and clause logic unchanged. The approach incorporates cross entropy (CE), symmetric cross entropy (SCE), and asymmetric loss (ASL). We evaluate Loss-Guided TM across six datasets that span multiclass, multilabel, and binary classification tasks. With a suitable loss, Loss-Guided TM matches or improves accuracy and F1 over MBUR on five of the six datasets. The gains are greatest for the imbalanced multilabel datasets, where ASL improves macro F1 by up to 9 percentage points. With CE, Loss-Guided TM is also significantly better calibrated than MBUR on all six datasets, reducing calibration error by a factor of 3 to 11. A qualitative inspection shows that the clause-based global interpretability, examined at the per-class level on image and text data, is preserved when using the Loss-Guided mechanism. These results show that our explicit TM learning objective allows direct targeting of uncertainty calibration, data imbalance, and task structure, opening the TM to the broader literature on loss functions.

</details>


### [113] [NariQ: Sub-3-Bit Quantization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26157)
*Doyeob Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NariQ 是一种基于网格编码（trellis-coded）的训练后量化方法，采用 BF16 模型在智能体会话中生成的 token 进行校准，在 Qwen3.8-27B 上以 3.0/2.5 bit/weight 达到与 BF16 持平的长程智能体任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务需在 100K+ token 上下文中多步调用工具，而现有低比特量化方法多用短网络文本校准、以困惑度评估，存在校准-评估错配。实测表明 ≤3 bit 的 Qwen3.8-27B 量化在 AIME 等短任务上与 BF16 持平，但在 DeepSWE 长程智能体基准上最多仅达到 BF16 成功任务数的 72%。

Method: 提出 NariQ：trellis-coded 训练后量化（PTQ），关键设计是用 BF16 模型在真实智能体会话中生成的 token 作为校准数据，使量化误差分布对齐长程智能体的实际激活/上下文分布；并提供 GGUF 权重与 llama.cpp fork 推理支持。

Result: 在 Qwen3.8-27B 上，3.0 bit 与 2.5 bit 权重下于 DeepSWE、Terminal Bench 两个长程智能体基准及 AIME、RealWorldQA 上均与 BF16 持平；2.0 bit 版本性能落后于该水平。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首批被证明在长程智能体任务上可匹敌全精度模型的 ≤3 bit/weight 量化结果；强调校准数据与评估场景匹配的重要性，并开源权重（Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF）与推理实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks require a model to call tools over many steps at contexts of 100K tokens or more, yet many low-bit quantization methods are calibrated on short web text and judged on perplexity. This mismatch is costly: existing quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B at or below 3 bits per weight match BF16 on shorter tasks like AIME, yet in published results solve at most 72% as many tasks as BF16 on DeepSWE, a long-horizon agentic benchmark. We propose NariQ, a trellis-coded post-training quantization method that calibrates on tokens generated by the BF16 model in agent sessions. On Qwen3.8-27B, NariQ at 3.0 and 2.5 bits per weight stays level with BF16 on two long-horizon agentic benchmarks, DeepSWE and Terminal Bench, as well as on AIME and RealWorldQA. Our checkpoint at 2.0 bits per weight trails this performance. To our knowledge, these are the first quantizations at or below 3 bits per weight shown to match their full-precision version on long-horizon agentic tasks. We release the files as Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF (https://huggingface.co/nari-labs/Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF), with inference available through our llama.cpp fork at https://github.com/nari-labs/nari-llama.cpp.

</details>


### [114] [Budget Internalization: Fixed-Budget RLVR Trades Test-Time Scaling for In-Budget Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26172)
*Ilia Zavidnyi,Alexander Kovrigin,Arina Puchkova,Kirill Gelvan,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现RLVR中固定的rollout长度上限会被模型"内化"为一种长度约束（budget internalization），使模型在推理时几乎不超出训练预算使用token，导致大预算下准确率反而低于基座模型；该效应非RL特有，而源于长度截断的后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型具备test-time scaling能力（答案更长时更准确），但RLVR训练通常设定固定rollout上限。以往认为该上限只控制训练成本，作者要检验它是否改变模型行为、限制测试时扩展能力。

Method: 在Qwen3.5-4B、Gemma4-E2B、Nemotron-3-Nano-4B上，针对数学推理与程序化生成问题两类任务进行RLVR训练，并测试跨任务（科学、代码）迁移；在Qwen3.5-4B上做训练目标层面的消融：增强KL正则、降低训练数据截断、对截断rollout强制给出答案；另做SFT对照（人工截断轨迹 vs 自然短轨迹）。

Result: 训练后模型仅比训练预算多用少量token，在预算附近优于基座模型，但更大预算下准确率停滞并低于基座；效应可迁移至科学和代码任务。多数目标层消融难以恢复test-time scaling；更强KL和更低的训练数据截断能保留更多扩展性，但强KL降低预算内准确率；对截断rollout强制答案基本消除budget internalization，同时损失大部分预算内收益；人工截断轨迹的SFT可复现该效应，自然短轨迹SFT则不会。

Conclusion: budget internalization源于长度截断的后训练（length-censored post-training），而非RL特有的目标项。rollout上限不仅控制训练成本，还可能以低预算收益换取在更多推理算力下的性能下降，因此需要重新审视rollout长度设置。

Abstract: Reasoning models often become more accurate when they can generate longer answers at test time. This behavior is known as test-time scaling. However, RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually trains them with a fixed rollout cap. We find that this cap changes model behavior, not only training cost. After training, models use few additional tokens beyond the training budget. They outperform the base models near that budget, but their accuracy stops improving and falls below the base model at larger budgets. The rollout cap thus becomes a learned constraint, that limits model's test-time scaling. We call this effect budget internalization. We observe it across Qwen3.5-4B, Gemma4-E2B, and Nemotron-3-Nano-4B and two reasoning training tasks: mathematical reasoning and diverse, procedurally generated problems. This effect also transfers from math training to science and coding tasks. Training-objective level ablations on Qwen3.5-4B show that most changes recover little test-time scaling. Stronger KL regularization and lower training-data truncation preserve more scaling, but stronger KL reduces in-budget accuracy. Forcing an answer on truncated training rollouts largely removes budget internalization, but also removes most of the in-budget gain. SFT on artificially truncated traces reproduces budget internalization, whereas SFT on naturally short traces does not, which shows that the effect is not specific to RL. This result links budget internalization to length-censored post-training rather than to an RL-specific objective term. Thus, a rollout cap does not only control training cost. It can trade low-budget gains for lower performance when additional inference compute is available.

</details>


### [115] [Thermodynamically Consistent Neural Networks for a Universal Fluid Gibbs Free Energy Equation of State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26183)
*Xurui Li,Zhiguo Gan,Jiaming Zhang,Hongxi Zeng,Diannan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以Gibbs自由能为出发点，借助热力学一致神经网络构建流体状态方程GEoS，实现流体状态的统一表示，并在密度预测与泛化性上优于体积平移立方方程和现有机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去三百年热力学研究者致力于开发兼顾高精度与广适用性的流体状态方程，提出多种理论框架与模型；本文希望在既有工作基础上进一步实现高精度、强泛化的统一流体状态表示。

Method: 以Gibbs自由能为起点，引入热力学一致神经网络，构建基于Gibbs自由能的流体状态方程（GEoS），形成统一的机器学习框架；并采用UMAP降维方法分析测试集分子的外推特性，同时用GEoS拟合PC-SAFT方程。

Result: GEoS在气相与液相密度测试集上表现良好，平均绝对相对偏差（AARD）分别为5.25%和3.96%，优于传统体积平移立方状态方程与现有机器学习模型；UMAP分析显示测试集多数分子属于外推体系，验证了模型较强的泛化能力；用GEoS拟合的PC-SAFT方程保持较高预测精度。

Conclusion: GEoS作为一种热力学一致的机器学习状态方程框架，兼具高精度与强泛化性，为工业实际应用提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Over the past three centuries, many thermodynamics researchers have worked to develop fluid equations of state with both high accuracy and broad applicability, proposing various theoretical frameworks and models. Building on these efforts, this study uses Gibbs free energy as the starting point and introduces thermodynamically consistent neural networks to construct a fluid equation of state based on Gibbs free energy (GEoS). This approach establishes a novel machine learning framework that provides a unified representation of fluid states. GEoS performs well on the vapor- and liquid-phase density test sets, with average absolute relative deviations (AARDs) of 5.25% and 3.96%, respectively. It outperforms conventional volume-translated cubic equations of state and existing machine learning models, demonstrating its effectiveness. Further analysis using the UMAP dimensionality reduction method shows that most molecules in the test set belong to extrapolation systems, confirming the strong generalization ability of the model. Finally, the test results show that the PC-SAFT equation fitted using GEoS maintains high predictive accuracy, providing a reliable basis for its practical application in industry.

</details>


### [116] [Invertible Transport Between Sample-Only Distributions via Projected Characteristic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26187)
*Juan Gutiérrez,Victor Gutiérrez-García,José Luis Blanco-Murillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Projected Characteristic-Function matching (PCF)，一个无需密度的框架，用于学习数据分布与仅由样本给定的先验之间的可逆映射，避免密度估计与对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多有用的潜分布只能采样而无可处理密度（如经验观测集合、科学模拟器、黑盒生成模型），而基于似然的normalizing flows需要评估先验密度，因此这些"仅样本"先验无法直接使用，需要一种密度无关的传输方法。

Method: 利用投影经验特征函数对齐分布，无需密度估计、对抗训练或配对观测；针对传输映射的非唯一性，引入可选的传输正则项，在选择更低位移变换的同时保持端点对齐。

Result: 在受控传输、样本效率研究和高维对齐任务上评测：在受控基准和原始像素MNIST–USPS上精度与kernel MMD相当，且代价随batch size线性增长；在15组配对实验中13组达到比MMD更低的端点误差；避免成对核计算使不一致性内存降低29.8%，并可在kernel MMD超出GPU显存的batch size下训练。

Conclusion: PCF提供了准确且高效的可逆传输，尤其在样本与内存受限场景中具有优势。

Abstract: Many useful latent distributions can be sampled but do not have a tractable density. Such distributions may be represented by empirical collections of real-world observations or generated by scientific simulators and black-box generative models. These sample-only priors cannot be used directly in likelihood-based normalizing flows, which require evaluating the prior density. We propose Projected Characteristic-Function matching (PCF), a density-free framework for learning an invertible map between a data distribution and a prior specified only through samples.
  Our method aligns distributions using projected empirical characteristic functions, eliminating the need for density estimation, adversarial training, or paired observations. To address the non-uniqueness of the transport map, we introduce an optional transport regularizer that selects lower-displacement transformations while preserving endpoint alignment.
  We benchmark PCF on controlled transports, sample-efficiency studies, and high-dimensional alignment tasks. PCF matches the accuracy of kernel MMD on the controlled benchmark and on raw-pixel MNIST--USPS at a cost linear in the batch size, and it is more sample-efficient, achieving lower endpoint error than MMD in 13 of 15 paired runs across library sizes. By avoiding pairwise kernel evaluation, it reduces discrepancy memory by 29.8% and trains at batch sizes where kernel MMD exceeds GPU memory. These results show that PCF provides accurate and efficient invertible transport, particularly in sample- and memory-limited settings.

</details>


### [117] [Learn, Freeze, and Transfer an Activation Function Across Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26201)
*Arun Prakash A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"学习-冻结-迁移"（learn-freeze-transfer）协议，验证了学习得到的激活函数可与权重解耦地迁移复用：以FAct（五参数、两谐波截断傅里叶级数表示的激活，由GELU的傅里叶近似初始化）在ViT/ImageNet-1K上学习后冻结，作为目标网络的固定激活。同架构从头训练的ViT上Top-1提升1.46个百分点；在4种较小架构×4个数据集共16组设置中15组取得最高平均精度，最多优于最佳常规激活1.71个百分点；逐激活调参后仍11组第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习通常只迁移源网络的权重，作者希望探究一种互补形式：学习到的激活函数能否脱离权重、在从头训练的目标网络中继续保持有效性，从而检验激活函数所编码的知识是否具有架构与任务层面的可迁移性。

Method: 采用learn-freeze-transfer协议：激活函数与源网络在同一次训练中联合优化，随后冻结并植入从头训练的目标网络。选用全局共享参数的点式激活FAct（五参数、两谐波截断傅里叶级数），由GELU的傅里叶近似初始化，在ImageNet-1K的ViT上学习；由于点式且参数全局共享，其与层维度无关，可直接插入不同架构。实验在4个较小目标架构（标准ViT、Swin Transformer、线性注意力ViT、MLP-Mixer）与4个图像分类数据集上，与11种激活函数比较，并分别在统一训练配置与逐激活独立调参（相同搜索预算）两种设定下评估。

Result: 同架构ViT从头训练时，Frozen FAct较GELU的Top-1提升1.46个百分点。跨4架构×4数据集共16组设置，统一配置下15组取得最高平均精度，最多优于最佳常规激活1.71个百分点；逐激活调参后仍11组第一、4组第二、1组第四。

Conclusion: 学习得到的激活函数可以作为一种独立于权重的可迁移知识，在跨视觉架构与数据集时保持有效性；这为激活函数层面的迁移学习提供了实证支持，并补充了以权重为中心的常规迁移范式。

Abstract: Transfer learning typically reuses knowledge acquired by a source network through its learned weights. We investigate whether a learned activation function can also be transferred and remain useful in networks trained from scratch. We study this complementary form of transfer through a learn-freeze-transfer protocol, in which the activation is optimized jointly with a source network in a single training run, and is then frozen and reused in target networks trained from scratch. A pointwise activation with globally shared parameters is independent of layer dimensions and can therefore be incorporated into networks of different architectures trained from scratch. We instantiate this protocol with FAct, a five-parameter activation represented by a two-harmonic truncated Fourier series, initialized from a Fourier approximation of GELU and learned in a Vision Transformer (ViT) on ImageNet-1K. When reused in a ViT of the same architecture trained from scratch on ImageNet-1K, the frozen activation (Frozen FAct) improves top-1 accuracy by 1.46 percentage points over GELU. Across four smaller target architectures (standard ViT, Swin Transformer, linear-attention ViT, and MLP-Mixer) and four image-classification datasets, it achieves the highest mean accuracy among eleven activation functions in 15 of 16 settings under a common training configuration, with improvements of up to 1.71 percentage points over the best conventional activation. When the learning rate and weight decay are tuned separately for each activation under the same search budget, Frozen FAct still ranks first in 11 of the 16 settings, second in four, and fourth in one. These results provide empirical evidence that a learned activation function can retain its usefulness when transferred across vision architectures, independently of the weights with which it was learned.

</details>


### [118] [Attention Head Specialization: Phase Transitions in Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26228)
*Léo Catteau,O. Duranthon,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从训练数据量的角度研究多头注意力中的头专业化（head specialization），发现其在样本复杂度上表现为真正的相变，并与在线学习中的分阶段涌现相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于注意力头功能专业化的研究多从训练时间（training time）角度考察，而作者关注头专业化如何依赖于可用训练数据量（样本复杂度），以厘清专业化现象中统计起源与动力学起源的区别。

Method: (1) 在 BERT 上做经验研究，考察针对句法关系的专门化头随数据集规模增大而涌现的现象；(2) 构建可解析求解的高维多注意力头模型，从理论上分析头专业化的相变性质及数据结构和正则化的影响。

Result: 经验上，BERT 中专用于句法关系的头随数据量增加而出现；理论上，头专业化是高维模型中样本复杂度上的真正相变，对应测试误差等可观测量（observables）的非解析性（non-analyticity）。依数据结构和正则化不同，专业化可能先伴随无信息量的头分化，或通过一系列相变级联逐步恢复较弱的潜在特征，构成一种统计形式的简单性偏置（simplicity bias）。

Conclusion: 头专业化具有统计层面的相变本质，可与在线学习中的分阶段涌现相对应，从而将头专业化的统计起源与动力学起源加以区分。

Abstract: Attention heads specialize into distinct functions during training, often emerging in stages together with sharp changes in model behavior. While this phenomenon has mostly been studied as a function of training time, we ask how head specialization depends on the amount of available training data. Empirically, we find that heads specialized to syntactic relations in BERT emerge as the dataset size increases. We then study this phenomenon in a solvable high-dimensional model of multi-head attention and establish that, in this model, head specialization is a genuine phase transition in sample complexity, corresponding to a non-analyticity in the test error and other observables. Depending on data structure and regularization, specialization can follow uninformative head differentiation or occur through a cascade of phase transitions that progressively recover weaker latent features, providing a statistical form of simplicity bias. Finally, we relate these statistical transitions to the stagewise emergence of specialized heads during online learning, disentangling statistical from dynamical origins of head specialization.

</details>


### [119] [Time Series Structure Learning with Local Causal Parent Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26232)
*Jacqueline Maasch,Shu Wan,Hank Zhu,Huan Liu,Zhiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ITPD——一种基于约束的、局部到全局的多元时间序列因果结构学习算法，通过利用时间结构先验、图划分与局部马尔可夫条件，用低阶条件独立检验替代高阶检验，从而降低计算与样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列上的学习与推断面临自相关观测、分布漂移和维数灾难等统计挑战，使本就计算与样本复杂度高的因果发现算法难以实用。

Method: ITPD采用局部到全局策略：依次为每个变量发现其父集以构建全局图；利用时间结构先验、图划分和局部马尔可夫条件，将高阶条件独立检验替换为低阶检验；并被证明是可靠（sound）且完备（complete）的约束型算法，适用于具有因果非平稳性的多元时间序列。

Result: 在精确oracle下，ITPD的检验次数最少、平均条件集规模最小；仿真基准中在F1与运行时间上处于Pareto前沿，在大图（≥1000节点）上快于基线；2000节点时分别比iterative IAMB、DF-PC和PCMCI快2.3×、9.1×和40.9×。

Conclusion: ITPD通过利用时间序列结构先验有效降低条件独立检验阶数，在保持可靠性与完备性的同时兼顾精度与效率，适用于大规模多元时间序列的因果发现。

Abstract: Learning and inference on time series can pose significant statistical challenges, including autocorrelated observations, distribution shift over time, and the curse of dimensionality. These challenges complicate the practical application of causal discovery algorithms, which already suffer from high computational and sample complexity in the general case. To mitigate these issues, this work introduces Iterative Temporal Parent Discovery (ITPD): a sound and complete constraint-based procedure for causal structure learning on multivariate time series with causal nonstationarity. ITPD takes a local-to-global learning approach that discovers the global graph by sequentially discovering the parent set for each variable. ITPD exploits temporal structural priors, graph partitioning, and the local Markov condition to swap higher-order conditional independence tests for lower-order alternatives. With an exact oracle, ITPD performed the least number of tests and used the smallest average conditioning set sizes relative to baselines. In benchmarking simulations, ITPD sat on the Pareto frontier with respect to F1 and wall-clock time and was faster than baselines on large graphs ($\geq$ 1000 nodes). For graphs with 2000 nodes, ITPD ran $2.3\times$, $9.1\times$, and $40.9\times$ faster than iterative IAMB, DF-PC, and PCMCI, respectively.

</details>


### [120] [The Stability Gap in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26252)
*Sebastian Pusch,Matthia Sabatelli,Gido M. van de Ven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个简单的持续强化学习（CRL）基准，通过改变观测表示而保持环境动态与奖励不变，并据此揭示CRL中的"稳定性差距"现象：引入新任务后旧任务性能先显著下降、随后部分或完全恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL评估多依赖最终聚合指标，这类指标会掩盖适应过程中出现的临时性能下降，因而需要一个轻量、可复现的基准来暴露训练过程中的动态行为。

Method: 构建一个简单CRL基准：仅变换观测表示（representation），而环境动态与奖励函数保持不变；该设置便于概念验证、低成本实验与可复现的健全性检查，并用于分析学习曲线而非只看最终聚合结果。

Result: 在该基准上观察到"稳定性差距"：新任务引入后，先前任务性能出现大幅下降，随后部分或完全恢复；且该现象在智能体可完整访问过往任务的情况下依然出现。

Conclusion: 最终聚合指标不足以刻画CRL的适应过程；观测表示变化本身即可诱发稳定性差距，表明该现象并非仅源于任务分布或奖励变化，提示需要关注训练过程中的性能波动并提出相应缓解方法。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) aims to enable agents to adapt to changing tasks while preserving previously acquired capabilities. Evaluation often relies on final aggregate metrics that can hide temporary performance degradation during adaptation. This work introduces a simple CRL benchmark that varies observation representations while preserving environment dynamics and rewards, supporting proof-of-concept demonstrations, inexpensive experimentation, and easily reproducible sanity checks. Using this benchmark, we demonstrate the stability gap in CRL: a substantial drop in prior-task performance after the introduction of new tasks, followed by partial or complete recovery. This pattern occurs despite the agent having complete access to past tasks.

</details>


### [121] [SKILL.PNG: Learning Agent Skills in Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26296)
*Maximilian Seeth,Chengzhi Martin Hu,Marie Kempkes,Paul Röttger,Leo Schwinn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Skill.png：将智能体"技能"表示为可微优化的图像输入，通过冻结 VLM 的视觉编码器做梯度优化，兼顾连续优化与跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本技能是离散的自然语言指令，无法连续优化；而微调等梯度方法又与具体模型绑定，难以在不同智能体间迁移。二者存在"可优化性"与"可迁移性"的矛盾。

Method: 利用多数智能体基于视觉语言模型这一前提，将技能编码为图像（skill-like image），保持 VLM 冻结，仅通过视觉编码器对像素做梯度下降优化；同一图像可针对多个模型的集成联合优化；并可与文本技能联合优化形成 hybrid skills。

Result: 在 4 个 VLM × 4 个基准共 16 个设置中，Skill.png 于 9 个设置显著优于由前沿 LLM 优化的 SOTA 文本技能，且推理时仅需 64–256 个图像 token（对比约 900–2000 个文本 token）；文本与图像联合优化的混合技能进一步提升性能；集成优化得到的图像技能可在集成内的各模型间成功迁移。

Conclusion: Skill.png 是首个既可梯度优化、又保留文本技能迁移优势的智能体技能形式，为技能表示提供了像素级连续优化范式。

Abstract: AI agents are commonly specialized for new tasks and domains using skills, i.e. natural language instructions placed in their context. However, the discrete nature of textual skills prevents continuous optimization. Gradient-based approaches such as finetuning, on the other hand, are typically model-specific, preventing skill transfer across different agents. Since many agents today are built on vision-language models (VLMs), we can address these limitations by introducing Skill.png: a method for obtaining skill-like image inputs whose pixels are optimized with gradient descent through the vision encoder of a frozen VLM. Image skills are compatible with any VLM, and a single image can be optimized jointly for an ensemble of models. We show that Skill.png significantly outperforms state-of-the-art textual skills optimized using frontier LLMs in 9 of 16 settings (across 4 VLMs and 4 benchmarks) while requiring only 64 to 256 image tokens instead of roughly 900 to 2000 text tokens at inference time. Hybrid skills, i.e. text and image skills jointly optimized, further improve agent performance. Moreover, an image optimized with an ensemble of VLMs can successfully transfer across the models in the ensemble. Skill.png thus presents the first agent skill that can be optimized with gradient descent while retaining transfer benefits of text skills. Our code is available at: https://github.com/omseeth/skill.png

</details>


### [122] [A Control-Theoretic Approximation to Predictive Coding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26319)
*Ryan Fayyazi,Kyle Daruwalla,Mitra Javadzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明深度反馈控制（DFC）可作为预测编码（PC）动力学在不动点附近的局部近似，提出"Approx PC"并分析其偏差来源与缓解方法，从而统一了能量视角与控制论视角下的局部学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖非局部计算，缺乏生物合理性，因此出现预测编码（PC）与深度反馈控制（DFC）两类局部学习框架，但二者关系尚不明确。作者希望厘清DFC与PC的联系，从而统一能量型与控制论型学习理论，并把控制论工具引入PC网络训练。

Method: 从理论上推导DFC作为PC动力学在不动点附近的局部近似（称Approx PC）：在浅层线性网络下二者严格等价；随深度增加、非线性增强、偏离平衡点越远，近似偏差系统性增大。分析该近似在活动动力学上的偏差，并提出两种缓解手段：重新线性化反馈控制器、通过一连串中间"nudge"逐步逼近输出目标。随后在MNIST与FashionMNIST上训练验证，并与使用相同nudge的PC训练进行对比。

Result: Approx PC在MNIST与FashionMNIST上达到与使用相同nudge的PC训练相当的任务性能，且其学到的权重可直接迁移用于PC推理。

Conclusion: DFC是PC在不动点附近的局部近似，二者在浅层线性情形下精确等价，偏差随深度、非线性与离平衡距离增大；通过重线性化与逐步nudge可有效缓解偏差。该工作统一了基于能量与控制论的两种学习视角，为将控制理论工具用于预测编码网络训练开辟了道路。

Abstract: Backpropagation remains the dominant method for training deep neural networks, but its reliance on non-local computations has motivated the search for biologically plausible alternatives. Two prominent frameworks, predictive coding (PC) and deep feedback control (DFC), offer distinct approaches to local learning, yet their relationship remains unclear. Here, we show that DFC arises as a local approximation to predictive coding dynamics near fixed points, with exact equivalence in shallow linear networks and systematic deviations arising with depth, nonlinearity, and distance from equilibrium. We characterize how this approximation, which we call Approx PC, deviates from PC in its activity dynamics, and show that these deviations can be mitigated by relinearizing the feedback controller and approaching the output target through a sequence of intermediate nudges. In networks trained on MNIST and FashionMNIST, Approx PC reaches task performance comparable to PC trained with the same nudges, and weights learned with it transfer directly to PC inference. Our results unify energy-based and control-theoretic perspectives on learning and open the door to applying tools from control theory to the training of predictive coding networks.

</details>


### [123] [Predicting Surface Displacement Fields of Laser Powder Bed Fusion Parts Using STL-Derived Graphs and Part-Level Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26323)
*Saima Ahmed Suhi,Lang Yuan,Md Habibor Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双分支图注意力网络，直接从STL网格图预测LPBF零件三维表面位移，R²达0.946、MAE为0.0178 mm，优于MLP、GraphSAGE与GCN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPBF成形前零件变形难以预测，热力耦合仿真在反复设计评估中计算代价过高，需要快速的代理预测方法。

Method: 构建双分支GAT：第一分支在加密STL表面网格的连通顶点上学局部几何关系；第二分支引入12个零件级描述符（质量分布、基板接触、悬垂、尺寸、形状）。以高斯核加权平均将ANSYS Additive Print仿真位移标签映射到STL顶点。117个零件、按零件划分的五折交叉验证，并与节点级MLP、GraphSAGE、GCN及消融实验对比。

Result: 位移幅值R²=0.946、MAE=0.0178 mm；较节点级MLP的R²由0.913提升至0.946，构建方向位移R²由0.903升至0.939；五折均优于GraphSAGE（0.936）与GCN（0.934）。消融显示去除质量分布描述符幅值R²降至0.900，去除基板接触与悬垂描述符降至0.920。

Conclusion: 局部网格连通性与全局几何描述符相结合可有效预测LPBF变形，支持更快的设计迭代与变形敏感几何的早期识别。

Abstract: Predicting part distortion before fabrication remains challenging in laser powder bed fusion (LPBF), while thermomechanical simulation is computationally expensive for repeated design evaluations. This study presents a two-branch graph attention network (GAT) to predict three-dimensional surface displacement directly from STL-derived graphs. The first branch learns geometric relationships among connected vertices of a densified STL surface mesh, while the second incorporates 12 part-level descriptors of mass distribution, build-plate contact, overhang, size, and shape. Displacement labels from ANSYS Additive Print simulations are mapped onto STL vertices using Gaussian-kernel weighted averaging. Evaluated on 117 parts using five-fold cross-validation split by part, the model achieves an R^2 of 0.946 and a mean absolute error of 0.0178 mm for displacement magnitude. Relative to a node-wise multilayer perceptron, GAT improves R^2 from 0.913 to 0.946; for build-direction displacement, R^2 increases from 0.903 to 0.939. GAT also outperforms GraphSAGE (R^2 = 0.936) and a graph convolutional network (R^2 = 0.934) across all five folds. Ablation experiments show that removing mass-distribution descriptors reduces magnitude R^2 to 0.900, while removing build-plate contact and overhang descriptors reduces it to 0.920. These results demonstrate the value of combining local mesh connectivity with global geometric descriptors to predict LPBF distortion, enabling faster design iteration and earlier identification of distortion-prone geometries.

</details>


### [124] [OceanCoder: A Verification-Guided Large Language Model for Automated Simulation Modelling in Ocean Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26333)
*Can Xue,Changhong He,Ziyi Sun,Anliang Ge,Shimin Yu,Bingchen Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OceanCoder：基于Qwen3-8B的海洋工程自动化建模仿真大模型，通过可扩展数据流水线、三层工程验证框架与分级奖励GRPO训练，并构建首个该领域基准，Pass@1从基座0%提升至SFT 46.77%、GRPO 51.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋工程数值仿真建模高度依赖专家知识；现有大模型自动化建模仅关注代码语法正确与可运行，无法保证生成模型与结果满足工程需求，且领域训练数据稀缺、缺乏评测基准。

Method: 构建可扩展数据流水线，覆盖12类工程任务、产出12000条经校验的SFT样本；设计三层验证框架（输入与工程参数/任务约束一致性、所需仿真分析完成度、输出结果的工程合理性），并将其结果转化为分级奖励用于GRPO；基于Qwen3-8B训练，同时构建首个海洋工程自动化建模基准（含分布外任务与真实工程案例）。

Result: 消融实验验证了数据流水线、验证框架与奖励设计的有效性；OceanCoder优于基座模型与闭源对比模型，Pass@1由基座0%升至SFT后46.77%、GRPO后51.43%。

Conclusion: 将工程验证作为训练信号可有效驱动自动化仿真建模，说明“可执行”不等于“工程可用”，以工程合理性为奖励维度是提升领域建模可靠性的关键路径。

Abstract: Numerical simulation is important in ocean engineering, but model construction requires expert knowledge. Large language models support automated modelling, but code syntax and successful execution alone do not establish whether generated models and results meet engineering requirements. We present OceanCoder, a Qwen3-8B-based large language model for automated simulation modelling in ocean engineering. To address specialised programming requirements and scarce training data, we develop an extensible data construction pipeline producing 12,000 verified supervised fine-tuning (SFT) examples across 12 engineering task categories. A three-layer verification framework checks model inputs against engineering parameters and task constraints, verifies completion of required simulation analyses, and assesses the engineering plausibility of computed outputs. Its outcomes provide graded rewards for group relative policy optimisation (GRPO), guiding the model towards engineering task completion. We also construct the first benchmark for automated modelling in ocean engineering, covering out-of-distribution tasks and real-world engineering cases. Experiments and ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the data pipeline, verification framework and reward design. OceanCoder outperforms both base models and the closed-source comparison, with Pass@1 increasing from 0% for the base model to 46.77% after SFT and 51.43% after GRPO. These results support engineering verification as a training signal for automated simulation modelling.

</details>


### [125] [TRCF: Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26351)
*Xudong Zuo,Shusen Ma,Qirui Chen,Bing Fan,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRCF：以紧凑的通道独立（CI）预测器为主体，外加一个互补的通道依赖（CD）分支，通过ANCHOR特征聚合与BRIDGE历史补全两模块引入跨变量信息，在四个真实数据集上取得最低MSE/MAE，并将峰值推理内存较APN降低17.28%–87.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则多元时间序列预测中，CI方法刻画各变量自身时序模式但不显式利用其他变量的观测；CD方法可利用变量间关系，却因异步采样难以学习。核心挑战在于：在保留各变量不规则历史的同时融入跨变量信息。

Method: 提出TRCF框架，在紧凑CI预测器上增加互补CD分支。ANCHOR：在共享时间位置聚合观测值与时间戳，保留观测支撑以构造紧凑CI特征。BRIDGE：通过历史补全构造互补特征——先由各变量自身观测估计其历史值，再利用邻近时刻其他变量观测值与估计值之差修正缺失项；补全后的历史保留原始观测值，经编码后与直接CI表示并列提供上下文。最后融合两类表示并结合查询时刻嵌入，送入浅层MLP完成预测。

Result: 在四个真实数据集的主基准对比中，TRCF取得最低MSE与MAE；相对APN，实测峰值推理内存降低17.28%至87.98%。

Conclusion: TRCF表明，以CI为主干、CD为互补的架构可有效兼顾不规则历史保真与跨变量信息利用，并在精度与推理内存效率上同时优于基线。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting supports healthcare monitoring, environmental analysis, and activity recognition. Channel-independent (CI) approaches capture each variable's temporal patterns but do not explicitly use observations from other variables. Channel-dependent (CD) modeling exploits relationships among variables, yet asynchronous measurements make these relationships difficult to learn. Incorporating cross-variable information while preserving each variable's irregular history therefore remains a key challenge. We propose Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting (TRCF), a framework that augments a compact CI forecaster with a complementary CD branch. ANCHOR aggregates observed values and timestamps at shared temporal locations, retaining observation support to form compact CI features. BRIDGE constructs complementary features through history completion. It first estimates each variable's historical values from its own observations, then uses differences between other variables' observed and estimated values at nearby times to correct missing entries. The completed history preserves observed values and is encoded to supply context alongside the direct CI representation. Finally, the two representations are fused, combined with the query-time embedding, and passed to a shallow multilayer perceptron for forecasting. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that TRCF achieves the lowest mean squared error and mean absolute error in the main benchmark comparison, while reducing measured peak inference memory by 17.28% to 87.98% relative to APN.

</details>


### [126] [Mamba Flow Matching Neural Processes: Linear-Time Inference for Irregularly Observed Spatial Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26355)
*Cosmo Santoni,Giovanni Charles,Timothy James Hitge,Oliver Watson,Elizaveta Semenova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MambaFlowNP：一种基于条件流匹配训练、采用多方向 Mamba-2 扫描的线性复杂度联合预测神经过程，用于稀疏不规则观测的空间场插值；在 NOAA 温度插值上取得最低 RMSE/CRPS 与近名义覆盖率，比最强注意力基线快 114–784 倍，单次前向可在 L40S 上于 1 秒内处理 10^6 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯过程克里金需为每个新场重拟合超参数并求解 O(N^3) 线性系统；神经过程虽可一次前向完成插值，但联合预测变体依赖上下文与查询点间的自注意力，复杂度为 O(K^2)，限制在数千点规模。状态空间模型虽线性，但直接将上下文压缩为单一隐状态后再读取查询，削弱了解码器可见信息。

Method: MambaFlowNP 结合条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）与多方向 Mamba-2 扫描：将上下文 token 与查询 token 放入同一共享序列，使每个查询在其自身扫描位置读取速度场，而非依赖压缩的上下文摘要；由于查询只读取扫描状态，预测在不同查询集合间保持一致。

Result: 在合成 Matérn 高斯过程基准上与最强注意力联合预测基线相当；在区域至大陆尺度 NOAA 温度插值各层级上取得最低 RMSE 与 CRPS、近名义覆盖率，速度提升 114–784 倍；单次前向在单张 NVIDIA L40S 上不到 1 秒处理 10^6 点；预测质量评估至 K=5,580。

Conclusion: MambaFlowNP 以线性计算实现高质量联合预测，使大陆尺度、不规则采样传感器网络的联合预测变得可行，兼具精度与显著效率优势。

Abstract: Interpolating a spatial field, such as daily maximum temperature, from sparse, irregularly located observations recurs across the environmental and health sciences. Standard Gaussian-process kriging refits hyperparameters and solves an $O(N^3)$ system for every new field. When many related fields are observed, such as daily maps from one sensor network, neural processes (NPs) instead learn the interpolation map from past fields and predict a new field in one forward pass. Joint-predictive variants, which sample whole fields rather than independent per-location values, typically use self-attention over context and query points, costing $O(K^2)$ in the combined point count $K$ and limiting practical use to a few thousand points. State-space models offer a linear-time alternative, but a direct state-space adaptation compresses the context into one hidden state before reading queries, reducing what the decoder sees. We propose MambaFlowNP, a linear-compute joint-predictive NP trained with conditional flow matching. A multi-directional Mamba-2 scan reads context and query tokens in one shared sequence, so each query's velocity is read from its own scan position rather than a compressed context summary, and because queries only read the scan state, predictions are consistent across query sets. MambaFlowNP is competitive with the strongest attention-based joint-predictive baselines on synthetic Matérn Gaussian-process benchmarks, and on regional to continental-scale NOAA temperature interpolation it attains the lowest RMSE and CRPS and near-nominal coverage on every tier, while running 114--784$\times$ faster than the strongest joint-predictive baseline. A single forward pass scales to $10^6$ points on one NVIDIA L40S GPU in under a second. This makes joint prediction feasible for continental-scale, irregularly sampled sensor networks; predictive quality is evaluated up to $K=5{,}580$.

</details>


### [127] [Learning from Context: Leave-One-Out Prediction in Jump Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26359)
*Christian Wald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出带坐标重置的跳扩散模型，并引入留一法(LOO)预测，使各坐标的预测仅依赖其他坐标的受损状态，避免模型依赖自身受损状态而弱化坐标间依赖学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型在嵌入空间中生成离散数据时，坐标自身的受损状态会使预测过于容易，从而降低学习坐标间依赖关系的压力，影响生成质量。

Method: 研究带完整坐标重置、状态无关时钟的跳扩散模型；采用LOO预测，即每个坐标由其他坐标的受损状态预测；并证明在精确LOO概率作为重置目标、完整端点后验作为漂移时保持规定边缘分布；进一步通过解析似然补全由LOO概率构造该漂移，无需额外网络评估。

Result: 在Text8与LM1B文本生成及ZINC250k分子图生成上，LOO变体在全部三个数据集上优于匹配的无约束对照模型的关键生成指标。

Conclusion: LOO预测能改善生成性能；对照干预实验评估了模型对局部输入与上下文的依赖，并分析了解析似然补全的作用。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion models generate discrete data through stochastic dynamics in an embedding space. We consider jump diffusions that additionally reset coordinates to embeddings of categorical values. A coordinate's own corrupted state can make prediction easy, potentially reducing the pressure to learn dependencies between coordinates. We study leave-one-out (LOO) prediction, in which each coordinate is predicted from the corrupted states of the other coordinates. We show that, under stated assumptions, jump diffusions with complete coordinate resets and state-independent clocks preserve the prescribed marginals when reset destinations use exact LOO probabilities and the drift uses the full endpoint posterior. We further show that this drift can be constructed from LOO probabilities by analytic likelihood completion without additional network evaluations. We evaluate unrestricted, LOO, and completed predictions on Text8 and LM1B text generation and ZINC250k molecular graph generation. LOO-based variants improve key generation metrics over matched unrestricted controls on all three datasets. Controlled interventions assess reliance on local inputs and context, and we analyze the effect of analytic likelihood completion.

</details>


### [128] [HiSA: Sparsifying the Hidden Dimension of RoPE Attention in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26363)
*Ambroise Borbely,Mathieu Salzmann,Olivier Verscheure*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hidden Sparse Attention (HiSA)，利用 RoPE 结构将 query/key 拆分为可省略部分，在隐藏（特征）维度上做稀疏的部分点积，仅计算约 1/3 的 QK 乘积，反而提升性能并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力几乎都在 token 维度稀疏，而注意力需对每个 query-key 做完整点积，计算开销大；作者质疑点积是否必须完整计算，探索较少被研究的隐藏维度稀疏。

Method: 保留 query/key 投影不变，基于 RoPE 结构将 key 和 query 分解为若干部分，按 token 位置的静态、无参数规则省略部分维度的点积计算，实现特征维度稀疏；从零训练 LLM，并移植到 FlashAttention-3 kernel 用于训练与推理。

Result: 仅计算约 1/3 的 QK 乘积，性能不降反升；推理阶段提升解码速度、缩小内存占用；并证明稠密注意力会学习抑制 HiSA 所丢弃的注意力分数部分。

Conclusion: 隐藏维度稀疏注意力是有效且高效的替代方案，部分点积足以甚至更利于注意力，为稀疏注意力提供新方向并提供可用代码实现。

Abstract: Attention in large language models (LLMs) scores every query against every key with a full dot product. We investigate whether this dot product must be computed completely for attention to be effective. We propose Hidden Sparse Attention (HiSA), which keeps the query and key projections intact but only computes a partial dot product, following a static, parameter-free rule set by the token positions: attention becomes sparse in the hidden (feature) dimension, an axis much less explored than token sparsity, rather than over tokens as in almost all sparse attention methods. Based on the structure of rotary position embeddings (RoPE), we decompose keys and queries into parts which can be omitted when computing attention. By training LLMs from scratch with HiSA, we find that this partial dot product does not hurt but improves performance compared with the complete product while computing only about a third of the query-key products. During inference HiSA improves decoding speed and shrinks memory footprint. We also extend HiSA to FlashAttention-3 kernels for training and inference. Finally, we motivate our approach by showing that dense attention learns to suppress the attention score parts that HiSA drops. Code is available at https://github.com/ambroise-b/hisa.

</details>


### [129] [From Benchmarks to the Bench: Preference, Correspondence and Selectivity in Bidirectional Drug-Target Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26369)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Chengrui Xu,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了13个已发布的药物-靶点相互作用（DTI）模型的推荐一致性、命中富集能力与决策可用性，发现多数模型对同一靶点或药物的Top-10推荐几乎完全不重叠，且部分模型的高命中率源于对特定候选的固定偏好，模型再训练后的推荐稳定性也很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTI模型可对海量药物-靶点对排序，但实验验证能力有限，只能测试少量候选。因此仅凭基准性能不足以说明模型能否真正指导实验决策，需要评估其推荐是否稳健、可复现并具备实际筛选价值。

Method: 选取13个已发布DTI系统，在711个FDA批准药物分子结构与614个人类蛋白上比较双向推荐结果；统计同一靶点/药物下各模型Top-10列表的重叠情况。利用79个分子与65个激酶的已发表抑制实测数据评估命中富集，并通过将推荐匹配到不同药物或靶点考察命中的稳健性；同时检验模型能否在一对药物偏好不同激酶时同时正确排序两者。此外，固定训练目标、改变随机种子，对同一架构在同一数据上重复训练，考察测试性能相近时在独立筛选目录上的Top-10重叠。

Result: 同一靶点或药物的模型对比较中，Top-10分子列表与蛋白列表完全不相交的比例分别为77.8%和70.1%；即便重叠，也主要反映对相同候选的重复偏好。实测抑制数据显示，部分系统能富集活性候选，但其命中率在推荐匹配到不同药物/靶点时变化很小，而另一些系统命中损失明显；前者中部分系统在两种药物偏好不同激酶时难以同时正确排序。重复训练后测试性能相似，但独立筛选目录上平均成对Top-10重叠仅约两个候选。

Conclusion: DTI模型的评测应超越基准指标，转向考察预测能否在药物发现与靶点识别中支撑有意义的实验决策；当前系统的推荐一致性、稳健性与可迁移性均存在明显不足。

Abstract: Identifying active molecules for known targets and potential targets for existing drugs is central to drug discovery and repurposing. Drug-target interaction (DTI) models can rank vast numbers of drug-target pairs, but limited experimental capacity restricts testing to a small number of candidates. We compared the bidirectional recommendations of 13 released systems across 711 molecular structures of FDA-approved drugs and 614 human proteins. For the same target or drug, pairs of models produced completely disjoint Top-10 molecule and protein lists in 77.8% and 70.1% of comparisons, respectively. Even where recommendations overlapped, agreement between some systems largely reflected recurring preferences for the same candidates. Published inhibition measurements for 79 molecules and 65 kinases revealed that some systems enriched active candidates, yet their hit yields changed little when recommendations were matched to different drugs or targets, whereas other systems lost substantially more hits. Some of the former systems also rarely ranked both molecules and kinases correctly when two drugs favoured different kinases. We further retrained the same architecture on the same data, with the training objective fixed and random seeds varied. Despite similar test performance, mean pairwise Top-10 overlap on a separate screening catalogue was about two candidates. Our study moves DTI model evaluation beyond benchmark performance to assess whether model predictions can guide meaningful experimental decisions in drug discovery and target identification.

</details>


### [130] [Evidence from a Multi-Regime Causal Discovery Benchmark: UCMT Algorithms, Comparative Performance, and Controlled Ablation Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26383)
*Mahyar Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对UCMT因果发现算法家族进行内部预注册评测，并与NOTEARS、DAGMA、BOSS、排列搜索类方法及方向判定基线对比。结果显示UCMT v2 Global在部分设定下有定向F1优势，UCMT v4在CSuite上相对BOSS有配对提升但数据集层面不显著；总体不支持"全面优越"，仅支持其作为已实现且经实证研究的算法家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法众多，但缺乏对内部自研算法家族（UCMT）进行预注册、分层、可复现的严格评测；作者希望在异质基准上同时回答两个问题：UCMT各配置相对已确立方法的实际表现如何，以及哪些内部阶段真正影响其结果。

Method: 采用内部预注册评测设计：主基准中按算法家族分层比较三种活跃开发中的UCMT配置（含UCMT v2 Global、基于BOSS的UCMT v4）与NOTEARS、DAGMA、排列搜索类方法；另设独立预注册的受控消融研究针对UCMT v2 Global组件，包括固定骨架下的方向判定消融（对比适配的R^2-SortnRegress、RESIT、ANM、DirectLiNGAM），以及对稀疏化、BIC精化、仲裁机制与自动机制选择的控制消融；统计上采用配对运行比较、数据集层面Holm多重检验校正，并报告运行时间开销与可复现性检查。

Result: UCMT v2 Global在部分真实、半合成与高斯网络比较中配对运行的定向F1高于NOTEARS和DAGMA，但在其他设定下落后于领先的排列搜索方法。UCMT v4在CSuite上相对BOSS平均配对差+0.279（配对Holm p<0.001），但数据集层面校正后不显著（p=0.188）。固定骨架消融中，UCMT v2 Global在标准化iSCM数据上定向F1为0.537，优于适配R^2-SortnRegress的0.468（数据集层面Holm p=0.031），绝对方向准确率为0.563，但相对RESIT、ANM、DirectLiNGAM无确立的广泛优势。消融显示稀疏化与BIC精化对部分结果有实质影响，而所考察的仲裁与自动机制选择未带来经确认的普遍改进。

Conclusion: 结果仅支持将UCMT定位为已实现并经过实证考察的算法家族，而非整体优越性主张；不构成独立外部验证，也未证明商业影响。作者同时报告了性能短板、多重检验校正、运行成本、评测局限与可复现性检查，强调需谨慎解读其优势结论。

Abstract: We present an internal, pre-registered evaluation of the UCMT causal discovery algorithm family alongside established alternatives across heterogeneous benchmark regimes, complemented by a separately pre-registered, controlled study of UCMT v2 Global components. The contribution is both empirical and methodological: a family-stratified comparison of three actively developed algorithm configurations and targeted evidence about which internal stages affect their outcomes. In the main benchmark, UCMT v2 Global has higher directed F1 than NOTEARS and DAGMA in selected real, semi-synthetic and Gaussian-network comparisons at the paired-run level, while trailing leading permutation-search methods in other settings. The BOSS-based UCMT v4 produces a CSuite-specific directed-F1 improvement over BOSS (mean paired difference +0.279; pair-level Holm p < 0.001), but the dataset-level adjusted result is not significant (p = 0.188). In a separate fixed-skeleton ablation, UCMT v2 Global exceeds an adapted R^2-SortnRegress orientation baseline on standardized iSCM data (directed F1 0.537 vs 0.468, dataset-level Holm p = 0.031); absolute orientation accuracy is 0.563 and there is no established broad advantage over RESIT, ANM or DirectLiNGAM. Controlled ablations show that sparsity and BIC-based refinement materially affect some outcomes, whereas the examined arbitration and automatic regime-selection mechanisms do not yield confirmed general improvements. We report performance shortcomings, multiple-testing correction, runtime costs, evaluation limitations and reproducibility checks. The results support UCMT as an implemented and empirically investigated algorithm family, not a claim of overall superiority, independent validation or demonstrated commercial impact.

</details>


### [131] [A Complete Geometric Message-Passing Architecture for Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26384)
*Nadav Dym,Tal Amir,Gia Chi Dang,Christopher Morris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖、简单的等变消息传递点云架构并证明其完备性，实验证明其能区分距离型消息传递无法区分的点云，表明高阶等变特征并非提升表达力的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维点云机器学习架构在复杂度与计算代价上差异巨大，需要一个原则性标准来比较，即表达力（完备性：能否区分任何不被所尊重对称性关联的两个输入）。已知基于距离的消息传递网络不完备，而现有完备方案依赖高阶旋转群表示或高阶Weisfeiler-Leman类检验，计算代价高昂。

Method: 设计一种新的简单等变消息传递架构，并通过理论证明其完备性；辅以实验验证其在具有挑战性的点云上的区分能力；并将该证明思路推广，证明稍作修改的SEGNN的完备性。

Result: 理论上证明了新架构的完备性；实验表明该架构能区分距离型消息传递可证无法区分的困难点云对；并给出SEGNN（略加修改）完备性的初步证据。

Conclusion: 提升点云架构表达力可能并不需要高阶等变特征；该结论有助于为其他等变架构确立完备性结果。

Abstract: Three-dimensional point clouds are a fundamental data modality, and a large family of machine learning architectures has been developed to respect their natural symmetries to rigid motions and permutations. A principled way to compare these architectures, which vary widely in complexity and cost, is through their expressive power. That is, an architecture is complete if it can distinguish any two inputs not related by the symmetries it respects. Simple distance-based message-passing networks are known to be incomplete, and while there exist complete alternatives, they typically rely on computationally intensive mechanisms like high-order representations of the rotation group or higher-order Weisfeiler-Leman-type tests.
  In this work, we devise a novel, simple, equivariant message-passing architecture for point clouds, and prove that it is complete. We complement our theoretical result with experiments showing that our architecture separates challenging point clouds that distance-based message passing provably cannot distinguish. Our results suggest that high-order equivariant features may not be necessary to enhance the expressivity of point cloud architectures, and can help settle completeness for other equivariant architectures for which such results are currently unknown. We give initial evidence for this by proving completeness of a slightly modified version of SEGNN.

</details>


### [132] [Sequential Anytime Valid Tests for Efficient Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26413)
*Vishesh Kumar Singh,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 e-RWSC：将推理时对齐的提前停止问题建模为对候选答案簇的序列检验，用基于投注上鞅（e-process）的 anytime-valid 停止规则，以误差容限 α 为输入，在始终不误认证非主导答案的概率 ≤ α 的前提下，显著降低 token 成本并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N 等推理时对齐方法对每个 prompt 施加固定预算，浪费大量计算；而自适应方法虽能在答案"看似稳定"时提前停止，但其阈值是经验性启发式，对早停错误缺乏统计控制。作者希望得到一种以误差容限 α 为输入的 anytime-valid 停止规则，仅在能证明某答案在生成分布下占多数（θ_a > 1/2）时才停止。

Method: 用奖励加权自一致性（RWSC）分数按最终答案聚合候选，将停止问题转化为对答案簇的序贯检验。直接对簇施加 Wald 的 SPRT 得到 SPRT-RWSC，但因其在采样过程中持续监控且领先答案会漂移，其理论保证失效。主方法 e-RWSC 为策略产生的每个答案构造基于投注的上鞅（e-process），替代 Wald 检验，从而对时间与所有答案一致地控制"曾认证非主导答案"的概率不超过 α；RWSC 分数仅驱动投注大小，只影响认证速度而不影响有效性。

Result: 理论上，认证主导答案 a* 所需样本量为 O(log(1/α)/d_KL(θ_{a*}, 1/2))。在多个语言模型、数据集与奖励模型上的实验验证了成本的 1/d_KL 缩放规律，并显示在显著更低的 token 成本下准确率优于基线。

Conclusion: e-RWSC 为推理时对齐提供了一种具有严格 anytime-valid 统计保证的自适应停止机制：在统一控制早停错误概率的同时，把计算集中在真正需要的 prompt 上，实现了更低成本与更高准确率的兼得。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-N (BoN), improve language model outputs by generating multiple candidates per prompt and selecting among them using a reward model. Much of this cost is wasted: a fixed budget is applied to every prompt, although the computation required by prompts varies widely. Adaptive methods halt once sampled answers appear settled, but their thresholds are tuned heuristics with no statistical control over early-stopping errors. We instead seek an anytime-valid stopping rule that takes an error tolerance $α$ as input and halts only once an answer is certified to hold a majority ($θ_a > 1/2$) under the generator's distribution. We aggregate candidates by final answer with a reward-weighted self-consistency (RWSC) score and treat stopping as a sequential test over the resulting clusters. Applying Wald's sequential probability ratio test to these clusters yields SPRT-RWSC, which performs well empirically, but its guarantee fails because the leading answer shifts during sampling, and the run is continuously monitored. Our main contribution, e-RWSC, replaces Wald's test with a betting supermartingale (an e-process) for every answer the policy produces, bounding the probability of ever certifying a non-dominant answer by $α$, uniformly over time and across all answers. The RWSC scores drive the bets, so they affect only how quickly a dominant answer $a^\star$ is certified, not validity; certification takes $O(\log(1/α)/d_{\mathrm{KL}}(θ_{a^\star}\|1/2))$ samples. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm the predicted $1/d_{\mathrm{KL}}$ scaling of cost and show that accuracy improves over baselines at substantially lower token cost.

</details>


### [133] [FEA solver integration of a composite World Model with a Spiking Neural Network memory Block for computationally efficient modelling of defects and variabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26419)
*Bassam El Said,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态脉冲Transformer的复合材料世界模型作为材料尺度代理模型，替代并行多尺度计算，并在含缺陷层合板算例中取得0.4%–4.8%的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强复合材料具有独特的材料构架，制造方法与缺陷导致局部性能变异，需要在结构每个位置实时解析材料尺度响应。并行多尺度（concurrent multi-scale）方案虽能实现，但积分点与时间增量随结构尺寸增长，计算成本对大结构不可承受，因此需要代理模型替代材料尺度模型。

Method: 设计Composites World Model：材料编码器 + 脉冲神经网络（SNN）记忆块跟踪材料长期内部状态 + Transformer块学习短期施加应变状态。三阶段训练：材料构架编码、短时间窗基础训练、带调度采样的递归微调以抑制累积误差漂移；推理时拆解模型并与非线性有限元求解器耦合。

Result: 后验与IM7/8552层合板在拉伸、压缩、横向剪切（无缺陷与褶皱缺陷）实验对比，SNN记忆块成功跟踪材料内部状态，预测误差0.4%–4.8%。

Conclusion: 以脉冲Transformer架构的世界模型可满足任意局部构架表示、不可逆状态跟踪、递归稳定性与积分点内存约束等要求，可支撑含嵌入缺陷复合材料的结构尺度仿真。

Abstract: Each fibre-reinforced composite structure has a unique material architecture. The manufacturing methods used and the presence of defects lead to local variations which require material-scale models that can resolve the response in real time at every location in the structure. Concurrent multi-scale schemes provide this capability, but at a prohibitive computational cost for large structures as the number of integration points and time increments scales with size. Surrogates have been studied extensively as replacements for material-scale models to mitigate these computational limitations. A surrogate must represent an arbitrary local architecture including defects, track the irreversible material state over an unknown number of increments, remain stable when driven recursively by its own predictions, and fit within the memory available at every integration point. Here, a Composites World Model based on a Multi-Modal Spiking Transformer architecture is designed to meet these requirements. The proposed architecture combines a material encoder, a Spiking Neural Network memory block that tracks the long-term internal state of the material, and transformer blocks that learn the short-term applied strain state. The system is trained in three stages: material architecture encoding, base training on short time windows, and recursive fine-tuning with scheduled sampling to suppress cumulative error drift. The trained model is disassembled at inference and coupled to a non-linear FE solver, enabling structural scale simulation of composites containing embedded defects. Benchmarks against experimental results for IM7/8552 laminates under tension, compression and transverse shear, for pristine and wrinkled composites, show that the SNN memory block successfully tracks the material internal state and results in prediction errors between 0.4% and 4.8%.

</details>


### [134] [Learning to Defer under Expert Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26440)
*Ziyao Mou,Andrea Wynn,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家漂移自适应的学习延迟框架EDA-L2D，通过序列模型利用专家历史表现动态建模其可靠性波动，从而改进人机决策分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习延迟（L2D）研究普遍假设专家可靠性固定不变，忽略了疲劳、认知偏差等因素导致的系统性、随时间波动的专家表现，这一假设在放射诊断、航空、司法及众包标注等场景中不成立。

Method: 提出EDA-L2D框架，将延迟决策头（deferral head）以专家近期结果历史为条件，采用序列模型建模专家可靠性的时间动态，从而学习能够预判可靠性波动、自适应分配人机决策的策略。

Result: 在图像分类、医学诊断、仇恨言论检测三类任务上开展实验，并在真实延迟条件与专家可靠性不规则漂移下进行泛化分析；结果表明EDA-L2D相比已有L2D方法能更有效适应多种专家可靠性时间模式，系统性能更优、延迟决策更合理。

Conclusion: 将专家可靠性的时间波动显式纳入建模，可显著提升学习延迟框架在人机协作系统中的有效性与安全性。

Abstract: The learning to defer (L2D) framework enables safety in machine learning and AI systems by allocating difficult or critical decisions to a human expert. However, most prior work on L2D assumes fixed expert reliability, overlooking temporal fluctuations in human performance due to factors such as fatigue and cognitive biases. Humans commonly exhibit such substantial, systematic performance shifts across a wide range of domains, from high-stakes settings such as radiology, aviation, and judicial decision-making, to everyday tasks such as crowdsourced image annotation. We propose Expert-Drift-Adapted Learning-to-Defer (EDA-L2D), a novel expert-aware L2D framework that explicitly models these temporal shifts by conditioning the deferral head on recent histories of expert outcomes via a sequence model. This history-aware approach enables adaptive deferral policies that anticipate reliability fluctuations and better allocate decisions between human and machine over time. We provide comprehensive experiments across three diverse tasks -- image classification, medical diagnosis, and hate speech detection -- including generalization analyses under realistic deferral conditions and irregular shifts in expert reliability. Our results show that EDA-L2D adapts more effectively than prior L2D methods to diverse temporal patterns of expert reliability, resulting in improved system performance and more effective deferral decisions. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiyaoMou/L2D-Expert-Drift.

</details>


### [135] [Neural Conditional Gaussian Model: Efficient Data Assimilation with Sequential Uncertainty Quantification for Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26463)
*Chuanqi Chen,Nan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neural CGM：将物理状态映射到条件高斯结构的潜空间，实现闭式数据同化更新，并用高斯解码器把潜变量后验均值与协方差传播到物理空间，在非线性基准系统上兼顾精度与在线效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分观测的非线性系统数据同化需反复估计隐状态及其不确定性，传统非线性滤波计算昂贵且依赖显式动力学模型；同时不确定性的刻画常与同化过程割裂（如事后估计）。

Method: 数据驱动的动力学模型：编码器将未观测物理状态映射到潜空间，潜状态动力学服从条件高斯结构，从而在潜空间进行闭式同化更新；解码器为高斯解码器，将潜后验均值与协方差通过局部矩传播加学习的非线性修正映射回物理空间；同一组后验统计量进入训练时的高斯负对数似然，使均值与不确定性共同参与学习。

Result: 在多个基准非线性动力学系统上实现准确的状态估计与预测，且相对传统非线性同化方法显著降低在线同化成本。

Conclusion: Neural CGM在无显式控制方程条件下兼具建模、同化与预测能力，适用于基于部分观测的重复状态估计、不确定性量化与预测。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) in partially observed nonlinear systems requires repeated estimation of both hidden states and their uncertainty. Conventional nonlinear filtering can be computationally expensive and often relies on an explicit dynamical model. In this paper, we develop the Neural Conditional Gaussian Model (Neural CGM), a data-driven dynamical model that maps unobserved physical states into a learned latent space, where the dynamics of the latent states follow a conditional Gaussian structure.
